On GeoText-1652, UniGeo improves R@10 and mAP by 13.59 and 2.83 percentage points, respectively, validating its effectiveness for fine-grained text-guided drone geo-localization.

</details>


### [34] [GeoMAD: Geometry-Aware Multi-View Anomaly Detection via Deformable Fusion and Distributional Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26724)
*Shang-Fu Chen,Jhih-Ciang Wu,Kuan-Chuan Peng,Wen-Huang Cheng,Kai-Lung Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMAD是一个统一的多视角多类别异常检测框架，通过Cross-view Deformable Fusion Module (CDFM)在2D特征图上学习内容自适应的跨视角采样偏移，无需相机标定或3D重建；同时引入Distributional View Alignment (DVA)自监督正则化损失对齐各视角的特征分布，实现几何感知与分布一致的跨视角融合，在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny数据集上取得优异检测与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角异常检测方法存在两难：基于体素的融合方法（如3D重建）虽能提供显式几何对齐但计算成本高且依赖类别特定假设；而轻量化的基于patch的融合方法虽然高效但依赖离散候选匹配，缺乏连续的跨视角对应关系。因此需要一个既能保持2D特征空间学习效率、又具备几何感知能力的统一框架。

Method: 提出GeoMAD框架，包含两个核心模块：1) CDFM (Cross-view Deformable Fusion Module)：直接在2D特征图上学习内容自适应、视图对特定的采样偏移，通过多尺度窗口金字塔配合图像全局参考采样实现层次化跨视角对应，无需相机标定、体素构建或类别特定的3D监督；2) DVA (Distributional View Alignment)：自监督跨视角正则化损失，将每个视角的bottleneck分布对齐到逐实例视图中心目标，在无像素级对应的情况下强制全局一致性。

Result: 在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny两个数据集上的大量实验表明，GeoMAD在统一的多视图多类别异常检测任务中实现了优异的检测和定位性能。

Conclusion: CDFM和DVA分别从局部几何对应和全局分布一致性两个层面桥接跨视角融合，既获得了几何感知能力又保持了2D特征空间学习的高效性，为多视图多类别异常检测提供了统一且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-view anomaly detection (MvAD) detects defects by exploiting complementary observations from multiple camera viewpoints. The central challenge is to fuse views with sufficient geometric awareness while remaining scalable to multi-class industrial settings. Existing methods typically fall into two extremes: voxel-based fusion provides explicit geometric alignment but requires costly 3D construction and class-specific assumptions, whereas lightweight patch-based fusion is efficient but relies on discrete candidate matching and lacks continuous cross-view correspondence. In this paper, we propose GeoMAD, a unified multi-view, multi-class AD framework that addresses both geometric correspondence deficiency and distributional inconsistency. Our \textit{Cross-view Deformable Fusion Module} (CDFM) learns content-adaptive, view-pair-specific sampling offsets directly on 2D feature maps and arranges them across a multi-scale window pyramid with image-global reference sampling, enabling hierarchical cross-view correspondence without camera calibration, voxel construction, or class-specific 3D supervision. We further introduce \textit{Distributional View Alignment} (DVA), a self-supervised cross-view regularization loss that aligns each view's bottleneck distribution against a per-instance view-centric target, enforcing global consistency without pixel-level correspondence. Together, CDFM and DVA bridge local geometric correspondence and global distributional consistency, providing geometry-aware and distribution-consistent fusion while preserving the efficiency of 2D feature-space learning. Extensive experiments on Real-IAD and MANTA-Tiny show that GeoMAD achieves strong detection and localization performance in unified MvAD.

</details>


### [35] [Generative Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26737)
*Shi Chen,Weifeng Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将室外LiDAR语义场景补全（SSC）重新定义为生成式语义场景补全（GSSC），采用单一离散扩散框架承担三个角色：配对稀疏-稠密场景合成（PS³）、语义引导生成场景补全（SGSC）、以及结构化源离散扩散（S²D²）。该框架在SemanticKITTI隐藏测试集上达到38.8% mIoU（无测试时增强），是目前因果、单扫、单样本场景下的最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室外LiDAR SSC面临两大核心挑战：扫描仅观测目标体积的1%，且类别不平衡超过7000倍。现有方法难以应对长尾分布和稀疏观测下的稠密语义补全问题，作者希望通过生成式建模从根本上解决数据稀缺和极端不平衡问题。

Method: 文章提出一个统一的离散扩散框架（GSSC），包含三个角色：(1) PS³：配对生成稀疏LiDAR观测与其稠密语义补全，构建PS³-SemanticKITTI语料库，从源头解决长尾问题；(2) SGSC：以鸟瞰语义图和稀疏3D特征流为条件，通过多项离散扩散从噪声生成场景；(3) S²D²：同一框架通过单步流匹配对现有补全结果进行精炼，无需重新训练基础模型或测试时自适应。

Result: S²D²在不重训基础模型的情况下提升了SGSC自身输出及所有外部SSC基线的mIoU。在最强基线上，无需测试时增强的单步精炼达到38.8% mIoU（SemanticKITTI隐藏测试集），为因果、单扫、单样本条件下leaderboard最佳，较此前最佳结果提高2.1个百分点；四步精炼配合八视图测试时增强达到39.2%。

Conclusion: 通过统一的离散扩散生成式框架，GSSC不仅从源头缓解了数据长尾问题（PS³），实现了高质量的生成式场景补全（SGSC），还以无需重训的通用精炼范式（S²D²）大幅提升了各类SSC基线的性能，确立了生成式方法在室外LiDAR SSC任务中的优势地位。

Abstract: Outdoor LiDAR semantic scene completion (SSC) recovers a dense semantic voxel grid from a scan observing 1% of the target volume, under class imbalance beyond 7,000x. We recast SSC as generative semantic scene completion (GSSC): a single discrete-diffusion formulation in three roles. First, paired sparse-dense scene synthesis (PS$^3$) generates matched sparse LiDAR observations with their dense semantic completions, addressing the long tail at its source and yielding the PS$^3$-SemanticKITTI corpus we train on alongside SemanticKITTI. Second, semantic-guided generative scene completion (SGSC) generates the scene from noise with multinomial discrete diffusion, conditioned on the sparse scan through a bird's-eye-view semantic map and a sparse 3D feature stream. Third, the same framework instead refines an existing completion in one flow-matching step: structured source discrete diffusion (S$^2$D$^2$). S$^2$D$^2$ improves the mIoU of SGSC's own output and every external SSC base tested, without base retraining or test-time adaptation. On the strongest base, one step without test-time augmentation reaches 38.8% mIoU on the SemanticKITTI hidden test. To our knowledge that is the best causal, single-sweep, single-sample result on that leaderboard, +2.1 pp over the previous best published score under the same restriction. Four correction steps with eight-view test-time augmentation reach 39.2%, outside that restriction.

</details>


### [36] [G2D: Generative-to-Discriminative Collaborative Inference for Zero-Shot Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26744)
*Zehua Hao,Fang Liu,Qinliang Wang,Yaoyang Du,Xinyan Huang,Puhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G2D是一个无需训练的框架，结合判别式CLIP模型（用于广泛的候选标签检索）与生成式视觉语言模型（VLM，用于对CLIP检索到的候选标签进行细粒度的、基于图像的验证），通过固定置信度路由、熵自适应候选规模和trie约束解码等机制，在八个基准上达到68.85%的平均准确率，显著优于单独的CLIP（59.35%）和独立的VLM（63.11%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本分类面临高效标签检索与细粒度视觉推理的双重挑战，而判别式模型（如CLIP）和生成式VLM在能力上存在互补性缺陷：CLIP的top-1预测错误时正确标签往往仍在其top-K候选列表中，因此关键问题在于消歧而非召回；而独立生成模型在大标签空间和不受约束的输出下表现受限。这种互补性促使作者将宽泛的候选检索与细粒度、基于图像的验证相分离。

Method: G2D采用训练免费的两阶段框架：(1) 使用CLIP进行宽泛的候选标签检索；(2) 使用生成式VLM对检索到的候选标签进行针对图像的验证。候选标签名称和CLIP概率提供结构化先验以解决视觉相似类别的消歧。具体机制包括：固定置信度路由（决定何时调用生成模型）、熵自适应候选规模（根据不确定性动态调整候选数量）、以及trie约束解码（保证每个输入在测试时只生成一个有效输出）。

Result: 在八个基准上，G2D达到68.85%的平均准确率，而CLIP和独立VLM分别为59.35%和63.11%。在七种生成器配置下，候选集验证将平均准确率提升了1.08至27.42个百分点。G2D还成功迁移到DCLIP、WaffleCLIP和CuPL等方法上，展示了判别式候选生成与生成式视觉推理之间的实用接口。

Conclusion: 通过将判别式模型与生成式模型的互补优势有机结合——用CLIP解决召回问题、用VLM解决消歧问题——G2D提供了一种无需训练的、通用且有效的零样本分类框架，并在多种基准和生成器配置下均表现出稳定的性能提升，同时具备良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: Zero-shot classification needs efficient label retrieval and fine-grained visual reasoning, yet discriminative and generative vision-language models fail in complementary ways.When CLIP's top-1 prediction is wrong, the correct label often remains in its top-$K$ shortlist, making disambiguation rather than recall the key challenge.Standalone generative models, however, are hindered by large label spaces and unconstrained outputs.This complementarity motivates separating broad candidate retrieval from fine-grained, image-grounded verification.We propose G2D, a training-free framework that uses a generative VLM to verify CLIP-retrieved candidates against the image.Candidate names and CLIP probabilities provide a structured prior for resolving visually similar classes.Fixed confidence routing, entropy-adaptive candidate sizing, and trie-constrained decoding focus generative reasoning on uncertain samples and ensure one valid output for each input at test time.Across eight benchmarks, G2D achieves 68.85% average accuracy, versus 59.35% for CLIP and 63.11% for the standalone VLM.Across seven generator configurations, candidate-set verification improves average accuracy by 1.08--27.42 percentage points.G2D also transfers to DCLIP, WaffleCLIP, and CuPL, supporting a practical interface between discriminative proposal and generative visual reasoning. Code: https://github.com/Harzva/G2D

</details>


### [37] [Glass Surface Detection Grounded in 3D Visual Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26752)
*Yiwei Lu,Ke Xu,Tao Yan,Xiaojun Chang,Radu Timofte,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新的玻璃表面检测（GSD）范式，将传统基于2D外观线索的方法转变为基于3D视觉几何的方法，通过蒸馏VGGT模型的3D先验，并设计包含频率自注意力模块（FSAM）和几何接地模块（GeGB）的检测头，在七个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃具有透明和反射特性，现有GSD方法依赖2D外观线索，在几何模糊的场景中容易失效。作者希望通过显式建模玻璃表面的物理存在性（即3D几何），克服2D方法的局限性。

Method: 1) 从视觉几何grounded transformer（VGGT）中蒸馏丰富的3D先验，生成玻璃感知的3D表示；2) 采用多任务学习框架，设计新的玻璃检测头，包含两个核心模块：频率自注意力模块（FSAM）识别玻璃特定的光谱特征用于定位，几何接地模块（GeGB）将2D特征选择性地接地到3D几何中用于分割。

Result: 在七个标准GSD基准上取得SOTA性能，能很好地泛化到视频和多模态数据，并显著提升玻璃场景中的三维重建质量。

Conclusion: 将GSD从2D外观线索转向3D几何推理是有效的范式转变，为玻璃场景的理解与重建提供了更可靠的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Glass surface detection (GSD) is critical for scene understanding and reconstruction, and yet remains challenging due to the transparency and reflectivity of glass surfaces. Existing GSD methods typically rely on 2D appearance cues, which may fail in geometrically ambiguous scenes. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift: grounding GSD in 3D visual geometry to explicitly model the physical existence of glass surfaces. Our method first distills rich 3D priors from the visual geometry grounded transformer (VGGT) and generates glass-aware 3D representations. It then exploits multi-tasking learning with a novel glass detection head, consisting of two core modules: a Frequency Self-Attention Module (FSAM) that identifies glass-specific spectral features for glass surface localization, and a Geometry Grounding Block (GeGB) that selectively grounds 2D features in 3D geometry for glass surface segmentation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across seven standard GSD benchmarks, generalizes well to video/multi-modal data, and substantially improves reconstruction in glass scenes. Code is available in https://github.com/YT3DVision/VGGT_GLASS.

</details>


### [38] [Cross-Architecture Knowledge Distillation from a Vision Foundation Model to a Lightweight Visual State Space Model for Tea Leaf Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26771)
*Zibo Zhou,Zongsen Qiu,Rui Chen,Yujie Yao,Yue Zhou,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究从微调后的DINOv2教师模型（Vision Transformer）向轻量级双向视觉状态空间模型（LVSSM）学生模型进行跨架构知识蒸馏，用于茶叶叶片病害分类。通过修复两个训练稳定性问题（单个大补丁嵌入卷积和切断残差路径的融合层），采用渐进式卷积主干和门控双向选择性扫描模块，4.45M参数的紧凑学生模型在三颗种子上将测试准确率从92.32±2.14%提升至95.41±1.17%，平均提升3.09个百分点，参数仅为教师的1/5，同时保留了教师98.3%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上部署茶叶病害分类模型面临计算预算紧张的问题：自监督视觉基础模型（如DINOv2）特征强大但体积过大不适合现场部署，而轻量级模型在小型农业数据集上从头训练容易欠拟合。本文探索将知识蒸馏作为弥合这一差距的手段，特别关注跨架构蒸馏这一未被充分研究的领域。

Method: 采用跨架构知识蒸馏框架，将微调后的DINOv2（ViT架构）作为教师，紧凑的双向视觉状态空间模型（LVSSM）作为学生。识别并修复了两个导致SSM学生训练不稳定的问题：单个大尺寸补丁嵌入卷积层和切断残差路径的融合层，改用渐进式卷积主干和门控双向选择性扫描块。蒸馏采用温度缩放的logit级别蒸馏，并对中间特征对齐损失、从零开始训练等配置进行了消融实验。

Result: 在三颗种子上，温度缩放logit蒸馏将测试准确率从92.32±2.14%提升至95.41±1.17%（最佳单次运行96.20%，宏F1为94.45%），平均提升3.09个百分点。学生模型仅4.45M参数，比22M参数的教师少5倍，保留其98.3%的准确率。消融实验表明中间特征对齐损失会降低准确率，简单logit级蒸馏是最强配置；公平的从头训练对比显示增益对起始于教师水平之下的学生具有特异性。研究还报告了每类指标、混淆矩阵、bootstrap置信区间以及FLOPs/延迟测量，并讨论了单数据集范围和简化非官方SSM实现等局限。

Conclusion: 跨架构知识蒸馏（ViT教师到SSM学生）可以有效提升轻量级模型在小规模农业数据集上的性能，但前提是必须解决SSM架构固有的训练稳定性问题。logit级蒸馏优于中间特征对齐，蒸馏增益在教师性能优于学生时最为显著。该工作为边缘设备上的农业AI部署提供了可行路径，但结论需在更多数据集和更全面的SSM实现中进一步验证。

Abstract: Automated tea leaf disease classification supports precision agriculture, yet deploying accurate models on edge devices remains challenging under tight compute budgets. Self-supervised vision foundation models such as DINOv2 provide strong features but are too large for field deployment, while lightweight models trained from scratch on small agricultural datasets often underfit. We study cross-architecture knowledge distillation (KD) from a fine-tuned DINOv2 teacher (Vision Transformer) to a compact bidirectional Visual State Space Model (LVSSM) student, an underexplored direction because the architectures use fundamentally different token-mixing mechanisms. We identify and fix two training-stability problems that prevent the from-scratch SSM student from learning on limited data: a single large patch-embedding convolution and a fusion layer that severs the residual path. With a progressive convolutional stem and gated bidirectional selective-scan block, the 4.45M-parameter student trains stably. Across three seeds, temperature-scaled logit distillation raises test accuracy from 92.32+/-2.14% to 95.41+/-1.17% (best single run: 96.20%; macro-F1: 94.45%), a +3.09 percentage-point mean gain. The student uses 5.0 times fewer parameters than the 22M-parameter teacher while retaining 98.3% of its accuracy. Ablations show that intermediate feature-alignment losses reduce accuracy, making simple logit-level KD the strongest configuration. A fair from-scratch comparison shows the gain is specific to students that start below the teacher. We report per-class metrics, confusion matrices, bootstrap confidence intervals, and FLOPs/latency measurements, and discuss limitations including the single-dataset scope and simplified non-official SSM implementation.

</details>


### [39] [Ring Forcing: Towards Precise Long-Term Memory for Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26794)
*Bowen Xue,Brandon Y. Feng,Chenguo Lin,Yuchen Lin,Yujia Zeng,Lvmin Zhang,Maneesh Agrawala,Honglei Yan,Panwang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ring Forcing，一种自回归视频扩散框架，用于解决长视频生成中缺乏长期记忆的问题。通过环状训练策略强制从历史中检索信息，结合压缩与时间步组合策略及稀疏RoPE机制，实现分钟级长时间视频的一致性和物体持久性，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成存在关键瓶颈：现有模型缺乏稳健的长期记忆。这一问题体现在两个方面——物体持久性（物体重现时能否精确复现其外观）和记忆容量（能否处理超长上下文并利用远距离历史信息）。两者缺一不可：仅有物体持久性而缺乏上下文处理能力会限制时间范围，仅有长上下文而缺乏持久性则无法维持身份一致性。

Method: 1) Ring Forcing：环状训练策略，强制模型从远距离历史中进行检索，平衡严格历史遵循与生成多样性之间的权衡；2) 压缩与时间步组合策略：在固定序列长度约束下扩展有效历史跨度至分钟级时长，实现对整个历史的全面感受野；3) 稀疏RoPE机制：实现灵活可扩展的记忆适应，同时充分利用预训练先验。

Result: 大量实验表明Ring Forcing在分钟级长时间一致性和物体持久性方面优于现有最先进方法，取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: Ring Forcing通过环状检索训练、压缩与时间步组合策略以及稀疏RoPE机制，有效解决了长视频生成中长期记忆缺失的问题，实现了分钟级长时间视频的稳健一致性和物体持久性。

Abstract: Scaling video generation to long durations reveals a critical bottleneck: current models lack robust long-term memory. This deficiency can be studied along two critical aspects: object permanence, the ability to precisely reproduce the appearance of objects upon re-entry; and memory capacity, the ability to process ultra-long context and use information from distant history. Robust long-term memory requires both: object permanence without sufficient context handling limits the temporal scope, while long context length without permanence fails to maintain identity. To address this, we present Ring Forcing, an autoregressive video diffusion framework designed to robustly construct and precisely utilize long-term memory. Our ring-structured training strategy enforces retrieval from distant history, effectively reconciling the trade-off between strict historical adherence and generative diversity. To expand memory capacity, we introduce a compression and timestep composition strategy. Under fixed sequence length constraints, this method extends the effective historical span to minutes-long durations and achieves a comprehensive receptive field over the entire history. Furthermore, we present a sparse RoPE mechanism to enable flexible, scalable memory adaptation while fully exploiting pre-trained priors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Ring Forcing achieves superior minutes-long coherence and object permanence, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [40] [Multi-Image Visual Token Pruning in Large Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26806)
*Rongyang Zhang,Chengqiang Lu,Cong Li,Hongchao Gu,Tingjia Shen,Xuyang Zhi,Qimeng Wang,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的自适应视觉令牌剪枝框架（AVTP），通过基于经验分析确定剪枝层和多图像场景下的自适应剪枝比率，在多种LVLM架构上实现推理加速的同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉令牌剪枝方法大多依赖静态策略，难以适应不同架构的LVLM和多图像场景，且受限于需要注意力计算，无法与FlashAttention等高效技术兼容。

Method: AVTP框架包含两个核心策略：（1）基于对不同LVLM中视觉注意力分布的经验分析，策略性地确定剪枝层；（2）在多图像上下文中实现自适应剪枝比率，对重要性更高的图像保留更多令牌。

Result: 在多个多图像基准测试中，Qwen3VL-8B实现2倍推理加速同时保持96.1%的原始准确率，InternVL3.5-8B保持94.1%准确率，LLaVA-OV-7B甚至超过原始基线性能。

Conclusion: AVTP作为训练免费的即插即用框架，能跨不同LVLM架构有效工作，兼顾推理效率与准确率，且不依赖注意力计算的限制，兼容高效的推理技术。

Abstract: With the growing demand for processing multiple image sequences in real-world applications, various visual token pruning methods have emerged to mitigate the computational and context length constraints faced by Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). However, most existing pruning approaches rely on static strategies that struggle to adapt across different architectural LVLMs and multi-image scenarios, and are additionally constrained by their dependence on attention computations that are incompatible with efficient techniques like FlashAttention. To address these limitations, we propose a training-free, Adaptive Visual Token Pruning (AVTP) framework, applicable to diverse LVLM architectures. We strategically determine pruning layers based on empirical analysis of visual attention distributions across various LVLMs, and implement adaptive pruning ratios in multi-image contexts where images of higher importance retain proportionally more tokens. We conduct extensive experiments across different LVLMs to demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of AVTP. Specifically, Qwen3VL-8B achieves 2 times inference speedup while maintaining 96.1\% of its original accuracy on multiple multi-image benchmarks, InternVL3.5-8B retains 94.1\% accuracy, and LLaVA-OV-7B even exceeds its original baseline performance. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/zry13/AVTP}{this link}.

</details>


### [41] [Thinking on Shots: Consistent Multi-Shot Video Editing with Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26809)
*Chenyang Wu,Fuchen Long,Binyuan Huang,Xinlong Sun,Xi Chen,Chun-Le Guo,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMLVE任务，针对长视频多指令编辑的挑战，引入基于LLM和VLM协同的智能体编辑框架，并构建MMLVE-Bench基准数据集与三个评测指标，实验证明其优于Seedance 2.0等现有闭源SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI视频编辑方法主要针对单镜头或短视频，编辑长视频并处理多个指令仍是巨大挑战。朴素的固定时长分块策略会导致实体碎片化、编辑幻觉和时序连续性断裂，亟需一种能够处理多指令多镜头长视频编辑的解决方案。

Method: 提出MMLVE-Agent智能体编辑框架，利用LLM与VLM的协同实现镜头级视频解耦与精确指令解析，围绕三个核心目标（CSEC、MID、ZDSS）设计；同时构建了包含复杂时空动态、高密度异构指令和稀疏随机实体分布的真实场景数据集MMLVE-Bench，并开发了三个专项评测指标。

Result: 大量实验表明MMLVE-Agent在长视频多指令编辑任务上超越了Seedance 2.0等闭源SOTA方法，成功消除了编辑幻觉，保持了跨镜头编辑一致性，并实现了无缝的时空过渡。

Conclusion: 通过LLM与VLM协同的智能体框架，可以有效解决长视频多指令编辑中的实体碎片化、编辑幻觉和时序断裂问题，为长视频编辑领域提供了新的任务定义、数据基准和可行方案。

Abstract: While generative AI has significantly advanced video editing, existing methods primarily focus on single-shot or short video clips. Editing long videos with multiple instructions remains a formidable challenge. Naive chunking strategies, e.g., fixed-duration segmentation, often lead to entity fragmentation, severe editing hallucinations, and disrupted temporal continuity. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Multi-Instruction Multi-Shot Long-Video Editing (MMLVE) task, which is structured around three core objectives: Cross-Shot Editing Consistency (CSEC), Multi-Instruction Decoupling (MID), and Zero-Destruction on Spatiotemporal Structure (ZDSS). To tackle these three unique challenges, we introduce an agentic editing framework that leverages the synergy of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to achieve shot-level video decoupling and precise instruction parsing. Furthermore, to comprehensively evaluate this task, we construct MMLVE-Bench, which is an MMLVE-focused dataset characterized by complex real-world spatiotemporal dynamics, high-density heterogeneous instructions, and sparse, random entity distributions. Three MMLVE-focused evaluation metrics are further exploited to assess the quality of the editing results. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our MMLVE-Agent outperforms existing closed-source SOTA approaches (e.g., Seedance 2.0), successfully eliminating editing hallucinations, preserving cross-shot editing consistency, and attaining seamless spatiotemporal transitions.

</details>


### [42] [Hyperspectral Diffusion Equivariant Imaging (HyDiff-EI): A Self-supervised Framework for Hyperspectral Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26812)
*Shuo Li,Mike Davies,Mehrdad Yaghoobi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的高光谱扩散等变成像框架（HyDiff-EI），用于解决高光谱图像（HSI）修复问题。与依赖大规模预训练的传统扩散方法不同，HyDiff-EI是一种测试时优化框架，直接从单个受损HSI采集学习，不需要大规模标注数据集。该方法将等变一致性约束嵌入扩散过程，利用HSI的几何对称性和内在特性，弥合生成扩散建模与自洽物理先验之间的差距。在Chikusei、Botswana和EMIT等真实数据集上的实验表明，HyDiff-EI在无噪声和有噪声情况下均显著优于现有的自监督和基于扩散的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩散的高光谱图像修复方法依赖大规模预训练，而实际遥感场景中标注的高光谱数据集稀缺，导致这些方法在不同传感器配置下的适应性受限。此外，无监督修复问题本身具有不适定性（ill-posed），需要有效的先验约束来保证求解质量。

Method: HyDiff-EI采用测试时优化框架，直接从一个受损HSI采集样本中学习，无需大规模预训练。核心创新在于将等变一致性约束（equivariant consistency constraints）嵌入扩散过程，利用高光谱图像固有的几何对称性和内在物理特征来约束修复过程，从而将生成扩散建模与自洽物理先验相结合。

Result: 在Chikusei、Botswana和EMIT等真实遥感数据集上的大量实验表明，HyDiff-EI在无噪声和有噪声两种情况下均取得了优于现有自监督算法和扩散算法的修复质量。等变先验与扩散建模的耦合显著增强了噪声鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将扩散模型与等变先验相结合能够有效提升高光谱图像修复的性能和鲁棒性。HyDiff-EI作为无需预训练的测试时优化框架，在标注数据有限的遥感场景中具有实用价值，为高光谱图像修复提供了一种灵活且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: A novel Hyperspectral diffusion Equivariant Imaging (HyDiff-EI) framework for solving the hyperspectral image (HSI) inpainting problem has been presented here. Unlike conventional diffusion-based methods that rely on large-scale pretraining, HyDiff-EI is a test-time optimization framework that learns directly from a single corrupted HSI acquisition. This makes it flexible for different sensor configurations and particularly well-suited for practical remote sensing scenarios where large annotated hyperspectral datasets are limited. To address the ill-posed nature of unsupervised inpainting, we embed equivariant consistency constraints within the diffusion process. By leveraging the inherent geometric symmetries and intrinsic characteristics of HSIs, HyDiff-EI bridges the gap between generative diffusion modeling and self-consistent physical priors. We empirically show that coupling diffusion modeling with equivariant priors substantially enhances noise robustness and generalizability. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets including Chikusei, Botswana, and EMIT demonstrate that HyDiff-EI offers remarkable inpainting quality over existing self-supervised and diffusion-based algorithms in both noiseless and noisy cases.

</details>


### [43] [LLaVAFlow: Preserving Latent Alignment Flow for Parameter-Efficient Multimodal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26820)
*Muyao Yuan,Muyan Jiao,Jiangyong Ying,Weizhan Zhang,Yuanhong Zhang,Lan Ma,Yuan Gao,Haipeng Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LLaVAFlow，一种基于信息论的蒸馏框架，通过压缩/最大化互信息来保留多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在视觉指令微调过程中的跨模态对齐流，缓解灾难性遗忘并同时提升下游任务性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉指令微调虽然赋予MLLMs下游任务能力，但不可避免地导致灾难性遗忘，损害整体泛化。现有方法仅通过约束权重更新来减少遗忘，却忽略了MLLMs中根本的跨模态对齐问题。作者基于先前工作与观察，认为跨模态对齐隐含在信息压缩轨迹中，因此需要一种方法在该轨迹上保留对齐流。

Method: 提出LLaVAFlow信息论蒸馏框架，包含两步：(1) 压缩提取关系与MLLM嵌入之间的互信息，促使可学习模块产生有益于下游任务的精炼对齐流；(2) 最大化预训练与微调后MLLM提取的对齐流之间的互信息，实现紧凑对齐信息的迁移。该方法设计为即插即用框架。

Result: 大量实验表明LLaVAFlow作为有效的即插即用框架，能够保留对齐流，同时提升下游任务性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态对齐流的信息保留是缓解MLLM灾难性遗忘的关键，通过信息论蒸馏框架可以有效实现对齐信息的可迁移压缩，从而兼顾下游性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit strong generalization, visual instruction tuning for downstream tasks inevitably causes catastrophic forgetting, impairing overall generalization. While existing methods regulate weight updates to reduce forgetting, they overlook the fundamental cross-modal alignment in MLLMs. Based on prior work and our observations, we argue that cross-modal alignment is implicitly captured in the information-compression trajectory. To preserve the alignment flow embedded in the trajectory, we propose LLaVAFlow, an information-theoretic distillation framework. First, we compress the mutual information between the extracted relations and MLLM embeddings, encouraging a learnable module to produce a refined alignment flow that benefits downstream tasks. Second, we maximize the mutual information between the extracted alignment flows of the pretrained and fine-tuned MLLMs, enabling the transfer of compact alignment information. Extensive experiments show that LLaVAFlow is an effective plug-and-play framework that preserves alignment flow and enhances both downstream performance and generalization.

</details>


### [44] [Evaluator-Dependent Patient-Adaptive ECG Lead-Channel Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26827)
*Xiaoyang Li,Zeyan Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过PTB-XL数据集上的对照实验证明，自适应ECG导联选择策略（如ECG-on-Demand和MGA）相对于固定方案的相对优势依赖于下游诊断评估器。当评估器从受控的逻辑回归更换为更强的掩码原始波形ResNet1D时，原本有利的自适应策略转为劣势（NLL交互效应+0.041，95%CI [0.030, 0.050]），且所有30个交互估计均为正且置信区间不包含零。三项敏感性分析排除了参考选择与掩码分布伪影的解释，表明自适应ECG通道分配应与其诊断骨干联合开发与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应ECG导联选择策略的优势是相对于特定下游诊断评估器定义的，但当前研究未充分关注边际效用的评估器依赖性问题——在一个评估器下学得的策略转移到另一个评估器时，其优势可能不复存在甚至逆转。作者旨在实证检验这一评估器依赖现象。

Method: 在PTB-XL上冻结两个预先训练的策略（ECG-on-Demand和MGA），这些策略最初在任意掩码的逻辑回归评估器下训练；然后用更强的掩码原始波形ResNet1D评估器在相同采集轨迹上评分。通过穷举搜索为每个评估器单独匹配群体固定的比较方案，以计算干净的交互对比。预算k=4在保留评估折上测量NLL交互效应，并执行三项敏感性分析（通用参考评分、混合掩码训练强评估器、评估器对齐的策略重训练）。

Result: 在k=4时，ECG-on-Demand从受控评估器下的D=-0.011（偏好自适应）翻转为强评估器下的D=+0.029（偏好固定），NLL交互效应为+0.041（95% CI [+0.030, +0.050]）。两个策略×五个预算×三个概率度量共30个交互估计全部为正且置信区间排除零。三项敏感性分析均保留正交互区间，但评估器对齐训练缩小而非消除差距。

Conclusion: 自适应ECG通道分配的优势具有显著的评估器依赖特征，其开发与验证必须与预期诊断骨架联合进行。现状下，跨评估器的策略迁移不可靠，联合优化的传感-诊断系统仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Patient-conditioned acquisition policies for ECG lead-channel selection can outperform population-wide fixed protocols by tailoring the channel budget to each patient's observed cardiac state. However, the value of acquiring any given channel is defined relative to a downstream diagnostic evaluator, so marginal utilities learned under one evaluator need not transfer when the evaluator is replaced. We study this evaluator dependence empirically on PTB-XL by freezing two policies (ECG-on-Demand and MGA) trained with a controlled arbitrary-mask logistic evaluator, then scoring their unchanged acquisition trajectories with a more predictive masked raw-waveform ResNet1D. Exhaustive search provides metric-matched population-wide fixed comparators separately for each evaluator, enabling a clean interaction contrast. At budget $k=4$ on a held-out evaluation fold, ECG-on-Demand shifts from $D_\mathrm{C}=-0.011$ (favoring adaptive under the controlled evaluator) to $D_\mathrm{S}=+0.029$ (favoring fixed under the strong evaluator), yielding an NLL interaction of $+0.041$ (95% CI $[+0.030, +0.050]$). Across two policies, five budgets, and three probabilistic metrics, all 30 interaction estimates are positive with paired confidence intervals excluding zero. Three post-hoc sensitivity analyses -- common-reference scoring, training the strong evaluator on a mixture of policy-generated and random masks, and evaluator-aligned Strong-MGA policy training -- each preserve a positive interaction interval, making reference-choice and mask-distribution artifacts less plausible explanations. Evaluator-aligned training reduces but does not eliminate the gap. These results indicate that adaptive ECG channel allocation should be developed and validated jointly with its intended diagnostic backbone, and that jointly optimized sensing-diagnosis systems remain an open problem.

</details>


### [45] [Rethinking Image Processing for the Age of AI: A Problem-First Framework for Scientific Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26833)
*Guoping Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文批评当前AI图像处理研究中"模型优先"的研究模式，主张回归"问题优先"的研究路径，并提出六阶段工作流程以区分物理成像问题、求解原理、统计估计器和计算实现，旨在推动图像处理研究从基准分数驱动转向真实问题驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代AI的快速发展使得可用模型、公开数据集和基准排行榜唾手可得，这催生了一种"模型优先"的研究模式：研究者从现成架构出发在公开基准上优化，而非从真实成像问题出发。这种模式可能产出亮眼的基准分数，却未必加深对真实问题的理解或解决实际问题。因此，作者呼吁回归以问题为先导的研究范式。

Method: 本文通过超分辨率和低光增强两个典型案例，论证基准数据集定义的任务与真实世界问题之间存在显著偏差，并指出性能提升必须在其获取条件下进行解读。在此基础上，提出六阶段工作流程：问题建模、图像采集、信息损失分析、假设确立、歧义识别和评估，全部置于模型与数据集选择之前。此外还建议建立更清晰的证据标准、可复现性标准、不确定度量化和SOTA声明规范。

Result: 论文揭示了基准驱动的图像处理研究范式所导致的任务定义偏差与性能评估误导问题，并系统性地提出了一套问题优先的研究框架与评估标准，同时呼吁文化与教育层面的研究范式转变。

Conclusion: 为推进图像处理的真实科学和技术进步，研究界需要将问题置于模型之前，将物理理解和信息分析置于基准优化之前，并在研究文化、教育和评估标准上进行根本性变革，使未来研究者能够深入理解成像问题并用现代AI实现真正的突破。

Abstract: Modern AI has greatly expanded the capabilities of image processing. However, the ready availability of powerful models, public datasets, and benchmark leaderboards has also en- couraged a model-first research pattern: researchers increasingly begin with an available architecture and optimize it on a public benchmark, rather than beginning with the underlying real-world imaging problem. This can produce impressive benchmark results without necessarily improving our understanding or solution of the real problem. This paper argues for a problem-first approach that distinguishes the physical imaging problem, solution principle, statistical estimator, and computational implementation, while clarifying what modern AI can achieve and which fundamental problems remain unsolved. Through case studies of super- resolution and low-light enhancement, we show how benchmark datasets may define tasks that differ substantially from the real-world problems they are intended to represent, and why performance improvements must be interpreted within the conditions under which they are obtained. We propose a six-stage workflow that places problem formulation, image acquisition, information-loss analysis, assumptions, ambiguity, and evaluation before model and dataset selection. The paper also proposes clearer standards for evidence, reproducibility, uncertainty, and claims of state-of-the-art performance. More fundamentally, it calls for a change in research culture and education so that future researchers learn to understand imaging problems deeply and use modern AI to achieve genuine scientific and technical advancement.

</details>


### [46] [MedFG-VQA: Low-Frequency Memory and Graph Attention for Lightweight Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26848)
*Haowen Gu,Gensheng Pei,Zeren Sun,Mingwu Ren,Xiangbo Shu,Yazhou Yao,Fumin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MedFG-VQA，一个轻量级医学视觉问答框架，通过DCT分解和可学习记忆库增强低频特征，并结合图增强交叉注意力实现视觉-文本对齐；同时构建了包含200万+问答对的合成数据集SynMed-VQA（覆盖9种成像模态、10个主要器官）。实验表明其以远低于大规模模型的算力获得了可比或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Med-VQA在临床决策支持中具有重要价值，但面临两个核心瓶颈：一是标注数据稀缺，二是现有大规模视觉-语言模型计算开销过高，难以满足临床部署需求。

Method: MedFG-VQA包含两个关键组件：(1) 频率记忆融合（FMF），通过对图像进行DCT分解，从可学习的记忆库中检索以增强低频特征；(2) 图感知交叉注意力（GACA），通过交叉注意力对齐视觉-文本特征，并利用图卷积聚合进一步细化特征。此外，利用GPT-4o构建了包含200万+问答对、覆盖9种成像模态和10个主要器官的大规模合成数据集SynMed-VQA以缓解数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在SynMed-VQA及三个标准医学VQA基准上的大量实验表明，MedFG-VQA在显著降低计算成本的同时，取得了与更大规模模型相比具有竞争力甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: MedFG-VQA通过轻量级设计和高效的特征增强与对齐策略，在性能和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，展示了其在临床场景中部署的潜力；大规模合成数据集SynMed-VQA也有望为未来Med-VQA研究提供数据支撑。

Abstract: Medical Visual Question Answering (Med-VQA) holds significant promise for clinical decision support, yet faces challenges due to limited annotated data and the high computational demands of existing large vision-language models. We propose MedFG-VQA, a lightweight framework that leverages a memory bank to augment DCT-based low-frequency features and employs graph-enhanced cross-attention for effective visual-textual alignment. Specifically, our approach features two key components: Frequency-Memory Fusion (FMF), which enhances low-frequency features by retrieving from a learnable memory bank built on DCT decomposition, and Graph-Aware Cross-Attention (GACA), which aligns visual-textual features via cross-attention and refines them through graph-convolutional aggregation. To address data scarcity, we construct SynMed-VQA, a large-scale synthetic dataset comprising over 2 million question-answer pairs across 9 imaging modalities and 10 major organs, generated with GPT-4o. Extensive experiments on SynMed-VQA and three other standard biomedical VQA benchmarks demonstrate that MedFG-VQA achieves competitive or superior performance compared to much larger models while maintaining significantly lower computational costs, highlighting its efficiency and potential for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [47] [From Reasoning to Pixels: Grounded Medical Multimodal LLMs for VQA and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26856)
*Haowen Gu,Gensheng Pei,Junzhu Mao,Qiong Wang,Mingwu Ren,Yazhou Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MedREAL，一个将语言推理与空间定位对齐的医学推理驱动问答与定位统一框架。通过引入 Seg Anchored Reasoning Pooling (SARP) 从 MLLM 的 [SEG] token 中提取任务相关的语义证据，并提出 Reasoning-to-Visual (R2V) 融合机制将这些推理感知特征注入分割管线，实现精确的掩码解码。同时构建了包含 13,824 个专家验证样本、覆盖四种成像模态的 MedRAVS-13K 数据集。实验表明 MedREAL 在基准评测上达到 68.49% gIoU 和 70.47% cIoU，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医学视觉问答（Med-VQA）中表现出色，但其依赖全局图像特征，缺乏精确的像素级定位能力，限制了临床可信度。为了弥合高层临床推理与空间定位之间的语义鸿沟，亟需一个能将语言推理与空间定位对齐的统一框架。

Method: MedREAL 包含两个核心模块：(1) Seg Anchored Reasoning Pooling (SARP)——从 MLLM 隐藏状态中的 [SEG] token 蒸馏任务相关的语义证据；(2) Reasoning-to-Visual (R2V) 融合机制——将这些推理感知特征有效注入分割管线，实现准确的掩码解码。此外，构建了包含 13,824 个专家验证样本、覆盖四种成像模态的 MedRAVS-13K 数据集以支撑该范式。

Result: 在基准评估中，MedREAL 显著优于现有最先进方法，达到 68.49% 的 gIoU 和 70.47% 的 cIoU。生成的证据掩码与文本诊断严格一致，提供了稳健且可解释的推理驱动医学图像分析框架。

Conclusion: 通过生成与文本诊断严格一致的证据掩码，MedREAL 成功弥合了高层临床推理与空间定位之间的鸿沟，为推理驱动的医学图像分析提供了一个稳健、可解释的统一框架，从而增强了 MLLM 在临床场景中的可信度。

Abstract: Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in Medical Visual Question Answering (Med-VQA), their reliance on global image features often lacks precise pixel-level grounding, thereby limiting clinical trustworthiness. To bridge the semantic gap between high-level clinical reasoning and spatial localization, we propose \textsc{\textsc{MedREAL}} (\textbf{Med}ical \textbf{RE}asoning-driven \textbf{A}nswering and \textbf{L}ocalization), a unified framework that seamlessly aligns linguistic reasoning with spatial grounding. Specifically, \textsc{MedREAL} introduces \textbf{S}eg \textbf{A}nchored \textbf{R}easoning \textbf{P}ooling (SARP) to distill task-relevant semantic evidence directly from \texttt{[SEG]} tokens within the MLLM's hidden states. Furthermore, a \textbf{R}easoning-to-\textbf{V}isual (R2V) fusion mechanism is proposed to effectively inject these reasoning-aware features into a segmentation pipeline for accurate mask decoding. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct MedRAVS-13K, a comprehensive dataset comprising 13,824 expertly validated samples across four diverse imaging modalities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textsc{MedREAL} significantly outperforms state-of-the-arts, achieving 68.49\% gIoU and 70.47\% cIoU on benchmark evaluations. By generating evidence masks that are strictly consistent with textual diagnoses, \textsc{MedREAL} provides a robust, interpretable framework for reasoning-driven medical image analysis.

</details>


### [48] [A Geometry-Driven, Framework-Agnostic Optimization for Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26859)
*Wei Chen,Tao Zhen,Zhongchen Shi,Jing Zhang,Liang Xie,Erwei Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的物体姿态估计优化方法：通过主惯性轴对齐来构建物理约束的旋转表示，在数据集层面进行规范化和对称性消歧，无需修改网络架构即可提升各类姿态估计模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体姿态估计研究主要聚焦于网络架构创新和后处理优化，忽视了数据层面的改进潜力。作者希望通过数据层面的物理几何约束来提升姿态估计性能，避免复杂的网络重新设计。

Method: 通过主惯性轴（principal axes）对齐将物体坐标系与其固有几何轴对齐，利用惯量属性推导旋转表示。核心包括三点：利用主轴的能耗最小化性质增强噪声和遮挡鲁棒性；在数据层面显式解决对称物体的旋转歧义，消除训练中标签混淆；优化仅在数据集层面进行，实现与现有网络的即插即用兼容。

Result: 在多种类别级和实例级姿态估计模型上进行了验证，实验表明该方法在保持基线网络完整性的同时，一致性地显著提升了姿态估计精度。

Conclusion: 本工作开辟了基于几何驱动的姿态估计增强新方向，通过在数据层面进行物理约束的旋转规范化，绕过了复杂网络重新设计的需要，建立了数据中心化的姿态估计优化范式。

Abstract: Current object pose estimation research remains predominantly model-centric, focusing on architectural innovations and post-processing refinements. This paper introduces a data-centric optimization by proposing a novel, physically grounded rotation representation through principal axes alignment. Our method aligns the object's coordinate system with its inherent geometric axes, derived from inertial properties, yielding three key advantages: Inherent Stability-leveraging the energy-minimizing property of principal axes provides a robust representation that is less sensitive to noise and occlusions; Symmetry-Aware Canonicalization-explicitly resolving rotational ambiguities for symmetric objects at the data level, which fundamentally eliminates label confusion during network training; and Framework Agnosticism-the optimization is applied purely at the dataset level, ensuring plug-and-play compatibility with existing networks without any architectural modification. We validate the framework across diverse category-level and instance-level models. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent and significant accuracy improvements, while preserving the integrity of the baseline network. This work establishes a new, geometry-driven direction for enhancing pose estimation, circumventing the need for complex network redesign.

</details>


### [49] [FIDA: Feature Instability-Driven Attack on Self-Supervised Facial Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26861)
*Zhiyang Chen,Changchun Yin,Huiqin Yang,Liming Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FIDA（特征不稳定性驱动的后门攻击）框架，针对自监督人脸表示学习中的后门攻击隐蔽性问题，通过特征不稳定性损失使后门特征沿扰动方向具有高灵敏度，从而绕过基于扰动的防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习模型容易遭受后门攻击，但针对人脸表示领域的系统性风险研究很少。自监督人脸学习中身份特征的纠缠性给攻击隐蔽性带来独特挑战，现有攻击存在特征模式僵化、易被扰动防御检测的问题。

Method: 提出FIDA框架，核心创新是特征不稳定性损失（Feature Instability Loss）。该方法使用微妙的语义触发器进行注入，通过在攻击优化过程中采样扰动方向，训练编码器增加触发特征沿该方向的灵敏度，从而避免后门表现出刚性特征模式，实现防御规避。

Result: 实验表明FIDA在评估设置下实现了高攻击成功率，同时保持良性输入的可用性，有效规避基于扰动的防御，对依赖人脸分析的真实多媒体应用构成显著威胁。

Conclusion: FIDA证明了自监督人脸表示学习中后门攻击的隐蔽性可以显著增强，其通过特征不稳定性设计突破了传统刚性后门特征的局限，揭示了现有扰动防御在实际部署中的脆弱性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) models are vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, the systemic risks they pose in face representation have received little attention. The entanglement of identity features in self-supervised face learning presents unique challenges for attack stealthiness. To address this gap, we propose FIDA (Feature Instability-Driven Attack), a novel backdoor attack framework. FIDA uses subtle semantic triggers for injection, but its key innovation is a novel objective called Feature Instability Loss. It trains the encoder to increase the sensitivity of triggered features along perturbation directions sampled during attack optimization . By preventing the backdoor from exhibiting the rigid feature patterns typical of previous attacks, FIDA effectively evades the evaluated perturbation-based defenses. Experiments show that FIDA achieves a high attack success rate and generally preserves benign utility across the evaluated settings , posing a significant threat to real-world multimedia applications relying on facial analysis.

</details>


### [50] [Order Matters: A Chinese Multi-Panel Meme Benchmark for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26866)
*Haihan Li,Haihao Li,Zhenfei Xu,Jize Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CMPM——一个中文多面板表情包基准测试集，包含1,214个标注样本，覆盖五种结构类型，并设计了双层评估框架（顺序敏感的面板排序与中文表情包解释生成），用于考察大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）能否进行面向序列的多模态推理。基准测试5个代表性LVLMs后发现：标准显示精度不能证明顺序理解能力，打乱顺序后精度显著下降，暴露了模型在顺序敏感推理上的持续短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多多模态任务的效果不仅取决于识别单个视觉元素，还取决于视觉元素的排列顺序与组合方式。互联网表情包正是这一问题的典型代表——其笑点常依赖于受限的阅读顺序和跨面板的图文线索。尽管大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在单图理解上表现强劲，但它们在结构化表情包布局上是否具备序列感知推理能力仍不清楚，尤其是在中文社交媒体场景下缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建CMPM（Chinese Multi-Panel Meme）基准，含1,214个标注样本，涵盖五种结构类型、排序依赖、面板顺序约束及可选评论上下文。采用双层评估框架：任务1测试结构类型识别和顺序敏感的面板排序（含上下文消融设置）；任务2评估中文表情包解释生成，通过五个1-3级李克特维度的人工评分（视觉、面板、幽默、上下文、忠实度）进行评价，并在统一协议下基准测试五个代表性LVLM。

Result: 标准显示顺序下的准确率不能作为顺序理解能力的证据：主要打乱条件（shuffled condition）下准确率急剧下降，揭示了模型在顺序敏感多模态推理上的持续差距。任务2偏好方面，Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.5优于开源模型，而评论上下文仅带来微小且混合的Core4增益。

Conclusion: 现有LVLMs在中文多面板表情包上缺乏稳健的顺序感知推理能力，标准评估设置可能高估其真实理解水平；该领域需要更严格的顺序扰动评测与进一步改进方向。

Abstract: Many multimodal tasks depend on how visual elements are ordered and composed, not only on recognizing them in isolation. Internet memes are a compact case of this problem: their punchline often depends on a constrained reading order and cross-panel visual--textual cues. While large vision-language models (LVLMs) show strong performance on single-image understanding, it remains unclear whether they can perform sequence-aware reasoning over structured meme layouts, especially in Chinese social media. We introduce CMPM, a Chinese Multi-Panel Meme benchmark with 1,214 annotated samples covering five structural types, ordering dependency, panel-order constraints, and optional comment context. We formulate a two-layer evaluation: Task1 probes structure typing and order-sensitive panel sequencing (with a context ablation setting), and Task2 evaluates Chinese meme explanation generation with human ratings on five 1-3 Likert dimensions (visual, panel, humor, context, and faithfulness). We benchmark five representative LVLMs under a unified protocol. Results indicate that canonical-display accuracy is not by itself evidence of order understanding: the primary shuffled condition produces a sharp accuracy drop, revealing a persistent gap in order-sensitive multimodal reasoning. Task2 preferences place Gemini 3.1 Pro and GPT-5.5 above the open models, while comment context yields only a small and mixed Core4 gain. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [51] [CGS-SLAM: Collaborative Gaussian Splatting based SLAM for Multi-Agent Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26868)
*Jean-Daniel de Ambrogi,Aladine Chetouani,Vincent Nguyen,Aurélien Chateigner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CGS-SLAM 是一个混合去中心化/中心化的多智能体 3DGS SLAM 系统，仅依靠 RGB 和惯性数据进行多智能体的稠密重建与协同定位。每个智能体利用惯性先验进行局部跟踪，并通过单目深度估计器重建带尺度的地图；关键帧编码在智能体间共享以实现动态关键帧选择，中心服务器使用 VGGT 进行子图对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 3DGS 的 SLAM 方法大多依赖 RGB-D 输入，而消费级智能手机不配备深度传感器；此外，很少有工作将 3DGS 集成到多智能体协作框架中。因此需要一种仅依赖 RGB 和惯性数据、同时支持多智能体协作的 3DGS SLAM 方案。

Method: 采用混合去中心化/中心化架构：(1) 每个智能体以惯性数据作为运动先验进行局部跟踪，使用度量单目深度估计器 Depth Pro 重建带尺度信息的稠密地图；(2) 智能体间共享关键帧编码，在空间重叠区域实现动态关键帧选择，增强子图对齐能力；(3) 中心服务器使用 VGGT 作为视图对齐模型进行子图配准；通过双向通信在构图和全局重建阶段保持低通信开销。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，CGS-SLAM 在跟踪精度方面具有竞争力，渲染质量优于现有最先进方法，且能实现准确的子图对齐。

Conclusion: CGS-SLAM 证明了仅使用 RGB 和惯性数据即可实现多智能体 3DGS SLAM，在 GNSS 受限环境下能以低通信成本完成高精度的全局重建与子图对齐，为消费级设备的协同 SLAM 提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in SLAM have leveraged 3DGS for photorealistic reconstruction and novel view synthesis. However, most methods rely on RGB-D input, which is unavailable on consumer-grade smartphones, and few integrate 3DGS within a collaborative framework. Therefore, we present CGS-SLAM, a hybrid decentralized/centralized system enabling multi-agent 3DGS SLAM using only RGB and inertial data. Each agent performs local tracking with inertial data as a motion prior and reconstructs a scaled map using a metric monocular depth estimator (Depth Pro). Keyframe encodings are shared among agents, enabling dynamic keyframing in regions of spatial overlaps with other agents, enhancing submap alignment. Afterwards, a central server aligns submaps using VGGT as a view alignment model. This bidirectional communication keeps communication cost low during mapping and global reconstruction in difficult GNSS-denied environments. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate competitive tracking performance, improved rendering quality over state-of-the-art methods, and accurate submap alignment.

</details>


### [52] [Self-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Flow Matching Models without Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26872)
*Shiyi Zhang,Mushui Liu,Yunze Tong,Wanggui He,Siyu Zou,Jinlong Liu,Yunlong Yu,Jian Song,Hao Jiang,Pipei Huang,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self-OPD，一种无需教师模型的流匹配模型在线策略蒸馏框架，通过学生自身的随机探索分支与确定性自参考基线对比生成逐步监督信号，利用全分支拉-推目标优化速度场，在多目标对齐中通过归一化分数融合避免梯度冲突，在单目标和混合奖励基准上超越现有RL和OPD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在流匹配模型中存在两大问题：一是为每个新目标训练单独的任务特定教师模型计算成本高昂；二是教师与学生分布之间的差异导致沿生成轨迹产生复合误差（compounding errors）。作者希望消除对独立教师模型的依赖，同时缓解分布差异带来的误差累积问题。

Method: Self-OPD框架的核心包括：（1）在每个时间步将确定性的下一状态预测分支为K个随机SDE候选，并用ODE采样器滚动展开；（2）将各分支的奖励与确定性自参考基线对比，计算归一化优势值；（3）采用全分支拉-推（pull-push）目标优化速度场——高优势分支吸引学生、低优势分支排斥学生，并引入方向感知衰减和SDE方差归一化；（4）多目标对齐时在奖励层面融合归一化分数，避免直接梯度冲突。

Result: 在单目标和混合（多目标）奖励基准上的实验表明，Self-OPD在不使用任务特定教师模型的情况下，性能优于先前的强化学习（RL）方法和在线策略蒸馏（OPD）方法。

Conclusion: Self-OPD证明了流匹配模型可以完全摆脱外部教师模型的依赖，通过学生自身的自探索与自参考基线实现有效的策略优化，同时以奖励级归一化分数融合的方式优雅地处理多目标对齐问题，为流匹配模型的在线优化提供了一种更高效、更泛化的范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD), which leverages a pre-trained, specialized teacher model to provide dense supervisory signals, has achieved significant success in Large Language Models (LLMs) and has recently been adapted to flow matching models. However, this paradigm suffers from two major issues: First, training a separate, task-specific teacher for every new objective incurs high computational costs. Second, the discrepancy between teacher and student distributions often leads to compounding errors along the generation trajectory. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{Self-OPD}, a teacher-free OPD framework for flow matching models that turns the student's own self-exploration into step-wise supervision. At each timestep, Self-OPD branches the deterministic next-state prediction into $K$ stochastic SDE candidates, rolls them out with the ODE sampler, and compares their rewards against a deterministic self-reference baseline to obtain normalized advantages. The velocity field is optimized with an all-branch pull-push objective, where high-advantage branches attract the student and low-advantage branches repel it under direction-aware attenuation and SDE-variance normalization. For multi-objective alignment, Self-OPD fuses normalized scores at the reward level, avoiding direct gradient conflict. Experiments on single and mixed reward benchmarks show that Self-OPD outperforms prior RL and OPD methods without task-specific teachers.

</details>


### [53] [Tether the Subject, Release the Scene: Query-Aware Memory Routing for Long-Horizon Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26902)
*Chen Li,Peng Zhang,Hanyu Zhou,Jialong Zuo,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TetherMem，一种无需训练的查询感知时空记忆路由器，用于冻结的自回归视频生成器。它通过分离主题查询和场景查询，并基于区域和年龄条件先验调节历史访问，解决了"记忆锚定场景欠进展"问题——即背景、视角和场景结构被锁定在历史状态而无法随局部运动推进。在2400次盲评中，TetherMem在整体质量和场景进展方面均优于8个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式自回归视频模型通常以相同策略让主题和场景查询访问历史记忆，虽然稳定了主题，却将背景、视角和场景结构锁定在先前生成的状态中，即使局部运动仍在继续。作者将这一失败模式称为"记忆锚定场景欠进展"，且仅靠一致性和运动指标难以捕捉。需要一个能区分主题与场景历史访问策略的解决方案。

Method: TetherMem是一种训练无关（training-free）的查询感知时空记忆路由器。核心设计包括：(1) 将主题（subject）查询和场景（scene）查询分离；(2) 通过区域条件和年龄条件先验（region- and age-conditioned priors）调节对历史记忆的访问——主题查询保留携带身份信息的历史，而场景查询减少对主题历史和过时背景的依赖。

Result: 在10位标注者的2400次盲法成对判断中，TetherMem在8个流式长视频基线中取得了最高的估计期望偏好：整体质量0.780，场景进展0.769。在完整的30秒视频上，它能够持续改变背景、视角和场景状态，同时保持主题可辨识性和时间连续性。

Conclusion: TetherMem通过查询感知的记忆路由策略，在不修改冻结视频生成器的前提下有效解决了长视频生成中场景进展不足的问题，实现了场景动态变化与主题一致性之间的平衡，为流式长视频生成提供了一个稳健且实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Streaming autoregressive video models generate long videos chunk by chunk, using historical memory to maintain consistency. Existing methods typically expose subject and scene queries to history through similar policies. This stabilizes the subject, but can also lock backgrounds, viewpoints, and scene structure to previously generated states even when local motion continues. We call this failure memory-anchored scene under-progression; consistency and motion metrics alone can miss it. We introduce TetherMem, a training-free, query-aware spatiotemporal memory router for frozen video generators. TetherMem separates subject and scene queries and modulates historical access with region- and age-conditioned priors: subject queries retain identity-bearing history, while scene queries reduce reliance on subject history and stale backgrounds. Across 2,400 blinded pairwise judgments from 10 annotators, TetherMem achieves the highest estimated expected preference among eight streaming long-video baselines for overall quality (0.780) and scene progression (0.769). On complete 30-second videos, it sustains changes in background, viewpoint, and scene state while preserving subject recognizability and temporal continuity.

</details>


### [54] [AraMS-28k: The Largest Publicly Released Line-Level Dataset of Historical Arabic Manuscripts with Margin and Insertion-Anchor Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26921)
*Mohamed Guechaoui,Mohamed Diaa Zellagui,Souleyman Chaib,Sahraoui Dhelim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了AraMS-28k，迄今最大的公开真实历史阿拉伯文字稿行级数据集，包含14本书、3,043页、28,600个标注文本行，涵盖Naskh、Ruq'ah、Maghrebi三种手写字体风格及一种石印印刷版。数据集首次在行级粒度上标注了页边注的插入锚点以恢复手稿的真实非线性阅读顺序，同时提供带变音符号原文及去变音符号两种转录版本，并给出了基于Kraken和HATFormer的基准HTR结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史阿拉伯文字稿的自动识别（HTR）、版式分析和阅读顺序恢复研究因缺乏大规模、高质量的公开数据集而受限。现有数据集规模小、缺少行级细粒度标注，且未涵盖阅读顺序恢复所需的插入锚点标注，无法支撑对该领域复杂挑战的深入研究。

Method: 构建了RefLAM参考锚定标注流水线：将多模态大模型OCR输出与独立来源的干净转录对照对齐，每行均经人工审查，结合自动验证与专家监督。数据集包含14本书（13本手抄本、1本石印本），每行标注为正文或页边注，其中可明确关联正文的页边注进一步标注插入锚点。所有行同时发布带变音符号的原始转录和去变音符号版本。

Result: 数据集包含28,600个标注行（27,971正文、629页边注），覆盖Naskh、Ruq'ah、Maghrebi三种脚本传统。提供了Kraken和HATFormer的基准HTR结果，展示了从分布内页面到完全未见书籍的跨脚本泛化梯度。数据集以CC BY-NC-SA 4.0许可发布，包含页面图像、行级标注和固定的训练/验证/测试划分。

Conclusion: AraMS-28k是历史阿拉伯文字稿领域首个提供行级阅读顺序恢复标注的公开数据集，其RefLAM构建流水线有效结合了自动验证与专家审查。该数据集为阿拉伯文字稿识别、版式分析和阅读顺序恢复提供了可复现的研究基准，填补了该领域的公开数据空白。

Abstract: We introduce AraMS-28k, the largest publicly released line-level dataset of genuine historical Arabic manuscripts, comprising 14 books, 3,043 pages, and 28,600 annotated text lines (27,971 main-text, 629 margin). Thirteen books are hand-copied manuscripts spanning three script traditions -- Naskh, Ruq'ah, and Maghrebi -- and one is a lithographed printed edition included to broaden format diversity. Each line is labelled as main-text or margin, and margin lines that have an unambiguous attachment point in the main text are further annotated with an insertion anchor, recovering the manuscript's true non-linear reading order at line-level granularity -- to our knowledge the first such annotation released for a historical Arabic manuscript corpus. Because reference transcriptions are fully vocalised while manuscript hands are typically undiacritised, we release both the raw diacritised transcription and a diacritic-normalised counterpart for every line. The dataset was constructed with RefLAM, a reference-grounded annotation pipeline that aligns multimodal-LLM OCR against independently sourced clean transcriptions and routes every line through human review, combining automatic verification with expert oversight. We describe the construction and quality-control process, present the annotation schema, report dataset statistics at both the corpus and per-book level, and provide baseline HTR results using Kraken and HATFormer, including a cross-script generalisation gradient from in-distribution pages to fully unseen books. AraMS-28k is released with page images, line-level annotations, and fixed train/val/test splits under CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 to support reproducible research on Arabic manuscript recognition, layout analysis, and reading-order recovery.

</details>


### [55] [KISS-GS: 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression Kept Simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26948)
*Wieland Morgenstern,Friedrich Elias Branschke,Florian Fleischmann,Adrian Szatmari,Paul Schlack,Florian Barthel,Peter Eisert,Anna Hilsmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KISS-GS 是一个模块化的3D高斯溅射(3DGS)压缩管线，将压缩与训练完全解耦。通过剪枝压缩15.7倍，结合SOG-XT图像编码格式压缩6.6倍，可选的编码感知微调再提升2.2倍，整体实现85x-319x的压缩率，解码仅依赖Web原生图像格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS场景重构虽然普及，但未压缩文件体积过大导致部署困难。现有3DGS压缩系统将多种策略混合使用，难以确定压缩收益的来源，也限制了组件在不同训练管线间的复用。

Method: 提出模块化压缩管线KISS-GS，核心设计为压缩与训练完全解耦。具体包括：(1) 利用最先进的剪枝策略组合进行紧凑化处理，压缩15.7倍；(2) 提出SOG-XT格式，包含两个主要贡献：自组织2D码本(Self-organizing 2D Codebooks)和平行代表赋值平滑(PRAS)，利用四元数和尺度参数化的对称性生成更适合编码的2D属性网格；(3) 可选的编码感知微调。

Result: 在标准3DGS基准上实现85x到319x的总压缩率，紧凑化贡献15.7倍，图像编码贡献6.6倍，可选微调再贡献2.2倍。在率失真性能上超越紧密集成的现有方法，并在真实场景中刷新基准。

Conclusion: KISS-GS通过将压缩各步骤模块化分离，使得收益来源透明化，解码仅依赖Web原生图像格式，具备普适性；模块化设计便于与未来重建和紧凑化技术结合，为3DGS高效部署提供了简洁可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Scene reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become common, however deployment remains painful as the uncompressed file sizes can be massive. Current 3DGS compression systems combine multiple strategies for file size reduction, which can obscure where gains come from and limit component reuse across training pipelines. To make the gains more transparent, we propose KISS-GS, a modular compression pipeline named after the principle of keeping things simple, designed to decouple compression entirely from training.
  Given a 3DGS scene reconstructed with vanilla 3DGS, we are able to reduce it through compaction by 15.7x using a combination of state-of-the-art pruning schemes. Then we encode it into an image-based format designed for simple, ubiquitous decoding. With the SOG-XT format, we propose a novel extension to Self-Organizing Gaussians with two main contributions: (i) Self-organizing 2D Codebooks and (ii) Parallel Representative Assignment Smoothing (PRAS), which leverages the symmetry of quaternion and scale parameterizations to produce 2D attribute grids more amenable to encoding.
  This encoding reduces scene size by 6.6x. We show that optional encoding-aware fine-tuning yields a further 2.2x. Across standard 3DGS benchmarks, our simple and modular approach thus achieves a total of 85x to 319x reductions in the size of the scene over uncompressed vanilla 3DGS, setting new benchmarks for real-world scenes and surpassing tightly integrated methods in rate-distortion. Decoding relies solely on web-native image formats, and the modular design makes each stage easy to combine with future advances in reconstruction and compaction.
  Code and project page: https://fraunhoferhhi.github.io/KISS-GS/

</details>


### [56] [Per-View Gaussian Predictions Enable Training-Free Distractor Filtering in Feed-Forward 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26951)
*Kangmin Seo,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的过滤流程，用于前馈3D高斯泼溅重建中的瞬态物体剔除。该方法利用逐视图预测结构，排除每个输入对应的Gaussians后渲染同一相机，通过特征相似性形成候选区域，并利用渲染验证保留能降低其他视角重建误差的候选区域，从而在不重训练或场景特定优化的前提下提升新视角质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D高斯泼溅重建虽然使3D重建对随拍场景更加便捷，但随拍中常含仅出现在部分视角的瞬态物体（transient objects）。这些内容会被编码进相关的逐视图Gaussians中，保留在组合表示里，导致新视角出现模糊、重复或漂浮伪影。现有方法未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出一种训练免费的过滤流程：1）对每个输入视角，排除其关联的Gaussians并用剩余表示渲染同一相机，以揭示与其他输入不一致的内容；2）利用特征相似性形成候选区域；3）通过基于渲染的验证，仅保留那些移除后能降低其他输入视角重建误差的候选。整个过程在单个冻结预测上运行，无需重训练或场景特定优化。

Result: 在三个重建模型和两个干扰物基准上，该方法一致性地提升了不同输入视角数量下的新视角质量。在干净场景中，四个模型的评估表明原始重建质量基本得到保留。

Conclusion: 该训练免费的过滤流程有效解决了前馈3D高斯泼溅重建中瞬态物体引发的伪影问题，在提升含干扰物场景的新视角质量的同时，保持了干净场景的原始重建性能，具有跨模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs an explicit Gaussian representation from multiple input images in one network execution, making 3D reconstruction increasingly accessible for casual captures. However, such captures frequently contain transient objects that appear in only a subset of the views. Such content can be encoded into the per-view Gaussians associated with the inputs that observe it and remain in the combined representation despite being observed by no other input. As a result, it may produce blurred, duplicated, or floating artifacts in novel views. We introduce a training-free filtering procedure that exploits this per-view prediction structure. For each input, we exclude its associated Gaussians and render the same camera using the remaining representation, revealing content that is inconsistent with the other inputs. Feature similarity forms candidate regions, and rendering-based verification retains only candidates whose removal reduces reconstruction error in the other input views. The procedure operates on a single frozen prediction without retraining or scene-specific optimization. Across three reconstruction models and two distractor benchmarks, it consistently improves novel-view quality with varying numbers of input views. On clean scenes, evaluations across four models show that the original reconstructions are largely preserved.

</details>


### [57] [RubricRM: Generative Reward Modeling via Dynamic Rubrics for Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26956)
*Zijian Kan,Wei Wang,Long Luo,Bing Zhao,Xuan Ren,Weixu Qiao,Wenbo Li,Hu Wei,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RubricRM提出了一种成对生成式奖励建模框架，先为每个输入生成包含评估维度、权重和评分标准的具体规则（rubric），再依据该规则对候选图像评分，在文生图和图像编辑任务上超越了现有专用奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉奖励模型大多使用单一标量分数或依赖固定标准，无法根据指令自适应调整评估维度，导致可解释性差、任务敏感度不足，尤其在文生图和指令式图像编辑中，不同输入需要不同的评估维度。

Method: 提出RubricRM成对生成式奖励建模框架，包括两个阶段训练：监督微调（SFT）教导模型基于规则的评分范式，GRPO通过细粒度维度级奖励进一步优化评分。为文生图和图像编辑分别训练专用模型。

Result: 在多个生成和编辑基准上的实验表明，RubricRM超越了现有专用奖励模型，并且在使用更小骨干网络的情况下仍能与强力的专有多模态大模型评判器相当。

Conclusion: RubricRM通过可解释的、输入自适应的规则生成与评分机制，有效提升了视觉奖励模型的评分质量与任务适应能力，同时模型、数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Reward models play an essential role in aligning visual generative models, yet most existing visual reward models use a single scalar score or rely on fixed criteria that cannot adapt to different instructions. This limits both interpretability and task sensitivity, especially for text-to-image generation and instruction-based image editing, where different inputs require different evaluation dimensions. We propose RubricRM, a pairwise generative reward modeling framework that first produces an input-specific rubric with evaluation dimensions, weights, and scoring criteria, and then applies the rubric to score candidate images. We train dedicated RubricRM models for text-to-image generation and image editing using a two-stage training pipeline: supervised fine-tuning teaches the model the rubric-based scoring paradigm, while GRPO further improves scoring through fine-grained dimension-level rewards. Experiments on multiple generation and editing benchmarks show that RubricRM outperforms existing specialized reward models and remains competitive with strong proprietary MLLM judges despite using smaller backbones. Our models, data, and code are available at https://github.com/zijiankan/RubricRM.

</details>


### [58] [Geo-LoRA: Geometry-Aware Subspace Evolution for Low-Rank Adaptation in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26960)
*Yibo Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Geo-LoRA是一个几何感知框架，通过显式调控低秩子空间（共享和任务特定）在Grassmann流形上的演化，解决无重放类增量学习中LoRA适配器面临的不稳定共享表征和任务特定子空间拥挤问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无重放类增量学习中的LoRA适配器存在根本性挑战：不同任务更新的低秩子空间缺乏几何控制，导致共享表示不稳定，且任务特定的更新会重复坍缩到先前被占据的方向上，损害模型的可塑性。

Method: Geo-LoRA包含三个核心约束组件：(1) 共享分支中的子空间投影保持(SPP)约束连续更新在Grassmann流形上遵循平滑轨迹；(2) 自适应核心-松弛对齐(ACSA)将转换分解为主成分和残差成分，对齐前者并调制后者以平衡稳定性和可塑性；(3) 任务特定分支中的中位数校准块重叠(MCBO)通过归一化投影重叠施加统计约束，惩罚过度重用以缓解子空间拥挤。

Result: Geo-LoRA在多个基准数据集和不同任务长度的设置下持续达到最先进性能，且无需引入超越标准LoRA的额外适配器类型。

Conclusion: Geo-LoRA为持续低秩自适应提供了原则性的几何形式化方法，通过联合调控所有层和任务中LoRA子空间的演化，有效解决了无重放CIL中稳定性和可塑性之间的平衡问题。

Abstract: Rehearsal-free class-incremental learning (CIL) with LoRA adapters remains challenging because the low-rank subspaces updated across tasks evolve without geometric control, causing unstable shared representations and repetitive collapse of task-specific updates into previously occupied directions. We introduce Geo-LoRA, a geometry-aware framework that explicitly regulates how low-rank subspaces, both shared and task-specific, evolve during continual learning. For the shared branch, Subspace Projection Preservation (SPP) constrains consecutive updates to follow smooth trajectories on the Grassmann manifold, and Adaptive Core-Slack Alignment (ACSA) decomposes transitions into principal and residual components, aligning the former while modulating the latter to balance stability and plasticity. For the task-specific branch, Median-Calibrated Block Overlap (MCBO) imposes a statistical constraint via normalized projection overlap, penalizing excessive reuse to mitigate subspace crowding. These constraints jointly regulate the evolution of all LoRA subspaces across layers and tasks without introducing additional adapter types beyond standard LoRA. Geo-LoRA provides a principled geometric formulation for continual low-rank adaptation and consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmark datasets and different task lengths.

</details>


### [59] [TempJail: Temporal Jailbreak Attacks against Image-to-Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26971)
*Qi Lu,Zehui Guo,David Yuanda Gan,Zijing Li,Hengda Zhang,Weijun Xu,Qiankun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TempJail，一种针对图像到视频（I2V）生成模型的时序越狱攻击框架，利用视频生成的时序维度漏洞，通过将恶意语义分解为初始帧视觉条件和时序文本指令来实现攻击，在闭源商业模型上比现有方法提升23.3%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有I2V安全研究主要关注单帧违规的越狱攻击，忽略了视频生成模型特有的时序维度。研究者发现I2V系统存在时序漏洞：不安全的语义可能不是来自单个帧，而是来自随时间推移的语义组合。这类攻击面临两个关键挑战：时序抽象（temporal abstraction）和语义伪装（semantic camouflage）。

Method: TempJail框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 时序抽象：将目标恶意描述分解为初始帧视觉条件和时序文本指令；(2) 语义伪装：图像侧通过扩散采样中的可控潜在扰动建模语义注入，并引入预训练编码器的梯度引导；文本侧将描述重写为无恶意的'主体-动作-场景'模板，绕过安全过滤器同时保留时序引导。在黑盒推理阶段，两种模态联合使恶意语义随时间逐渐触发。

Result: 在闭源商业模型（Kling、Seedance、Veo和PixVerse）上的实验表明，TempJail相比现有最先进方法在GPT-5.2评估下攻击成功率提升23.3%，在人类评估下提升22.0%。

Conclusion: I2V生成模型存在可利用的时序安全漏洞，TempJail有效证明了这一漏洞的可行性和危害性，凸显了视频生成模型安全防护需要考虑时序维度的重要性。

Abstract: In recent years, image-to-video (I2V) generation models have made remarkable progress in subject consistency and temporal coherence, enabling high quality video synthesis. However, these advances also introduce new safety risks. Existing studies mainly focus on jailbreak attacks involving single frame violations, while largely overlooking the temporal dimension unique to video generation models. In this paper, we investigate three attack scenarios and uncover a temporal vulnerability in I2V systems: unsafe semantics may emerge not from a single frame, but from semantic composition over time. We further identify two key challenges in such attacks: temporal abstraction and semantic camouflage. To address these issues, we propose TempJail, a novel temporal jailbreak framework for I2V systems. For temporal abstraction, we decompose a target malicious caption into an initial frame visual condition and a temporal text instruction. For semantic camouflage, on the image side we model semantic injection as controlled latent perturbation in diffusion sampling and introduce gradient guidance from pretrained encoders. On the text side, we rewrite the caption into an innocuous ``subject-action-scene'' template that bypasses safety filters while preserving temporal guidance. In the black-box inference phase, these two modalities jointly enable malicious semantics to be gradually triggered over time. Experiments on closed-source commercial models, including Kling, Seedance, Veo and PixVerse, show that TempJail improves attack success rate over prior state-of-the-art methods by 23.3\% under GPT-5.2 evaluation and 22.0\% under human evaluation. Our codes are available at \href{https://github.com/luqi-glory/TempJail}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [60] [Aphanta: Diagnosing Task-Aligned Image-Edited Intermediates for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26993)
*Hengyuan Xu,Wei Cheng,Yumeng Ji,Xuanyang Zhang,Xianfang Zeng,Gang Yu,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Aphanta框架，自动发现任务并闭环诊断MLLM→图像编辑器→MLLM流水线。通过对20个候选任务和多组编辑器-MLLM组合的评估，发现视觉中间表示的效用高度依赖任务类型：视觉线索注入、接地和反事实状态实现上收益显著，而符号敏感构建和结构外推类任务可靠性较低。在正向任务子集上，整合的Qwen流水线将平均任务分数从0.343提升至0.445（+10.2分，+29.7%相对提升），表明图像编辑应被视为专门的视觉工作区而非通用推理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式视觉中间表示可以帮助多模态大语言模型外化空间证据并更新视觉状态，但其效用取决于图像编辑器能否忠实实现所需的变换。现有研究缺乏系统性方法来区分"理论上视觉中间表示的潜在收益"与"当前编辑器实际实现的效用"，需要一种自动化的任务发现与闭环诊断框架来评估这一流水线。

Method: 提出Aphanta框架，包含自动任务发现和闭环诊断两个模块。评估三种条件以分离潜在视觉空间与实际编辑器效用：直接推理、使用编辑器生成中间表示进行推理、使用理想化参考中间表示进行推理。跨20个候选任务和多个编辑器-MLLM组合进行系统评估。

Result: 效用的发挥强烈依赖任务类型。视觉线索注入、接地和反事实状态实现任务上增益集中，而符号敏感构建或结构外推类任务显著不可靠。在选定的正向任务子集上，整合Qwen流水线将平均任务分数从0.343提升至0.445（+10.2分，+29.7%相对提升）。完整研究保留过滤与失败任务以揭示边界。

Conclusion: 图像编辑应被定位为专门的视觉工作区而非通用推理机制。Aphanta可作为衡量任务-表征对齐、编辑器实现能力以及下游流水线效用的可复用协议。

Abstract: Explicit visual intermediates can help multimodal large language models (MLLMs) externalize spatial evidence and updated visual states, but their utility depends on whether an image editor can faithfully realize the required transformation. We introduce \textbf{Aphanta}, an automated task-discovery and closed-loop diagnostic framework for the MLLM -> image editor -> MLLM pipeline. Aphanta evaluates three conditions---direct reasoning, reasoning with an editor-generated intermediate, and reasoning with an idealized reference intermediate---to separate potential visual headroom from the practical utility of current editors. Across 20 candidate tasks and multiple editor--MLLM combinations, we find that utility is strongly task-conditioned. Gains concentrate in visual cue injection, grounding, and counterfactual state realization, whereas intermediates requiring symbol-sensitive construction or structural extrapolation are substantially less reliable. On the selected positive-task subset, our consolidated Qwen pipeline improves the mean task score from 0.343 to 0.445 ($+10.2$ points; $+29.7\%$ relative), while the full study also retains filtered and unsuccessful tasks to expose the boundary. These results position image editing as a specialized visual workspace rather than a universal reasoning mechanism, and establish Aphanta as a reusable protocol for measuring task--representation alignment, editor realization, and downstream pipeline utility.

</details>


### [61] [Virtual iEEG from Scalp EEG: Charting the Landscape of Source Imaging, Intracranial Inference and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26998)
*Dongyi He,Xiangkai Wang,Hongjie Yan,Luping Song,Wai Ting Siok,Nizhuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于"虚拟颅内脑电（virtual iEEG）"的综述性论文，系统梳理了从头皮脑电推断颅内脑电的研究框架与方法。文章构建了一个以目标为中心的分类框架，区分事件推断、特征转换和波形重建三类任务，并强调应将可预测性与可观测性、可辨识性、保真度和实用性相分离。通过对现有证据的严格评估，作者认为当前研究仅能支持对特定颅内事件的推断、低频成分和任务相关表征的提取，尚无法实现任意接触级活动的唯一重建。文章还提出了加强验证所需的方法学要求，并展望了未来依赖独立配对数据集和前瞻性临床价值证据的发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅内脑电图（iEEG）虽然能够以高时间精度和空间特异性记录局灶及深部脑区的神经活动，但其侵入性和有限的大脑覆盖范围限制了常规临床使用。这一矛盾促使研究者探索从非侵入性头皮脑电推断颅内脑电的替代方案（即虚拟iEEG）。然而，现有研究在评估标准和验证方法上缺乏统一框架，导致结论不一、证据强度参差不齐。因此，作者旨在建立一个目标中心的系统性框架，以厘清不同推断任务的能力边界，并为该领域提供严格的评估准则和未来研究方向。

Method: 本文采用综述与批判性评估的方法。首先构建了一个以目标为中心的分析框架，将头皮到颅内的推断任务分为三类：事件推断（event inference）、特征转换（feature translation）和波形重建（waveform reconstruction）。同时，作者将可预测性（predictability）与可观测性（observability）、可辨识性（identifiability）、保真度（fidelity）和实用性（utility）四个概念加以区分。在证据评估上，作者依据队列独立性、解剖与频谱覆盖范围、训练-测试分离以及目标患者自适应等标准对现有文献进行了严格审查。

Result: 综述发现：现有研究能够支持对部分颅内事件、低频成分以及任务相关神经表征的合理推断，但无法实现对任意接触级脑电活动的唯一重建。作者指出，当前多数研究的验证强度不足，需要加强适当的对照组设计、引入脑电源成像（EEG source imaging）作为基线对比、进行不确定性评估以及开展增量效用检验。此外，研究还揭示了该领域在独立配对数据集和前瞻性临床验证方面的明显空白。

Conclusion: 虚拟iEEG作为一种潜在的非侵入性替代方案具有应用前景，但其能力目前仅限于特定条件下的有限推断任务。要推动该领域发展，需要建立包含独立配对数据集的共享资源，并在前瞻性临床研究中证明虚拟iEEG相比传统头皮EEG和脑电源成像具有额外的临床价值。作者强调，只有通过更严格的验证标准和统一的方法学框架，才能可靠地界定虚拟iEEG的实现边界并促进其临床应用转化。

Abstract: Intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) provides temporally precise and spatially specific access to neural activity from focal and deep brain regions, but its invasiveness and restricted anatomical coverage limit routine use. These constraints have motivated scalp-to-intracranial inference, termed virtual iEEG when model outputs carry iEEG-defined event, feature, representation, or contact-level waveform semantics. This review presents a target-centred framework distinguishing event inference, feature translation, and waveform reconstruction, while separating predictability from observability, identifiability, fidelity, and utility. Evidence is evaluated according to cohort independence, anatomical and spectral coverage, train--test separation, and target-patient adaptation. Current studies support inference of selected intracranial events, low-frequency components, and task-related representations, but not unique recovery of arbitrary contact-level activity. Stronger validation requires appropriate controls, source-imaging baselines, uncertainty assessment, and incremental-utility testing. Future progress depends on independent paired datasets and prospective evidence that virtual iEEG adds value beyond scalp EEG and EEG source imaging.

</details>


### [62] [MVC-Bench: Benchmarking Calibration of Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27004)
*Ashshak Sharifdeen,Shihab Aaqil Ahamed,Ufaq Khan,Muhammad Akhtar Munir Sujair Ibrahim,Mohamed Rafeek Mareer Ahamed,Yutong Xie,Imran Razzak,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVC-Bench是一个针对医学图像分类中视觉语言模型（VLMs）和医学视觉语言模型（Medical-VLMs）校准问题的评测基准，涵盖8种骨干网络、3种医学模态和领域偏移场景，通过1638+受控实验评估校准鲁棒性、校准策略有效性及稳定性，并提出Multi-Class Margin (MCM)正则化方法，在12个设置中的10个取得最低ECE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的医学视觉语言模型研究主要聚焦于提升准确率，而忽略了模型置信度校准这一在临床安全关键场景中至关重要的问题。可靠的校准是医学AI实际部署的前提，但该领域目前缺乏系统性的评估基准和方法研究。

Method: 构建MVC-Bench基准，从三个维度评估校准：(1)对模态、骨干网络和领域偏移的鲁棒性；(2)校准策略和提示微调方法的有效性；(3)对提示模板和随机种子变化的稳定性。覆盖8种骨干网络、3种医学模态（眼底成像、组织病理学、胸部X光），在领域内和领域偏移设置下，比较事后校准、训练时校准和零样本推理方法，以及6种提示微调方法。此外提出Multi-Class Margin (MCM)正则化作为简单的训练时校准方法。

Result: 在1638+受控实验中，使用准确率和期望校准误差（ECE）作为主要指标，并辅以最大校准误差（MCE）和自适应校准误差（ACE）。提出的MCM正则化方法在12个领域内设置中的10个取得最低ECE，并在领域偏移下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: MVC-Bench提供了一个结构化的评估框架和可操作指南，用于提升安全关键医学工作流中VLMs和Medical-VLMs的置信度校准。同时揭示了校准误差的根本原因，并为训练时校准方法提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of vision-language models (VLMs) and medical vision-language models (Medical-VLMs) requires calibrated confidence, particularly under realistic clinical conditions. However, existing efforts mainly focused on improving accuracy, leaving calibration in the medical domain underexplored. To this end, we propose MVC-Bench, a calibration-centric benchmark for medical image classification with VLMs and Medical-VLMs. MVC-Bench assesses the calibration across three axes: (i) robustness to modality, backbone, and domain shift (ii) effectiveness of calibration strategies and prompt-tuning methods (iii) stability under prompt-template and random-seed variations. The benchmark covers eight different backbones, three medical modalities, including fundus imaging, histopathology, and chest X-ray under in-domain and domain shift settings. It compares post-hoc calibration, train-time calibration, and zero-shot inference methods, together with six prompt-tuning methods. Across more than 1638 controlled experiments, we report accuracy and Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as primary metrics, and further report results with complementary calibration measures, including Maximum Calibration Error (MCE) and Adaptive Calibration Error (ACE). We further investigate the underlying causes of miscalibration in VLMs and Medical-VLMs and propose a simple train-time calibration method, Multi-Class Margin (MCM) regularization, which achieves lowest ECE on 10 out of 12 settings in in-domain and remains competitive under domain shifts. Collectively, MVC-Bench provides a structured evaluation framework and actionable guidance for improving calibration in safety-critical medical workflows.

</details>


### [63] [Differentiable Jitter Correction using Deep Learning-based Image Quality Metric for Phase-Contrast Micro-CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27034)
*Junan Chen,Yiting Jia,Joscha Maier,Dominik John,Sami Wirtensohn,Imke Greving,Silja Flenner,Matthias Wieczorek,Julia Herzen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全可微的抖动校正方法，用于X射线相衬显微CT，利用深度学习图像质量指标直接从投影数据中估计并补偿每个投影的刚性抖动，无需预扫描无运动参考。方法基于梯度自动对焦策略，引入紧凑3D卷积神经网络预测VIF分数，并结合仅作用于背景的空间选择性总变分惩罚，在多个同步辐射束线的生物标本上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线相衬显微CT在扫描过程中常因样本或系统的刚性抖动引入投影间错位，导致重建图像出现伪影并损失精细结构。现有抖动校正方法通常需要预扫描无运动参考或依赖手工设计的图像质量指标，缺乏通用性和可微性，难以在多种成像条件下稳定工作。

Method: 1) 建立基于梯度自动对焦策略的完全可微抖动校正框架，适配平行束几何；2) 在受控研究中基准测试多种候选目标函数，验证VIF指标对抖动伪影的敏感性；3) 训练紧凑3D卷积神经网络以从单个损坏体数据预测VIF分数，免除干净参考需求；4) 引入仅作用于图像背景的空间选择性总变分惩罚，抑制优化过程中产生的虚假高频结构。

Result: 在模拟及实验获取的投影数据上，所提方法能可靠恢复因抖动丢失的精细结构细节，并在形态不同的样本上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 集成管道有效解决相衬显微CT的抖动伪影问题，无需干净参考即可实现跨样本的鲁棒校正，为生物样本的高分辨率成像提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fully differentiable jitter correction method for X-ray phase-contrast micro computed tomography using a deep learning-based image quality metric that estimates and compensates per-projection rigid jitter directly from the acquired projection data, without a pre-scan motion-free reference. The approach builds on a gradient-based auto-focus strategy adapted to parallel-beam geometry. A set of candidate objective functions is benchmarked in a controlled study, and the sensitivity of the visual information fidelity (VIF) metric to the jitter artifact is verified with the target phase-contrast data. To operate without a clean reference, a compact 3D convolutional neural network is trained to predict the VIF score from a single corrupted volume. A spatially selective total variation penalty applied exclusively to the image background is introduced to penalize spurious high-frequency structures that otherwise emerge during optimization. Experiments on biological specimens acquired at different synchrotron beamlines are conducted. Evaluation uses jitter motion applied to simulated and experimentally acquired projection data. The result confirms that the integrated pipeline reliably recovers fine structural detail lost due to jitter, with generalization demonstrated across morphologically distinct samples.

</details>


### [64] [Multi-Person Human Motion Forecasting in Complex Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27039)
*Serdar Ozsoy,Lars Doorenbos,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OCSD（Object-Conditioned Social Diffusion），一个将运动历史、多人交互和物体线索统一到单个条件扩散模型中的框架，用于复杂场景中的人群移动预测，在HiK和HOI-M3基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测复杂场景中的人群移动需要推理整个环境的历史和当前状态，但将物体信息和社交交互有效整合到一个统一框架中仍具挑战性。

Method: OCSD是一个条件扩散模型，包含两个关键组件：(1) 物体条件机制，在每个时间步调制去噪过程，实现细粒度的行人-物体推理；(2) 社交编码器，建模场景中所有人之间的交互。该模型能自然处理不同群体规模、复杂社交交互，并支持采样多个合理的未来轨迹。

Result: 在Humans in Kitchens (HiK)和HOI-M3基准上达到SOTA。与先前工作相比，两秒路径误差在HiK上降低121.5mm（31.3%），在HOI-M3上降低130.5mm（33.2%），并能产生更真实的长期预测。

Conclusion: OCSD通过统一的扩散框架有效地融合了物体信息与社交交互，显著提升了复杂场景下的人群移动预测精度，特别是在有物体干扰和多人交互的场景中表现出色。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting the movement of people in complex scenes requires reasoning over the past and present state of the entire environment. In this context, effectively incorporating object information and social interactions into a unified framework remains particularly challenging. To address this, we propose Object-Conditioned Social Diffusion (OCSD), a conditional diffusion model that integrates motion history, multi-person interactions, and object cues into a single framework. OCSD uses an object-conditioning mechanism that modulates denoising at every timestep, enabling fine-grained human-object reasoning, and a social encoder that models the interactions between all humans in the scene. As a result, our model naturally handles varying group sizes, complex social interactions, and supports sampling multiple plausible futures. Extensive experiments show that OCSD achieves state-of-the-art results on the Humans in Kitchens (HiK) and HOI-M3 benchmarks. It reduces the two-second path error by 121.5 mm (31.3%) on HiK and 130.5 mm (33.2%) on HOI-M3 compared to prior work, and produces more realistic long-term forecasts.

</details>


### [65] [Anatomy-Guided Foundation Model Adaptation with Within-Case Prototype Supervision for Standard Plane Detection in Fetal Ultrasound Blind Sweeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27051)
*Yuzhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AnatoProto，一个轻量级序列级框架，用于在低成本产科盲扫超声中检测胎儿腹围标准切面（正帧占比<3%的极端类别不平衡问题）。该框架通过解剖加权空间池化、病例内原型损失、三级级联精炼和混合预测头四部分，在ACOUSLIC-AI基准上达到F1=67.72，超越最强基础模型+13.20 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿腹围标准切面检测在低成本产科盲扫序列中是一个高度不平衡的帧分类问题：正帧仅占序列不到3%，形成短连续片段，且现成的超声与视觉基础模型处理效果差。现有方法无法有效利用序列级结构与解剖先验信息，亟需专门设计。

Method: AnatoProto包含四个核心组件：(i)解剖加权空间池化——利用nnU-Net腹部区域概率作为空间先验，对冻结的BiomedCLIP补丁token进行重加权聚合；(ii)病例内原型损失——将每帧嵌入拉向同一扫描中正帧均值，利用帧级不可用的病例级结构；(iii)三级级联精炼（帧→片段→病例级拒绝器），将预测单元从噪声帧提升到结构约束的片段；(iv)混合预测头——联合建模帧内稳定性和帧间边界转换以抑制边界假阳性。

Result: 在ACOUSLIC-AI基准上，AnatoProto测试F1达到67.72，超越最强基础模型基线（FetalCLIP+PRS，F1=54.52）+13.20 F1，以及最强视频时序动作检测基线（TriDet+PRS）+15.76 F1。协同分析显示原型损失与解剖加权池化并非简单叠加：单独使用原型损失使召回降低12点，但与解剖加权池化结合后召回提升6.5点，出现符号翻转。

Conclusion: AnatoProto通过将解剖先验与序列级结构信息相结合，有效解决了困难的正帧稀疏检测问题。协同效应分析揭示组件间的非加性交互，说明在高度不平衡的医学视频中，解剖感知的嵌入聚合是释放原型损失潜力的关键前提。

Abstract: Detecting the fetal abdominal circumference standard plane in low-cost obstetric blind sweeps is a highly imbalanced frame-classification problem: positive frames account for under 3% of a sequence, form short contiguous segments, and are poorly handled by off-the-shelf ultrasound and vision foundation models. We propose AnatoProto, a lightweight sequence-level framework that adapts a frozen BiomedCLIP encoder to fetal blind sweeps through four components: (i) anatomy-weighted spatial pooling that uses nnU-Net abdominal-region probabilities as a spatial prior to reweight BiomedCLIP patch tokens, so frozen semantic features are aggregated onto anatomically meaningful regions; (ii) a within-case prototype loss that pulls each frame embedding toward the mean of positive frames of the same sweep, exploiting case-level structure unavailable at the frame level; (iii) a three-stage cascade refinement (frame->segment->case-level rejecter) that lifts the prediction unit from noisy frames to structurally-constrained segments; and (iv) a hybrid prediction head that jointly models per-frame stability and inter-frame boundary transitions to suppress boundary false positives. On the ACOUSLIC-AI benchmark, AnatoProto reaches a test F1 of 67.72, outperforming the strongest foundation-model baseline (FetalCLIP + PRS, F1 = 54.52) by +13.20 F1 and the strongest video temporal-action-detection baseline (TriDet + PRS) by +15.76 F1. A synergy study, backed by embedding geometry and paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, shows that the prototype loss and anatomy-weighted pooling are not additive: applied alone the prototype loss reduces recall by 12 points, but combined with anatomy-weighted pooling it increases recall by 6.5 points -- a sign-flip we trace to the accuracy of the within-case prototype.

</details>


### [66] [Video-OPSD: Exploiting Privileged Visual Evidence for On-Policy Self-Distillation in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27065)
*Ziyue Wang,Shiqi Huang,Weiwen Xu,Bihan Wen,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video-OPSD是一种针对视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）的在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）后训练框架，利用长视频中固有的时间冗余性，将注释证据帧作为特权监督条件来构建教师模型，并通过证据引导的标记级优化来加权蒸馏损失，从而在多个视频理解基准上超越标准OPSD，并达到与GRPO相当的性能同时大幅减少训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频推理提供了主输入内部的特权监督来源：长视频包含大量时间冗余，仅少数帧包含回答问题所需的证据。现有OPSD方法通常通过向教师上下文添加额外信息来构建特权教师，而未充分利用视频主输入内部的这种特权证据。

Method: Video-OPSD包含两个核心组件：1）Evidence-Grounded Self-Teacher：教师仅基于注释证据帧进行条件处理，而学生基于完整视频推理，使教师能提供更具信息量的监督信号；2）Evidence-Guided Token Optimization：根据每个推理token对特权视觉证据的依赖程度自适应加权标记级蒸馏损失，以强调感知驱动的推理。

Result: 在视频理解和推理基准上，Video-OPSD在多个骨干网络上持续优于标准OPSD，达到与GRPO相当的性能，同时显著减少训练时间，是一种高效且有效的Video-LLMs后训练方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用视频主输入内部的特权视觉证据构建更好的教师并进行证据引导的蒸馏，Video-OPSD为视频大语言模型提供了一种高效且有效的后训练范式，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm that improves policy optimization through dense token-level supervision from a privileged self-teacher. Despite its promise, OPSD remains largely underexplored for Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs). Existing methods typically construct privileged teachers by augmenting their context with additional information while keeping the primary input unchanged for both teacher and student. Video reasoning, however, offers a distinct source of privileged supervision within the primary input itself: long videos contain substantial temporal redundancy, and only a small subset of frames provides the evidence necessary to answer a question.
