Conclusion: 这套开源预训练配方大幅降低了预训练的成本门槛，使学术和开源社区能够在消费级硬件上训练有竞争力的模型。完整的配方（数据、代码、模型权重）已以Apache 2.0协议公开，为后续低成本预训练研究提供了可复现的基础，并首次通过可控实验展示了完整预训练管线的研究价值。

Abstract: Language model pretraining has become almost synonymous with prohibitive cost, placing it out of reach for much of the academic and open-source communities. Although strong open-source efforts already exist, including open-weight models and open-source training recipes, a cost-efficient, hardware-accessible, and open-source pretraining recipe has long been missing. Even at a small scale, training Llama-3.2-3B costs over \$1.5M, and reproducing SmolLM3-3B needs over \$700K. In this report, we present an open pretraining recipe designed to lower this barrier. Using this recipe, we train a collection of Puro-2B models from scratch on up to 1.4 trillion tokens with FP8 precision on consumer-grade RTX 5090 GPUs. The models in the collection differ in token budgets and selected recipe variants. Our best model is trained at a compute cost of less than \$6.9K and approaches Qwen2.5-1.5B performance under our evaluation protocol. This cost efficiency is enabled by a combination of approaches, including hardware selection, low-precision training, hyperball optimization, curriculum model averaging, and the data recipe. Beyond the recipe itself, we provide two additional results. First, across the Puro-2B collection, we derive a Puro Cost Scaling Law that relates training cost to average model performance; the fitted law suggests that about \$4.4K, less than \$5,090, is sufficient to reach the performance of Qwen2-1.5B. Second, as an end-to-end case study, we examine how pretraining data curricula shape downstream performance after post-training. Such controlled studies are enabled by having access to the full pretraining pipeline rather than model weights alone. We release the full training recipe for Puro-2B, including data, code, and model weights under Apache 2.0 at https://huggingface.co/collections/thu-pacman/puro-2b.

</details>


### [203] [D2C-Routing: Dimension-to-Composition Evidence Routing for Mixed-Origin AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27380)
*Xin Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Yiqi Tong,Wei Guo,Yutian Xiao,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出D2C-Routing框架，将混合来源文本检测重新定义为从维度到组合的来源归因问题，通过分离内容来源和表达来源并组合成四种协作类型来检测，在MixD2C基准上达到0.8603四类Avg TPR@1%FPR，比同分区的RACE-local重跑高6.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的AI生成文本检测被框定为二元的文档级判断（人写或机器生成），但在混合来源写作场景下，内容来源与表达来源可能不同，这种二元框架会失效。因此需要一种能识别混合来源（内容与表达可能来自不同来源）的检测方法。

Method: 提出Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing)方法：将混合来源检测建模为维度到组合的来源归因，先独立推断内容来源和表达来源两个维度，再通过一个有监督的门控组合层预测最终的四种协作类型标签。内容侧和表达侧的证据分别路由到独立的监督维度头。

Result: 在从HART混合来源基准重建的MixD2C数据集上，D2C-Routing检测系统达到0.8603的四类Avg TPR@1%FPR，比同分区RACE-local重跑高6.5个百分点。核心消融实验支持路由设计；误差分析显示AI内容/人类表达与完全AI生成文本之间的区分仍是最难的边界。

Conclusion: 维度到组合的路由框架优于原有方法，验证了将混合来源检测拆分为内容/表达双维度归因再合成的有效性，同时指出AI内容搭配人类表达与完全AI生成文本的区分仍是未来最具挑战的方向。

Abstract: AI-generated text detection is commonly framed as a binary document-level judgment about whether a text is human-written or machine-generated. This framing breaks down for mixed-origin writing, where content origin and expression origin may differ. We cast mixed-origin detection as dimension-to-composition source attribution, inferring content origin and expression origin before composing them into four collaboration types. We propose Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing), which routes content-side and expression-side evidence to supervised dimension heads before a learned gated composition layer predicts the final label. On MixD2C, a reconstructed split derived from the HART mixed-origin benchmark, our disclosed D2C-Routing-based detector system reaches 0.8603 four-way Avg TPR@1%FPR, 6.5 points above the same-split RACE-local rerun. Core ablations support the routing design, while error analysis shows that distinguishing AI-content/human-expression from fully AI-generated text remains the hardest boundary. Code is available at https://github.com/bystander563/d2c-routing-artifact.

</details>


### [204] [RATIO: A Benchmark for Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations in Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27394)
*Maayan Sharon,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RATIO（Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations），一个面向科学灵感检索的大规模基准数据集。RATIO基于三种"构思操作"（ideation moves）定义相关性：Address（检索解决特定问题的潜在方法）、Broaden（检索更泛化的表述）、Specify（检索具体实例化）。该基准通过扩展话语标记远程监督方法，从百万级CS全文学术论文中构建，并经过LLM与人工双重筛选。实验表明，面向特定操作微调的检索器显著提升性能，但仍存在较大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究中，检索到的文献可以为人或AI科学家提供灵感，但现有检索基准仅关注"直接相关"（即Address），忽略了灵感可以来自不同抽象层级（更泛化的Broaden或更具体的Specify）。作者旨在建立一种能够捕捉多粒度科学启发的检索基准，弥补现有检索系统无法支持文献引导的构思生成这一缺口。

Method: 作者提出RATIO基准：(1)定义三种ideation moves（Address/Broaden/Specify）作为相关性判定的操作类型；(2)将原本仅用于分类的话语标记远程监督（discourse-marker distant supervision）扩展到语料库级检索，从数百万篇CS全文学术论文中构建数据集；(3)结合大规模LLM筛选与人工审核来保证质量；(4)基于该基准对检索器进行operation-specific微调并评估其性能。

Result: 实验表明，针对特定ideation move进行微调的检索器相较于通用检索器有显著性能提升，但整体表现仍有较大提升空间，说明RATIO基准具备足够的挑战性和区分度，能够有效衡量检索系统在科学灵感检索方面的能力。

Conclusion: RATIO为文献引导的构思生成提供了可扩展的训练与评估框架，开启了科学灵感检索这一新的研究方向。该基准不仅支持多种抽象层级的灵感检索，也为未来开发更强大的检索组件和构思辅助系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Retrieved scientific literature can serve as inspiration for both human and AI scientists. Inspiration can take different forms: prior work may directly suggest how to address a problem, or surface directions at different levels of abstraction - zooming out to a more general view or zooming in to a concrete realization. We introduce RATIO (Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations), a large-scale benchmark in which relevance is defined by three operations which we name ideation moves: Address retrieves potential approaches for stated problems, Broaden retrieves more general formulations, and Specify retrieves concrete instantiations. RATIO is constructed from millions of full-text scientific papers across CS literature via a general recipe that extends discourse-marker distant supervision - previously used only for classification - to corpus-scale retrieval, combined with extensive LLM and human vetting. Experiments show that operation-specific fine-tuning substantially boosts retrievers but leaves much room for further improvements. RATIO provides a scalable training and evaluation framework for retrieval components that support literature-grounded ideation, opening up new research avenues on scientific inspiration retrieval.

</details>


### [205] [Making Clinical Language Models Auditable: Concept-Guided Fine-Tuning for Robust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27397)
*Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAST框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）暴露Transformer中间层的稀疏、人类可审计特征，通过LLM辅助解释和ICD-10检索约束标记SAE潜在变量，在微调期间通过残差减法抑制已验证的伪影（artifact）潜在变量，并提供事后逐概念归因用于审计模型决策。在MIMIC-IV出院记录死亡率预测任务上，CAST优于对应微调编码器基线，与强LLM基线相当，同时提供支持每个预测的临床概念及训练中抑制的伪影概念的逐特征审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型在院内数据上准确率高，但在部署偏移下失败，因为它们利用了笔记特有的伪影（如模板、分隔符、样板文本）而非真实反映患者状态的临床内容。亟需一种可审计的临床文本分类框架，既能抑制伪影又保持临床概念的可解释性。

Method: CAST包含四个组件：（1）使用SAE从Transformer中间激活中提取稀疏、人类可审计的特征；（2）通过LLM辅助解释管线配合ICD-10检索约束对SAE潜在变量进行标注；（3）在微调期间通过残差减法抑制验证为伪影的潜在变量；（4）提供事后逐概念归因支持审计模型决策。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV出院记录死亡率预测任务上，CAST优于对应的微调编码器基线，并保持与强LLM基线的竞争力，同时生成特征级审计轨迹，展示支撑每次预测的临床概念及训练期间被抑制的伪影概念。

Conclusion: CAST证明了通过SAE揭示和抑制伪影特征能够提升临床文本模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，同时保持模型决策的可审计性，为临床AI的安全部署提供了一种兼具性能与透明度的解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical language models can achieve strong in-hospital accuracy yet fail under deployment shifts because they exploit note-specific artifacts (e.g., templates, separators, boilerplate) that do not reflect patient state. We propose CAST (Concept-guided Artifact Suppression Tuning), an SAE-based framework for auditable clinical text classification. CAST uses Sparse Autoencoders to expose sparse, human-auditable features from intermediate Transformer activations, labels SAE latents with an LLM-assisted interpretation pipeline and ICD-10 retrieval constraints, suppresses verified artifact latents via residual subtraction during fine-tuning, and provides post-hoc per-concept attributions for auditing model decisions. On MIMIC-IV discharge-note mortality prediction, CAST improves over its corresponding fine-tuned encoder baselines and remains competitive with strong LLM baselines, while producing a feature-level audit trail of the clinical concepts that support each prediction and the artifact concepts suppressed during training.

</details>


### [206] [How Language Models Organize and Structure Moral Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27402)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究大型语言模型（LLMs）如何组织道德知识，通过训练六个线性探针（对应MFT的六个道德基础类别），发现这些道德方向的表征既不坍缩为单一检测器，也不彼此孤立，而是跨越近最大维度并共享正公共成分——这是道德整合的几何特征。该几何结构在架构和规模上一致，且在预训练早期即达到整合状态。模型中未发现MFT理论预测的个体化/约束性区分，结构反映的是语料统计。在道德困境中，困境方向部分由其基础成分组成（2.7倍于错配基线），大部分方差编码冲突特有结构，表明模型表征的是道德张力本身而非预判结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM能检测道德内容，但检测是低标准。作者追问LLM是否更进一步：能否区分不同道德基础并几何地组织它们之间的关系，从而理解LLM内部道德知识的结构化组织方式。

Method: 在开放权重语言模型上为MFT的六个类别各训练一个独立线性探针，检查所得方向在表征空间中的相互关系（方向间余弦相似度、维度跨度）。与匹配的非道德概念探针进行比较，并跨架构、规模与预训练阶段分析几何结构，最后扩展到道德困境方向的组合性分析。

Result: 六个道德方向跨越近最大数量的独立维度，同时共享正公共成分（平均成对余弦0.26，远高于非道德对照的0.013）。该几何跨架构和规模一致，在预训练早期即达到整合状态（早于探针精度饱和）。未发现MFT预测的个体化/约束性二分（检验功效有限，仅20种候选划分）。道德困境方向的部分由基础成分组成（2.7倍于错配基线），但大部分方差编码冲突特有结构。

Conclusion: LLM的道德知识既非单一道德检测器也非相互孤立的分立模块，而是呈现"整合"几何：六个道德基础方向共享公共成分但保持独立维度。该结构源于语料统计而非理论驱动的概念区分，且模型表征的是道德冲突本身的张力结构，而非预先解析好的道德判断。

Abstract: How do large language models (LLMs) organize moral knowledge? Models detect moral content broadly, but detection is a low bar. We ask whether they go further, distinguishing moral foundations from one another and organizing the relationships between them geometrically.
  We train six independent linear probes on open-weight language models, one per Moral Foundations Theory (MFT) category (care/harm, fair/cheat, lib/oppress, loy/betray, auth/subv, sanc/degrade), and examine how the resulting directions relate to each other in representation space. We find the directions neither collapse into a single moral detector nor isolate from one another. Rather, they span a near-maximal number of independent dimensions while sharing a positive common component. The shared component is the signature of integration, and it is moral-specific relative to a matched non-moral concept battery built identically (mean pairwise cosine 0.26 vs. 0.013).
  The geometry is consistent across architectures and scale and reaches its integration regime early in pre-training, well before probe accuracy saturates. The structure the model discovers shows no evidence of the individualizing/binding distinction predicted by Moral Foundations Theory (an underpowered test: only 20 candidate partitions exist) but rather reflects corpus statistics. Extending to moral dilemmas, each dilemma direction partially composes from its component foundations, at 2.7x a mismatched-pair baseline, while the majority of its variance encodes conflict-specific structure. The model represents moral tension itself, not a pre-resolved judgment.

</details>


### [207] [Consolidating RLVR Capabilities Across Domains: A Deep Dive into Fusion Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27409)
*Siye Wu,Kai Yang,Yuchen Cai,Xin Xu,Peng-Yuan Wang,Jiaxuan Wang,Jiashun Liu,Jiafei Lyu,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种大语言模型多能力融合范式——Merge（合并专家任务向量）、Mix RL（专家数据集池化训练）、以及多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD，同时利用专家向量与数据），在共享专家和数据的条件下，跨模型规模和多个基准域进行了公平对比。结果表明三者平均性能差异不超过1.4分，但单基准上最大差距达8.6分，领域级差异与任务向量几何中的跨域关系相关。论文分析了各范式的训练动力学约束，并给出了实用的选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能提升LLM的特定能力，但覆盖多能力通常需要分别训练领域专家再融合。由于三种融合范式（Merge、Mix RL、MOPD）大多在孤立场景下被研究，它们在统一条件下如何比较、以及如何选择缺乏系统性认识。

Method: 论文组织了三种融合范式：Merge（将专家任务向量相加合并）、Mix RL（将各领域数据集混合池化后统一训练）、MOPD（多教师在线蒸馏，同时复用专家和数据集）。作者使用共享的专家与数据，跨多个模型规模和多领域基准套件进行公平对比实验，并结合任务向量几何分析跨域关系，考察训练动力学及各范式对样本准确率、解决方案覆盖率和保留能力的影响。

Result: 三种范式的平均性能差异最多仅1.4分，但在单一基准上差距可达8.6分；领域级表现变化与任务向量几何中的跨域关系一致。训练动力学方面：Mix RL依赖领域混合比例，MOPD受限于其教师性能上限，Merge将所有专家更新压缩为单一向量。三种方法均能提升单样本准确率，但没有带来可测量的解决方案覆盖率提升，也未造成保留能力损失。

Conclusion: 论文提出实用指南：若专家已存在且追求低成本融合，选Merge；若无需专家来训练统一模型，选Mix RL，并根据跨域迁移调整领域比例；若更看重保留领域特定增益而非超越教师或最小化端到端成本，选MOPD。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves specific capabilities of large language models, but covering multiple capabilities often involves training separate domain experts and subsequently consolidating them. We organize three fusion paradigms by the artefacts they reuse: Merge combines expert task vectors, Mix RL pools their datasets, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) uses both. Because they have largely been studied in isolation, how they compare and how to choose among them remain unclear. We compare all three using shared experts and data across model scales and a multi-domain benchmark suite. Although their average performance differs by at most 1.4 points, the gap reaches 8.6 points on a single benchmark, with domain-level variation tracking cross-domain relations visible in task-vector geometry. Training dynamics expose distinct constraints: Mix RL depends on domain mixture proportions, MOPD remains bounded by its teachers, and Merge compresses all expert updates into one. All three improve single-sample accuracy without measurable gains in solution coverage or losses in held-out capabilities. These results yield a practical guideline: use Merge when experts already exist and cheap fusion is paramount; Mix RL when training a unified model without experts, with domain proportions adjusted for cross-domain transfer; and MOPD when preserving domain-specific gains matters more than surpassing teachers or minimizing end-to-end cost.

</details>


### [208] [Boosting LLM Exploration via Weak-Model Guidance in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27420)
*Xingyu Shen,Huishuai Zhang,Peng Li,Yinchun Wang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单有效的RLVR多样性保持方法：通过用更小更弱的语言模型生成部分推理轨迹作为前缀，强制目标模型基于这些"不熟悉"的前缀继续生成答案，从而打破过度自信、鼓励不同推理路径的探索，缓解RLVR训练中的熵塌缩问题，并在多个数学基准上提升pass@k表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能显著提升LLM推理能力，但常导致策略熵下降，使推理覆盖范围收窄、大k值下的pass@k退化。现有方法多通过算法正则化缓解熵塌缩，但忽略了跨模型非参数扰动的潜力，作者希望探索一种不依赖内部探索的简单方法。

Method: 提出"外部前缀"方法：在RLVR训练中，使用一个更小更弱的语言模型生成部分推理轨迹作为前缀，强制目标模型基于这些不熟悉的、分布外的前缀继续完成答案生成。这些前缀干扰目标模型的过度自信，促使其探索不同的推理路径。作者还实证研究了外部前缀的机制，分析了分布差异对RLVR训练中探索动力学的影响。

Result: 在多个数学基准上，该方法一致优于vanilla RLVR。性能提升随着k增大而愈发显著，表明推理覆盖范围得到了实质性扩展。同时该方法无需额外的SFT、复杂的奖励设计或复杂的prompt工程，有效缓解了熵塌缩。

Conclusion: 跨模型非参数扰动是一种简单而有效的RLVR多样性保持手段。通过借助更弱模型的推理前缀作为"外部扰动"，可以在不增加复杂机制的前提下显著扩展目标模型的推理覆盖范围并缓解熵塌缩，且收益随k增大而放大。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) significantly improves LLM reasoning but often causes a drop in policy entropy, leading to narrowed reasoning coverage and degraded pass@$k$ for large $k$. While existing methods mitigate this entropy collapse through algorithmic regularizations, cross-model non-parametric perturbation is also neglected. In this work, we propose a simple yet effective approach to preserve the generative diversity of LLMs during RLVR. Instead of relying solely on internal exploration, we force the target model to generate answers based on partial reasoning trajectories generated by a smaller, weaker language models. These unfamiliar prefixes effectively disrupt over-confidence and encourage the exploration of distinct reasoning paths. We empirically study the potential of outer prefixes, revealing the mechanism of the impact of distributional discrepancy to the exploration dynamics in RLVR training. Experiments across multiple mathematical benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms vanilla RLVR. Notably, the performance gain becomes increasingly pronounced as $k$ scales up, demonstrating a substantial expansion of reasoning coverage. Furthermore, our approach efficiently mitigates entropy collapse without requiring additional SFT, intricate reward designs, or complex prompting.

</details>


### [209] [Stochastic Estimation of Transduced Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27428)
*Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Samuel Kiegeland,Manuel de Prada Corral,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无放回重采样的束求和算法，为转换语言模型（TLM）的目标前缀概率估计提供无偏估计器，并估计阈值剪枝造成的概率质量损失。该方法在文本和DNA任务上优于顺序蒙特卡洛基线，在DNA转氨基酸任务上将运行时间减少数个数量级，且能保证算法以概率1终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算TLM中目标前缀概率需要累加所有映射到该前缀的源字符串的源模型概率，该集合可能指数级甚至无限大。先前工作采用阈值剪枝的束求和来计算下界，但存在未知误差，无法量化近似精度。因此需要一种既能高效估计概率又能提供无偏性与误差估计的方法。

Method: 采用无放回重采样的束求和算法：从源前缀集合中无放回抽取样本，并以入选概率的倒数对每个选中前缀进行加权，递归应用该修正得到目标前缀概率的无偏估计量。算法在保留的源前缀上扩展束，同时采样决定保留哪些前缀，随着累计概率质量增加而逐步减少保留前缀数量，从而节省计算量并保证以概率1终止。同时该方法可用于估计阈值剪枝所损失的概率质量。

Result: 在百科文本与DNA数据上，与使用有放回重采样的顺序蒙特卡洛基线相比，该方法在文本上取得更好的计算-方差权衡，在DNA上以相同的最大粒子数获得更低误差。在DNA到氨基酸的转导任务上，相对阈值剪枝束求和将运行时间降低数个数量级，使长目标字符串的前缀概率估计变得可行。将其应用于已发表的阅读时间分析中，显著降低语料库惊奇度估计，但不改变原有结论。

Conclusion: 无放回重采样的束求和是一种优于现有方法的高效、无偏的TLM目标前缀概率估计方案，兼具理论保证（无偏性、概率1终止）与实证优势（更低方差/误差、更快速度），且不会改变下游分析结论，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Transduced language models (TLMs) compose a pretrained \emph{source} language model with a functional finite-state transducer to induce a language model over \emph{target} strings. Computing the probability of a target prefix under a TLM amounts to summing the source-model probabilities of all source strings that the transducer maps to target strings beginning with that prefix. This set can be exponentially large or infinite. Prior work uses a computational shortcut based on source prefix probabilities, then approximates the resulting sum with threshold-pruned beam summing. This produces a lower bound with unknown error. Instead, we resample source prefixes without replacement and reweight each selected prefix by the inverse of its inclusion probability. We show that applying this correction recursively gives an unbiased estimator of the target prefix probability and lets us estimate the mass lost by threshold pruning. Our beam-summing algorithm extends the retained source prefixes and samples which prefixes to keep, reducing their number as more probability mass is added to the running estimate. This can save computation and guarantees that the run halts with probability one. We evaluate the method on encyclopedic text and DNA against sequential Monte Carlo baselines that resample with replacement. It achieves a better compute--variance tradeoff on text and lower error at the same maximum number of particles on DNA. On a DNA-to-amino-acid transduction, it reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude relative to threshold-pruned beam summing and makes estimating prefix probabilities for long target strings feasible. Replacing threshold pruning with unbiased sampling in a published reading-time analysis substantially lowers the estimated corpus surprisal but leaves the published conclusions unchanged.

</details>


### [210] [TTPO: Test-Time Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27448)
*Aozhe Wang,Zhengxi Lu,Jianze Wang,Shangke Lv,Ying Liu,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Hua Yang,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出测试时策略优化（TTPO），一种无需标签的测试时训练方法。观察到与伪标签不一致的rollout通常都是错误的（这种失败模式不对称），因此用一个非对称目标：用OPSD蒸馏一致的rollout，用分组RL惩罚不一致的rollout，并通过token级选择进一步细化两个分支。TTPO在五项竞赛级基准上达到与标签监督OPSD相当的性能，无需标签即可将Qwen3-1.7B从38.0%提升至45.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练方法（如RL和OPSD）严重依赖真值标签，这排除了测试时训练（TTT）的可能性。用多数投票伪标签替代真值标签虽然自然，但非常脆弱：一旦投票错误，就会误导教师并污染所有token。论文观察到这种失败模式具有不对称性——与伪标签不一致的rollout通常无论投票是否正确都是错误的，基于此设计更稳健的无标签TTT方法。

Method: 提出TTPO：一个非对称目标函数，对与伪标签一致的rollout用OPSD进行蒸馏，对不一致的rollout用分组RL进行惩罚。此外引入token级选择机制：蒸馏分支对已收敛位置降权，RL分支仅惩罚高置信度的错误。多数投票路由机制使得随着模型提升，自监督信号更加紧密。两个分支更新在频繁伪标签错误下仍然有良好的理论保障。

Result: TTPO在零标签条件下匹配了五项竞赛级基准上标签监督的OPSD性能；将Qwen3-1.7B从38.0%提升至45.2%；无思考模式下提升+25.2%至+36.4%；并展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过利用伪标签失败模式的不对称性，TTPO成功地在无需任何标签的情况下实现了与有监督方法相当的测试时训练性能，表明自监督的TTT在实际竞争级数学推理任务中是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Recent prominent post-training methods, such as Reinforcement Learning (RL) and On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), have driven rapid progress in mathematical reasoning for large language models, yet their reliance on ground-truth labels precludes test-time training (TTT). Replacing ground truth with majority-vote pseudo-labels is a natural alternative, yet it is fragile: an incorrect vote corrupts the teacher and misleads every token. We observe that this failure mode is asymmetric: rollouts that disagree with the pseudo-label are typically wrong regardless of whether the vote itself is correct. Building on this observation, we propose Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO), an asymmetric objective that distills agreeing rollouts via OPSD and penalizes disagreeing rollouts with Grouped RL. Token-level selection further refines both branches: distillation down-weights already-converged positions, while RL penalizes only confident errors. Both updates remain well-grounded even under frequent pseudo-label errors, and majority-vote routing yields tighter self-supervision as the model improves. Without any labels, TTPO matches label-supervised OPSD on five competition-level benchmarks, raises Qwen3-1.7B from 38.0% to 45.2% in TTT, yields +25.2% to +36.4% without thinking, and shows strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [211] [CritICL: Inference-Time Weak-to-Strong Generalization from Small Language Model Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27455)
*Yufan Wu,Yinghui He,Zhengyi Hu,Lang Wei,Ruichen Li,Qifan Yang,Ting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CritICL，一种高效的推理时框架，利用同族模型中较弱模型的结构化失败模式作为批判性上下文示例来改进LLM推理，无需重复生成或外部验证，在显著降低token成本的同时达到与测试时扩展方法相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法虽然能显著提升LLM的推理性能，但通常依赖重复生成或外部验证，计算开销大、token成本高。作者观察到同族LLM的失败模式在模型规模间呈现结构化规律，提出可将这些"失败"转化为有用的指导信号，而非简单丢弃，从而实现高效且免外部验证的推理增强。

Method: CritICL包含两个变体：(1) CritICL-dynamic：自适应地预测输入特有的失败模式并检索相应的批判性上下文示例；(2) CritICL-static：使用全局失败模式画像提供稳定的指导。两者均通过将较弱模型导出的失败模式以批评式上下文示例（critique-based in-context examples）的形式注入推理过程。

Result: 实验表明CritICL在标准上下文学习中表现一致更好，并在性能和测试时扩展方法相当或更优的同时，所需的生成次数和token成本显著更低。

Conclusion: CritICL验证了"失败作为指导"这一新范式的有效性，表明利用较弱模型的结构化失败模式可以在不牺牲推理性能的前提下大幅提升推理效率，为推理时优化提供了新的低成本思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in inference-time scaling have significantly improved the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, these methods typically rely on repeated generation or external verification. To address this limitation, we introduce CritICL, a novel inference-time framework that improves reasoning while maintaining high efficiency. Our key insight is that LLM failure modes exhibit structured patterns across model scales within the same family. Instead of treating failures as undesirable outputs, CritICL leverages them as a source of guidance. Specifically, we utilize failure modes derived from weaker models and incorporate them into inference through critique-based in-context examples. We propose two variants: CritICL-dynamic, which adaptively predicts input-specific failure modes and retrieves critiques, and CritICL-static, which uses a global failure mode profile to provide stable guidance. Experimental results show that CritICL consistently outperforms standard in-context learning and achieves performance competitive with or superior to test-time scaling methods, while requiring significantly fewer generations and lower token cost. Code available at: https://github.com/umwyf/CRITICL

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Zero-Shot Self-Orchestration with Ledger-Based Control for Improved LLM Coding Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26480)
*Victor Gao,Vida Khosrowshahi,Ali Khosrowshahi,Xihao Sun,Juhyun Lee,Simon,Lee*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究在多智能体LLM系统中引入"经理-工人"(manager-worker)脚手架结构的效果。通过对9个模型（从9B到~2.8T参数）在LiveCodeBench的100个最新困难问题上进行受控实验，发现该脚手架能带来真实但有条件的性能提升：对某些模型（如Qwen3.8-27B +23.4、Kimi-K3 +30.4）显著有效，对另一些模型（如Qwen3.6-35B -1到-9）无效甚至有害。脚手架使token消耗约翻三倍，但比换用更大模型更经济（例如GPT-5.6-Terra配脚手架以1/5价格接近Fable 5的精度）。机制分析发现两个关键因素：上下文管理和问题分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍声称多智能体LLM系统优于单模型基线，但证据混杂且比较通常被混淆——流水线同时改变token预算、工具调用和提示词，使聚合增益难以归因到具体因素。本文旨在通过受控实验，在无训练、无基准调优的条件下，严格评估管理器-工人脚手架结构引入的实际影响。

Method: 在9个模型（5个开源权重模型，参数从9B到~2.8T；4个前沿闭源模型）上，使用LiveCodeBench的100个最新困难问题作为基准。将同一模型在共享文件系统工作区中运行管理器-工人脚手架，与单遍回答进行配对比较（每次5个配对遍），控制模型本身作为唯一变量，无训练、无每基准调优。同时进行token成本与经济性分析，以及transcript机制分析。

Result: 脚手架效果是真实但有条件的：Qwen3.8-27B提升+23.4，GPT-5.6-Luna+10.6，GPT-5.6-Terra+8.0，Kimi-K3+30.4，Minimax-M3+11.0（均p<10^-4）；但对Qwen3.6-35B（推理关闭时）为-1到-9的负效果。Opus-5配管理器达到研究最高分91%。token成本约翻三倍，但在经济性上优于换更大模型：GPT-5.6-Terra配管理器以$11.71 vs $61.11每100题（p<10^-4）接近Fable 5的单次调用精度（85.0 vs 87.4, p=0.59）；Qwen-27B配管理器仅需$51.75且权重可自托管。关键机制为上下文管理和问题分解。大模型开推理时提升有限，推理关闭的模型和小模型开推理时提升更大。

Conclusion: 多智能体脚手架（管理器-工人结构）的性能提升并非普适，而是依赖具体模型和设置。该结构在token计算效率上显著优于升级更大模型，尤其在中等规模模型和推理关闭场景中收益最大。研究者应避免无条件宣称多智能体的优越性，而应结合模型规模、推理设置和成本约束进行针对性的实证评估。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model systems are widely reported to beat single-model baselines, but the evidence is mixed, and comparisons are usually confounded: pipelines change token budgets, tool calls, and prompts simultaneously, so an aggregate gain rarely reveals what actually helped. We investigate the effect of introducing the manager-worker scaffold over a shared filesystem workspace, with no training and no per-benchmark tuning, measured against the same model answering in a single pass. Across nine models -- five open-weight, spanning 9B to ~2.8T parameters, and four frontier closed models -- on the 100 latest hard LiveCodeBench problems, the scaffold's benefit is real but conditional: large and statistically significant for some (Qwen3.8-27B +23.4, GPT-5.6-Luna +10.6 and GPT-5.6-Terra +8.0, each over five paired passes; Kimi-K3 +30.4 and Minimax-M3 +11.0 over five paired passes with reasoning off, both at $p < 10^{-4}$, and +42 and +12 in a single pass at a 128k cap) and null or negative for others (Qwen3.6-35B -1 to -9 with reasoning off). With the manager, Opus-5 achieves the highest score in the study at 91% in one pass. Running a manager roughly triples the token bill, but it buys accuracy more cheaply than moving to a larger model does: GPT-5.6-Terra with a manager nearly matches Fable 5's single-call accuracy (85.0 against 87.4, $p = 0.59$) at a fifth of the price (\$11.71 against \$61.11 per 100-problem pass, $p < 10^{-4}$), and the Qwen-27B arm does it for \$51.75 on weights anyone can self-host. Our transcript analysis finds several mechanisms behind the gains, of which two recur: context management, in which short worker calls and shared notes organize state and reduce truncation, and problem decomposition. Improvements are modest for large models with reasoning enabled, but larger for some models with reasoning disabled and for smaller models with reasoning enabled.

</details>


### [213] [Risks and Controls for Multi-Agent Systems: an analytical framework for deployment of AI agents across organisational boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26626)
*Alistair Reid,Simon O'Callaghan,Dustin Venini,Liam Carroll,Tiberio Caetano*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，帮助组织、政策制定者和研究人员分析AI代理之间交互时产生的风险，以及这些风险如何在跨组织边界时发生变化，并探讨相应的控制措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着组织部署AI代理，这些代理之间的交互将越来越频繁——在组织内部、与合作伙伴/客户/供应商的代理、以及与开放互联网上的未知对手方。当交互跨越组织边界时，没有任何单一组织能完全看到、控制或治理它们，由此产生的失败风险需要系统性分析框架。

Method: 报告引入了三个部署层级（deployment tiers），以约束任意两个交互代理的最低共同治理为标准进行划分：(1) 单一治理（singular governance），一个组织治理所有代理；(2) 联邦治理（federated governance），多个组织在共享环境中按照约定规则部署； (3) 开放环境（open environments），代理在没有中央权威的情况下运行，共享标准仅被自愿采用（如果有的话）。在每个层级内，报告考察了风险因素、失败模式及可用控制措施，识别谁有位置应用控制，以及在无行动者可介入之处，刻画治理空白及关闭该空白所需的集体行动。

Result: 该框架展示了三个部署层级中风险如何因治理结构不同而变化，识别了各层级中可施控的行动者，并指出在无行动者能够单独施控的场景下，需要何种集体行动来弥补治理缺口。

Conclusion: AI代理间交互带来的风险是多层次的，治理方案必须根据交互环境中的最低共同治理基础进行针对性设计；在跨越组织边界的场景中，单独的组织无法完全治理风险，需要识别治理空白并推动集体行动。

Abstract: This report presents a framework to help organisations, policymakers and researchers reason about the risks that emerge when AI agents interact with each other, how those risks change as interactions cross organisational boundaries, and the controls that may help address them.
  As organisations deploy AI agents, those agents will increasingly interact with each other: inside the organisation, with the agents of partners, customers and suppliers, and with unknown counterparties on the open internet. Failures can emerge from the interactions themselves, and once those interactions cross an organisation's perimeter, no single organisation can fully see, control or govern them.
  The report introduces three deployment tiers, defined by the minimum common governance binding any two interacting agents: singular governance, where one organisation governs every agent; federated governance, where multiple organisations deploy into a shared environment under agreed rules; and open environments, where agents operate with no central authority and shared standards are adopted voluntarily if at all.
  Within each tier, the report examines risk factors, failure modes and available controls. It identifies who is positioned to apply the controls, and where no actor is positioned to act, it characterises the gap and the collective action required to close it.

</details>


### [214] [Fixed-Haven Reservation for Online Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Dense Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26759)
*Taisei Hirayama,Kohei Yoshida,Hiroki Sakaji,Itsuki Noda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定的安全港（Haven）预留机制用于在线多智能体取送货（MAPD）问题。每个智能体拥有一个固定的安全港（通常是其起始单元），仅所有者可占用，其他智能体将其视为障碍。作者证明在该固定安全港契约、Haven可达性以及明确的规划/进展假设下，所有有限释放的任务均可完成。他们实现了SHARP规划器，保证忙碌或撤退中的智能体始终在无碰撞的预留路径上返回其安全港。实验表明SHARP在稳健性上优于TP、PIBT及PIBTTP-TA基线，是唯一在所有测试配置上实现100%成功的方法，但代价是树状布局下显著更高的集中式规划开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集仓库常包含单通道巷道、死胡同和树状引导路径，几乎没有空间让空闲智能体等待而不阻塞他人。现有MAPD任务的完成保证通常依赖额外的等待端点或双连通拓扑，这些假设在密闭布局中可能不成立。本文旨在为这种受限布局提供可证明的任务完成保证。

Method: 提出固定安全港（Haven）预留契约：每个智能体拥有固定的安全港（通常为起始单元），仅所有者可占用，其他智能体视其为障碍。证明在Haven可达性与显式规划/进展假设下所有有限释放任务均可完成。实现SHARP（安全港撤退规划器），保证忙碌或撤退中的智能体始终沿无碰撞的预留路线返回其Haven。对比实验包括TP、PIBT、PIBTTP-TA等多条基线，并设计TP风格的固定回家反事实实验与无覆盖变体实验。

Result: 在稳健性扫描中，SHARP是唯一在所有测试配置上100%成功的方案，但在树状布局上集中式规划成本显著更高。TP风格的固定回家反事实（含全路径验证）在测试的树状布局上也恢复了稳健性，表明固定返回是该场景下稳健性的核心机制。无覆盖变体（禁用撤退中途重新分配）使服务时间（释放到交付延迟）恶化1.89倍、完工时间恶化1.53倍（高负载树状条件）。

Conclusion: 固定安全港预留是密集树状布局下MAPD任务完成的稳健机制，SHARP通过避免其他智能体占用关键等待位置来保证任务完成，但以更高的集中式规划成本为代价。撤退中途重新分配能力对性能有显著帮助。

Abstract: Dense warehouses often contain single-lane aisles, dead ends, and tree-like guidepaths that leave little room for idle agents to wait without blocking others. Existing Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD) guarantees for completing all finitely released tasks typically rely on extra waiting endpoints that planned paths can avoid, or on biconnected topology; these assumptions may fail in such layouts. We study fixed-Haven reservation for online MAPD, where pickup-delivery tasks are released over time. Each agent owns a fixed Safe Haven (Haven for short), usually its start cell, that only the owner may occupy and that other agents treat as blocked. For finite task releases, we prove that this fixed-Haven contract completes all released tasks under Haven-Reachability and explicit planning/progress assumptions. We implement the contract in SHARP, a Safe-Haven Retreat Planner that keeps every busy or retreating agent on a collision-free reserved route ending at its Haven. We compare SHARP with representative TP and PIBT-family MAPD baselines: Token Passing (TP), Priority Inheritance with Backtracking (PIBT), and PIBT with Temporary Priority and Temporary Avoidance (PIBTTP-TA) for biconnected main areas with attached trees. In the robustness sweep, SHARP is the only method with 100% success on all tested configurations, at substantially higher centralized planning cost on tree-like layouts. A TP-style fixed-home-return counterfactual with full-route validation also recovers robustness on tested tree-like layouts, suggesting that fixed return is a central robustness mechanism there. A no-overwrite variant shows that disabling mid-retreat reassignment worsens service time (release-to-delivery latency) by 1.89 times and makespan by 1.53 times in the tested high-load tree condition.

</details>


### [215] [Dynamic Haven Selection for Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Constrained Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26939)
*Taisei Hirayama,Kohei Yoshida,Hiroki Sakaji,Itsuki Noda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出A-sharp（自适应SHARP）算法，通过改变智能体在任务分配时的撤退目标（Haven）来优化受限仓库布局中的多智能体取送货（MAPD）问题。A-sharp通过候选Haven可用性测试和挂起释放规则，防止两个智能体依赖同一等待位置或路径穿过仍被占用的位置。在显式Haven结构和SIPP假设下，作者证明了不变量的保持和有限释放完整性。在72,000次运行、4张地图、14,400个配对场景中，A-sharp在包含更多Haven的138个配置中的107个配置上显著优于SHARP，且从未显著更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间高效的仓库布局通常包含单智能体宽的通道和尽头工作站，机器人在其中的等待位置有限，容易相互阻塞。SHARP虽然引入了撤退机制为每个任务路径扩展经过验证的撤退路线，但其固定Haven承诺可能使智能体在交货后被送往遥远的Haven，导致效率低下。因此需要一种能自适应选择撤退目标的方法。

Method: A-sharp在任务分配时改变智能体的撤退目标。具体包括：1）候选Haven的可用性测试，防止两个智能体依赖同一等待位置；2）挂起释放规则，在智能体离开之前保持先前Haven受保护，防止其他承诺路径穿过仍被占用或保留的位置。在显式Haven结构和SIPP假设下，通过归纳证明不变量保持和有限释放完整性。

Result: 在72,000次运行（14,400个配对的图-智能体数量-速率-种子案例，覆盖4张地图）中，SHARP和A-sharp均完成了各自的14,400次运行。对于完工时间（最终交付时间），在所有138个配置（Haven多于智能体）的配对比较中（使用Holm校正），A-sharp在107个配置中显著优于SHARP，从未显著更差；在测试的树形地图上，中位数减少16.7%。

Conclusion: A-sharp通过在任务分配时自适应选择撤退Haven，在保持SHARP安全性和完整性保证的同时，显著改善了完工时间表现。特别是在Haven多于智能体的配置中，A-sharp在大多数情况下显著优于SHARP，证明了自适应撤退目标机制在受限仓库布局中的有效性。

Abstract: Space-efficient warehouse layouts often contain single-agent-width aisles and dead-end workstations where robots have few places to wait without blocking others. In Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD) on such constrained layouts, robots must accept online pickup-delivery tasks while preserving protected waiting locations called Havens. The Safe HAven Retreat Planner (SHARP) introduced a mechanism that extends each committed task path with a validated retreat to the agent's dedicated initial Haven, but fixed-Haven commitments can send agents toward distant Havens after deliveries. We present A-sharp (Adaptive SHARP), which changes an agent's retreat target at task assignment time. A naive switch can cause two agents to rely on the same waiting location or let another committed path pass through a location that is still occupied or reserved. A-sharp prevents these failures with an availability test for candidate Havens and a pending-release rule that keeps the previous Haven protected until the agent departs. Under explicit Haven-structure and Safe Interval Path Planning (SIPP) assumptions, we prove invariant preservation and finite-release completeness: every task in any finite release sequence is delivered in finite time. Across 72,000 runs on 14,400 paired map-agent-count-rate-seed cases over four maps, both SHARP and A-sharp complete their respective 14,400 runs. For makespan (final delivery time), a prespecified paired comparison with Holm correction over all 138 configurations with more Havens than agents finds A-sharp significantly better in 107 configurations and never significantly worse than SHARP; on the tested tree map, the median reduction is 16.7%.

</details>


### [216] [One Model, Many Minds: Unlocking Multi-Agent Synergy in a Single Agent via Mixture of Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27338)
*Zhichen Zeng,Huiyuan Chen,Jingru Cheng,Juan Zha,Ming Liu,Ying Chen,Xiyuan Yang,Chaosheng Dong,Haiyang Zhang,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出MoRe（Mixture of Roles）方法，通过学习一个多样化的steering vector码本，并利用查询感知路由器将多个角色动态融合为单一steering vector，在单智能体、单轮推理中实现多视角专业化，性能与多智能体系统相当的同时将token成本降低20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体范式依赖预定义人设或steering vector，只能提供固定的单一专业化，无法适应多样化查询；而多智能体系统虽能实现动态多视角推理，但需要多轮交互融合不同角色，导致上下文长度和推理成本显著膨胀。

Method: MoRe包含三个核心组件：(1) 学习一个多样化的steering vector码本（codebook），每个向量编码一个潜在角色；(2) 设计查询感知路由器（query-aware router），根据输入查询动态将码本中的多个向量融合为组合steering vector；(3) 通过三阶段SFT课程学习和GRPO后训练高效训练MoRe，同时保持骨干LLM冻结。

Result: 在推理和人格测试基准上，MoRe相比单智能体基线平均提升2.2%，性能与多智能体系统（MAS）相当，同时将token成本降低20倍。

Conclusion: MoRe成功将多角色专业化能力压缩到单个steering vector中，实现了单智能体、单轮推理下的动态多视角问题求解，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了推理成本，为LLM专业化提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Specializing Large Language Models (LLMs) toward distinct abilities underpins successes ranging from personalized assistants to multi-agent systems (MAS). Single-agent paradigms rely on pre-defined personas or steering vectors to induce specialization, yet they impose a single fixed specialization that fails to adapt to diverse queries. Conversely, MAS achieves dynamic multi-perspective problem solving by orchestrating agents with distinct text-based roles, but fusing these specializations requires multi-turn interactions that inflate context length and inference cost. To address these limitations, we propose Mixture of Roles (MoRe), which adaptively composes multiple specializations into a single steering vector for single-turn inference. Specifically, MoRe learns a diversified codeboox of steering vectors, each of which encodes a latent role. A query-aware router dynamically fuses the codebook into a steering vector that encompasses multiple roles. By steering the backbone LLM with the composed vector, MoRe enables multi-perspective specialization in a single-agent, single-turn inference process. The proposed MoRe can be efficiently trained via a three-stage SFT curriculum and GRPO post-training, while the backbone LLM remains frozen. Experiments across reasoning and personality benchmarks show that MoRe outperforms single-agent baselines by 2.2% on average, and achieves performance on par with MAS while reducing token cost by 20x.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EduRiskX，一个结合Transformer时间序列预测器与F-Logic符号推理的神经符号框架，用于在线教育学生学业风险预测，在OULAD数据集上达到0.900准确率和0.894的F1分数，平均早期检测周为9.32周。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线教育学业风险预测模型存在两个关键缺陷：早期检测能力有限和可解释性不足，这导致"黑箱"信任危机，阻碍了模型在实际教学场景中的部署应用。

Method: EduRiskX包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于时间注意力的Transformer预测器，利用类加权损失和动态周截断对纵向学生活动序列建模；(2) 基于F-Logic的符号规则库，依据参与理论（Engagement Theory）和学生整合模型（Student Integration Model）构建，仅从训练数据中提取知识，模仿人类教育者的诊断逻辑；(3) 基于逻辑回归的融合机制，学习神经风险概率与符号置信度分数的相对贡献。

Result: 在OULAD数据集上采用严格的80/10/10学生级别划分，期末（第38周）达到0.900的准确率和0.894的F1分数，平均早期检测周为9.32周，检测率达94.30%。相较于PatchTST、iTransformer等SOTA时序模型以及LSTM、CNN等深度学习基线，EduRiskX在相同条件下实现了更高的召回率和更早的风险识别。

Conclusion: EduRiskX不仅取得了更优的预测性能，其F-Logic模块还能提供结构化的基于规则的解释，将预测结果与可观察的行为模式和教育理论关联起来，兼顾了预测准确性与可解释性，解决了现有模型的可信度问题。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [218] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本可行性研究比较了独立LLM与四步智能体管线在ICU死亡风险解释中的表现。XGBoost模型AUROC为0.855，在38例解释子集上，独立LLM存在1例显式结局泄漏，而智能体管线无泄漏；智能体管线在指南依据、数值特异性与合理性上更优，但在SHAP对齐与方向一致性上弱于独立LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型能准确预测ICU死亡率，但特征归因方法单独使用难以提供临床所需的叙事性解释。研究者探索用大语言模型弥合这一差距，并验证多步智能体管线（将数据解释、指南检查和最终解释分离）是否是合理的延伸方案。

Method: 使用eICU Demo数据集（2,353例ICU住院，8.1%死亡率），XGBoost模型预测ICU死亡率；保留独立LLM与四步智能体管线的对比设计，在38例分层解释子集及14例与SHAP复核子集重叠的案例上，比较两者在结局泄漏、SHAP对齐（Jaccard）、方向一致性、指南依据、数值特异性和合理性等方面的表现。

Result: XGBoost AUROC为0.855（95%CI 0.796–0.906），AUPRC为0.332（95%CI 0.217–0.494）。独立LLM产生1例显式结局泄漏，智能体管线无泄漏。在14例重叠案例中，独立LLM的SHAP对齐（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）和方向一致性（92.9% vs 78.6%）更高；智能体管线的指南依据（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）和合理性（0.700 vs 0.671）更高。

Conclusion: 智能体分解可能改善安全相关的指南依据和患者个体化细节，但在高风险风险解释场景中应结合基于归因的检查后再使用。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [219] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了大语言模型在电池预测与健康管理（BPHM）中的应用，涵盖Transformer架构、自监督预训练、多模态数据集及PEFT等基础技术，从缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化鲁棒性、融合领域知识提升可解释性、以及系统级自动化四个维度分类梳理研究进展，并提出了包括构建协作数据生态、验证工业智能、物理信息增强可信度及高效端侧部署的未来路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的BPHM方法（如基于物理的模型和任务驱动的深度学习）面临计算效率低、参数化困难、跨域泛化能力差、依赖大量标注的run-to-failure数据以及可解释性不足等瓶颈。大模型基于Transformer架构和自监督预训练，为克服这些长期存在的问题提供了新的变革性范式，亟需系统梳理和总结。

Method: 本文是一项综述性研究，首先阐明了支撑大模型的基础技术（Transformer架构、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据集、PEFT技术），然后沿四个关键维度对最新进展进行分类：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、融合领域知识实现可解释性、以及实现系统级自动化。

Result: 大模型在BPHM领域展现出缓解数据稀缺、提升跨域泛化能力、增强模型可解释性及推动系统级自动化的潜力，但数据可获取性、智能验证、可信赖性以及部署可行性等方面仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: 本文提出了面向未来的研究路线图，包括构建协作式数据生态系统、验证工业应用中的智能能力、以物理信息设计增强可信赖性、以及实现高效的设备端部署，为下一代电池管理系统（能够实现安全、可靠、自主运行）的开发提供系统化的指导。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [220] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PICasso，一个基于AI辅助的自动化光子集成电路（PIC）设计框架，能够从自然语言规格自动完成合成、验证和优化。作者还引入了PIC-Set基准测试集（36个参数化PIC设计任务），并在此基准上系统评估了多种大语言模型。实验表明PICasso显著提升了规格满足率（结构Spec@3达92.7%，功能Spec@3达52%），并通过仿真引导优化将平均插入损耗从4.98 dB降至3.25 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子集成电路（PIC）设计目前高度依赖人工GUI操作，流程繁琐且效率低。尽管大语言模型在许多领域展现出自动化潜力，但在PIC设计中需要严格的领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈才能真正生成可制造的布局。现有研究缺乏系统的评估基准和统一的评测协议，因此需要一个端到端的AI辅助框架和标准化基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: PICasso采用"NL -> YAML -> GDS"结构化生成流水线，包含四个关键组件：（1）PDK（工艺设计套件）感知的知识注入，确保生成符合工艺规则；（2）自动化布局与布线；（3）DRC/LVS物理验证；（4）基于SAX的光子仿真。同时引入PIC-Set基准，包含36个参数化PIC任务，并设计了新的评测指标（结构/功能Spec@k、优化效率、扰动鲁棒性）来统一评估各类LLM。

Result: 在PIC-Set基准上，PICasso相比普通LLM生成显著提升了端到端规格满足率：高复杂度电路上结构Spec@3达92.7%，功能Spec@3达52%。通过仿真引导优化，平均插入损耗从4.98 dB降低到3.25 dB（改善1.74 dB）。运行时间与手动GUI流程具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈的有机结合，能够将LLM从脆弱的网表生成器转变为实用的PIC设计代理，能够生成可制造的布局并满足端到端设计规格。这为AI驱动光子设计领域提供了可行的方法论和可复现的评测基准。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [221] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFQA，一个确定性工具驱动的多智能体LLM框架，用于计算密集型金融问答。该框架将语言理解与数值执行分离，通过专业智能体分工和Python工具完成金融计算，在定期存款查询基准上达到95.54%的计算密集型查询准确率和90.87%的整体准确率，显著超越直接使用LLM的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自然语言任务上表现优异，但面对涉及结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和规则约束的多步金融计算时，经常产生数值错误但看似合理的答案。这一局限促使研究者探索将语言理解与数值执行分离的新方法。

Method: CIFQA是一个确定性工具驱动的多智能体LLM框架，将语言理解与数值执行解耦。框架分配专业化智能体分别负责查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，同时使用确定性的基于Python的工具执行金融计算和规则应用。该方法在定期存款查询问答场景中进行了实例化。

Result: CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%的准确率，整体准确率为90.87%，大幅超越提供完整公式、利率表和基准指令的直接LLM基线。消融研究表明，精确利率查询、存期计算、滚动年份调整和提前支取逻辑等确定性组件是性能的关键贡献者。值得注意的是，17B开源模型在CIFQA框架内的表现优于使用相同金融信息的大型前沿模型。

Conclusion: 研究证明架构设计比模型规模更能决定数值可靠性，CIFQA为计算密集型金融推理任务提供了一个可泛化的框架。虽然当前在定期存款查询上进行了评估，但该框架可推广至更广泛的金融推理场景。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [222] [The Artificial Experimentalist: Discovery and Control of Self-Organizing Phenomena with Autotelic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26116)
*Marko Cvjetko,Benedikt Hartl,Michael Levin,Clément Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于自决强化学习（autotelic RL）的闭环框架CARL，用于探索和控制Lenia这类连续元胞自动机中的涌现现象。CARL通过最小局部扰动自主采样多样目标并学习目标条件策略，能发现稳定孤子、控制已有孤子的运动方向，并支持人类通过高层指令实时引导孤子穿越迷宫环境，且策略可零样本泛化到分布外条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索元胞自动机等复杂系统的方法多为开环操作：设定初始条件、执行完整模拟、观察结果，过程中无法干预。这种方式限制了研究者对涌现现象的主动发现和控制能力，缺乏一个能在系统演化过程中实时介入的闭环框架。

Method: 采用自决强化学习框架：智能体自主采样多样化的目标，学习一个目标条件策略（goal-conditioned policy），通过对复杂系统施加最小、局部的扰动进行干预。该框架实例化为CARL系统，应用于Lenia（一种以类生命自组织模式著称的连续元胞自动机）。训练时覆盖多样目标、更新规则和随机初始状态。

Result: CARL在广泛Lenia更新规则下以比启发式基线更高的速率发现稳定孤子；能够通过少量干预学会引导已有孤子的运动方向，表明不仅能创造还能控制自组织模式；训练好的智能体可将人类的高层方向指令转化为低层干预，支持人类实时引导孤子通过迷宫环境；学到的策略可零样本泛化到各种分布外条件。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将自决强化学习应用于复杂系统控制的可行性，为构建"人工实验者智能体"开辟了路径——这类智能体能在自主或人类引导下，对复杂系统中的涌现现象进行发现和控制。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>


### [223] [Agent Mesh: Reliability Primitives for Non-Idempotent Agent Delegation - Identity Adequacy and Evidence Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26225)
*Mazhar Shaikh,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde,Harshal Pathak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对生产级智能体软件交付平台的147起编号事故（分布于81次运行）进行失败研究，揭示以服务网格的"重试、超时、错误率熔断"为代表的编排机制所依赖的三大假设在实践中全部失效，并量化了后果（如54次连续成功工具调用的循环、恒定的进度信号导致误熔断等）。研究发现跨切面根因是"身份充分性"与"证据充分性"的双重缺失，并据此提出七个以委派（delegation）为执行单元而非消息的可靠性原语，同时给出了受此研究启发但尚未开展的受控评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自治智能体在编排器（orchestrator）的控制下执行有界软件任务，编排器依赖重试、超时和错误率熔断等源自服务网格的机制。作者希望检验这些机制在真实生产环境中的假设是否成立，量化其失效代价，并从失败数据中提炼更合理的可靠性原语。

Method: 对生产级智能体软件交付平台的147起编号事故进行分析，覆盖81次运行，每次运行均有测量成本且多数事故附带可复现缺陷的突变证明（mutation proof），据此量化三种编排基础假设的违反及其后果，并归纳出跨切面根因。

Result: 所有三个支撑原语（重试、超时、错误率熔断）的假设在实际中均被违反：发现54次连续成功工具调用构成错误率断路器不可见的循环；恒定的进度信号必然在第三轮修复时误触发，使一次运行从六个组件成功降为三个；六次委派累计21个事件使一个正确且幂等的组件无法取胜；一次路由错误的失败唤醒五个组件却只为两个组件故障服务，三个旁观者回退工作代码；还有12起事件是执行层阻止了正确工作，最昂贵的一起耗107个agent轮次、零次被接受的写入。根因为身份充分性和证据充分性双重缺失。

Conclusion: 可靠性决策应基于能够移动、可归因于所测对象、且在相同条件下确定性的证据；在执行单元上应从消息转向委派。基于研究发现提出七个可靠性原语，并明确示意了后续应进行的受控评估（本研究尚不构成受控实验）。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly perform bounded software tasks under an orchestrator that retries, resumes, and budgets them. The machinery such orchestrators reach for is the service mesh's: retry, timeout, and error-rate circuit breaking. We report a failure study of a production agentic software-delivery platform over 147 numbered incidents spanning 81 runs, each with a measured cost and, in most cases, a mutation proof reproducing the failure. All three assumptions those primitives rest on are violated in practice, and we quantify the consequences: a loop of fifty-four consecutive successful tool calls no error-rate breaker could see; a progress signal constant by construction, guaranteeing a false trip on the third repair round and driving one run from six of six components to three; twenty-one events accumulated across six invocations of one delegation, making a correct, idempotent component unwinnable; a misrouted failure that woke five components for a two-component fault, leaving three bystanders regressing working code; and twelve incidents in which the enforcement layer blocked correct work, the most expensive costing 107 agent turns and zero accepted writes. We find one cross-cutting cause and its dual. Identity adequacy: in five separate subsystems an identity that failed to discriminate produced a confident wrong answer, and two of them derived the corrective rule independently. Evidence adequacy: a reliability decision may be taken only on evidence capable of moving, attributable to what it measures, and deterministic under identical conditions. From the findings we derive seven reliability primitives whose enforcement unit is the delegation rather than the message, and specify the controlled evaluation the study motivates but does not constitute.

</details>


### [224] [The Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox Quantifying Net Energy Loss in on-Device Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26134)
*Jaeik Jeong,Tai-Yeon Ku,Wan-Ki Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了能源预测中的"精确度-效率悖论"：高精度的能源预测模型可能因边缘AI推理能耗和电池老化而导致净能耗赤字。作者提出一个总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗与电池老化统一视为能量损耗，以最小化净能量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源预测旨在最大化精度以提升能效，但现有研究忽视了边缘AI推理本身的能耗和电池老化带来的能量损失。在任务关键型边缘环境（如军事系统）中，高精度复杂架构的能耗可能超过其节省的能量，形成净能耗赤字，这一悖论此前未被系统性揭示。

Method: 提出一个Total Cost of Ownership（TCO）框架，将推理能量消耗与电池老化统一建模为一种能量损失形式——将退化视为系统未来能量承载能力的物理耗散，从而在热敏感边缘环境中评估不同预测架构的净能量效益。

Result: 证明了在热敏感边缘环境中，复杂架构凭借更高精度所节省的能量，往往被其高运算强度带来的总能量损失所抵消，甚至反超，导致净能量赤字。

Conclusion: 能源预测模型的设计不应只追求预测精度最大化，而应以最小化净能量损失为目标；TCO框架为在边缘环境中选择预测架构提供了更全面的评估准则。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss. This framework treats not only inference energy consumption but also battery aging as a unified form of energy loss, as degradation represents a physical dissipation of the system's future energy-carrying capacity. We demonstrate that in thermally sensitive edge environments, energy saved by the superior precision of complex architectures is often outweighed by the total energy lost through their high operational intensity.

</details>


### [225] [SKILL.state: Scalable Long-Horizon Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26263)
*Sanket Badhe,Priyanka Tiwari,Jonghyun Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SKILL.state运行时架构，以显式可变执行状态替代追加式对话历史，仅向模型提供技能规范、当前状态和最新观测，在提升任务准确率的同时大幅降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体运行时通过不断追加观测、动作和推理痕迹到对话历史来维持执行，导致长时程任务中出现延迟恶化和上下文中毒失败，需要新的运行时架构来支持可扩展的长时程智能体技能。

Method: SKILL.state是一种运行时架构，用显式可变执行状态取代追加式对话历史。每一步执行时，模型仅接收不可变的技能规范、当前结构化执行状态和最新观测；中间推理在产生验证通过的状态更新后立即丢弃，从而防止提示随执行历史增长。

Result: 在多种数据集、模型和执行环境下，SKILL.state在提升任务准确率的同时大幅减少累计token消耗。

Conclusion: 显式执行状态是一种有效且与架构无关的抽象，可用于可扩展的长时程智能体技能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents executing complex, long-running procedural skills. Existing agent runtimes maintain execution by continually appending observations, actions, and intermediate reasoning traces to an ever-growing conversation history, causing latency degradation and context-poisoning failures over long horizons. We present SKILL.state, a runtime architecture that replaces append-only conversational history with an explicit, mutable execution state. At each execution step, the model receives only the immutable skill specification, the current structured execution state, and the latest observation. Intermediate reasoning is discarded immediately after producing a validated state update, preventing prompt growth with execution history. Across diverse datasets, models, and execution environments, SKILL.state improves task accuracy while substantially reducing cumulative token consumption. Our results demonstrate that explicit execution state is an effective and architecture-agnostic abstraction for scalable long-horizon agent skills.

</details>


### [226] [LLMs for Academic Workflows: An Evaluation of Literature Reviews Generated with Short and Long Context Windows of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26145)
*Muhammad Ali Chaudhry,Xinyuan Hao,Haifa Alwahaby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了LLM在短上下文和长上下文设置下生成的20篇文献综述，由两名研究者基于15个维度进行评价。研究发现AI生成的文献综述需要人工监督才能达到学术出版标准；随着上下文窗口增大，LLM能整合更广泛的信息并保持长输入连贯性，但同时也加剧了内容重复、遗漏关键文献和重描述而轻综合等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究上下文窗口大小对AI生成文献综述质量的影响，以及AI在文献综述写作中能扮演什么样的支持角色，为学术写作中AI工具的使用提供实证依据。

Method: 基于Semantic Scholar和Arxiv的研究来源，生成20篇AI文献综述；由两名研究者跨15个维度对短上下文和长上下文两种设置下的生成结果进行评估和比较。

Result: AI生成的文献综述需要人工监督才能达到学术出版标准；更长的上下文窗口使LLM能纳入更广的信息并维持长输入的连贯性，但同时加剧了内容重复、错失关键研究以及过度描述而非综合分析的问题。

Conclusion: AI生成的综述可作为基础性概览，但必须由领域专家进行批判性评估和精炼。未来研究应探索集成不同LLM和微调模型，采用结合人类专业知识与AI能力的混合方法来解决当前识别出的局限性。

Abstract: Our research focuses on evaluating literature reviews generated in short and long context settings of large language models (LLMs) to investigate the impact of context window on the quality of AI-generated literature reviews and the role of AI in supporting literature review writing. Twenty AI-generated literature reviews based on research sources from Semantic Scholar and Arxiv were evaluated by two researchers across 15 dimensions. Our findings reveal that AI-generated literature reviews require human oversight to meet academic publishing standards. As context windows increase, LLMs can incorporate broader information and maintain coherence across longer inputs, but they also exacerbate issues such as content repetition, omission of critical work, and a tendency towards descriptiveness over synthesis. Our work shows that AI-generated reviews can provide foundational overviews, but their output must be critically evaluated and refined by domain experts. Future research should consider integrating other LLMs and fine-tuned models in different domains with hybrid approaches that combine human expertise with AI capabilities to address the limitations identified in this study.

</details>


### [227] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系超图Transformer（RHT），一种将关系数据库表示为超图、学习五维嵌入（PentE）并执行稀疏关系注意力的统一架构。其注意力复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数量的平方。在Synthea合成EHR数据上，RHT在SNOMED CT条件码多标签预测任务中展现出最强的嵌入语义连贯性，且计算上可扩展；XGBoost在稀有码召回率上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表学习在医疗等复杂信息系统中仍是重大挑战。关系数据包含多种复杂性来源：大体积数据、高维变量、高基数类别特征、复杂的表间依赖和重复的时间观测。现有方法难以同时处理这些挑战，因此需要一种统一的架构来有效学习关系数据。

Method: RHT将关系数据库建模为超图，引入五维嵌入（PentE）表示实体，并采用稀疏关系注意力机制。其注意力复杂度与平均关系度成比例，而非实体数量的平方，从而保证计算可扩展性。论文形式化定义了架构，推导了注意力机制的复杂度，并提供了开源参考实现。

Result: 在Synthea合成EHR数据集的SNOMED CT条件码多标签预测基准上，RHT相较于表格、关系和时序图基线产生更语义连贯的嵌入且计算可扩展。XGBoost在稀有码召回率上最优，RHT在嵌入语义连贯性上最强。此外还通过消融实验量化了各架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: RHT作为一种统一架构有效解决了关系数据学习中的复杂度问题，尤其在嵌入语义连贯性方面表现突出。计划在PhysioNet认证后于MIMIC-IV上进行临床验证。源码和实验协议已公开。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [228] [SIGMA: Structured Noise-Effect-Aware Grouped Multi-Agent Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26683)
*Li Mingqian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SIGMA框架，一个利用合作结构学习噪声观测下鲁棒表示的分层多智能体强化学习方法，通过密度聚类的组内共识聚合和组间注意力集成来应对结构化噪声效应，在星际争霸II中验证了鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在噪声观测下难以保持稳健协调。现有方法忽略了噪声引起的决策扰动会通过合作结构产生局域相关性（即结构化噪声效应），导致无法显式利用这种结构依赖的噪声特性来提升鲁棒性。

Method: SIGMA采用分层协作框架：(1) 通过密度聚类将智能体组织为自适应局部结构；(2) 组内共识聚合保留共享任务相关信息并平滑个体表征偏差；(3) 组间注意力机制在不同组间自适应整合信息，保持全局协调同时容纳异构贡献。

Result: 在星际争霸II的噪声观测任务上，实验验证了结构化噪声效应的存在，并证明SIGMA在观测噪声下持续提升鲁棒性，同时在无噪声环境中保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模和利用合作结构中的噪声效应是提升多智能体鲁棒性的有效途径，分层协作框架在噪声与无噪声场景下均能取得优越性能。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) faces significant challenges in maintaining robust coordination under noisy observations. Although observation disturbances are often introduced independently across agents, their downstream effects on cooperative decision-making can become structured through underlying cooperation structures. We characterize this phenomenon as structured noise effects, where noise-induced decision effects exhibit local correlation among agents with stronger task-related dependencies while remaining globally heterogeneous across different agents and local structures. Existing robust MARL methods, however, rarely explicitly characterize or exploit such structure-dependent noise effects. To address this limitation, we propose SIGMA, a hierarchical collaboration framework that exploits cooperation structures to learn robust representations under noisy observations. SIGMA first organizes agents into adaptive local structures through density-based grouping and performs intra-group consensus aggregation to preserve shared task-relevant information while smoothing agent-specific representation deviations. Inter-group attention then adaptively integrates information across different groups to preserve global coordination while accommodating their heterogeneous contributions. Experiments on noisy-observation tasks in StarCraft II empirically validate the structured noise effects and demonstrate that SIGMA consistently improves robustness under observation noise while maintaining competitive performance in noise-free environments.

</details>


### [229] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Systematic Literature Review of Disease Spread Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26150)
*Orhan Yagizer Cinar,Timur Emre Ozkose,Emma Von Hoene,Amira Roess,Taylor Anderson,Hamdi Kavak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个LLM流水线，用于从536篇基于代理建模（ABM）论文中提取模型相关信息，并与人工系统性文献综述（SLR）结果进行对比。GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0的论文级准确率分别约为77.95%和81.67%，字段级准确率在32.40%到100%之间。研究发现LLM间的共识度可作为输出质量的潜在指标，低共识可能暗示幻觉，高共识但低准确率则可能指向人工数据集中的噪声或错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的进步为自动化系统性文献综述等研究流程创造了机会。作者希望通过开发并评估LLM流水线，探索其在建模与仿真领域全规模系统性文献综述中的实际潜力与局限性，同时为提示工程提供实用见解。

Method: 开发了一个LLM流水线，用于从536篇经同行评审的ABM论文中提取模型相关信息。使用GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0两种模型进行信息提取，并将结果与人工进行的系统性文献综述进行逐字段和逐论文级别的对比，同时分析LLM间的一致性与输出质量之间的关系。

Result: GPT-4.1的论文级准确率约为77.95%，GPT-5.0约为81.67%。字段级准确率范围为32.40%至100%，复杂或主观性较强的字段可靠性较低。LLM间一致性与输出质量存在相关性：低一致性可能预示幻觉，高一致性但低准确率则可能反映人工数据集本身存在噪声或错误。

Conclusion: 研究为LLM在建模与仿真领域的全规模系统性文献综述应用提供了实践见解，展示了LLM自动化SLR的潜力，同时明确指出了其在复杂、主观字段上的局限性，并提出将LLM间共识度作为质量诊断指标这一有价值的方法论贡献。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to streamline and potentially automate many research processes, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). This study reports an LLM pipeline development for extracting model-relevant information from 536 peer-reviewed agent-based modeling papers. We compare the results with those of a human-conducted SLR. Our results show paper-level accuracies of approximately 77.95% for GPT-4.1 and 81.67% for GPT-5.0. Field-level accuracy ranges from 32.40% to 100.00%, with more complex or subjective fields performing less reliably. Importantly, we find that agreement between LLMs is a potential indicator of output quality: low agreement may signal hallucinations, whereas high agreement combined with low accuracy may point to noise or errors in the human dataset. Overall, our study provides practical insights into prompt development and highlights both the potential and limitations of using LLMs for full-scale SLRs in the modeling and simulation domain.

</details>


### [230] [Decoupling Planning and Control for Instructable Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26788)
*Zineng Tang,Kelsey R. Allen,Sjoerd van Steenkiste,Ishita Dasgupta,Alane Suhr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Instruct-to-Act系统，将VLM规划器的高层文本指令与世界模型控制器的低频/高频动作生成能力结合，通过行为克隆和语言指令重标注训练控制器，在七个具身环境中验证了该解耦架构优于纯控制器和直接VLM动作生成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练指令微调的视觉语言模型（VLM）擅长将指令和观察映射为高层计划，但在陌生环境中难以生成可靠的低延迟动作序列；而世界模型控制器擅长快速的观察到动作的控制，但缺乏开放式的任务引导。二者互补，需要一种将两者优势结合的系统。

Method: 将VLM规划器（低频、稀疏、高层文本指令）与世界模型控制器（高频自主动作）解耦组合。训练控制器时，用合成指令对控制器策略回放段进行重新标注，并联合优化行为克隆目标与原有的奖励最大化和世界建模目标。

Result: 在七个具身环境（含三个多智能体环境）中，在匹配观察和动作空间的条件下，该解耦方法一致优于纯控制器和直接VLM动作生成变体；保持快速控制能力；可无需微调替换不同预训练VLM规划器；在七个任务中的六个上与强VLA和多智能体RL基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: VLM规划器与世界模型控制器的解耦结合是一种有效范式，既保留了VLM的开放性任务理解与规划能力，又保持了世界模型控制器的高频低延迟控制性能，且具备灵活的模块可替换性。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pre-trained, instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) perform well at mapping from instructions and observations to high-level plans, but struggle to realize such plans as reliable low-latency action sequences in unfamiliar environments. At the same time, world-model controllers excel at fast observation-to-action control, but lack open-ended task guidance. In this work, we combine these strengths into a single system, Instruct-to-Act, where we train a world-model controller to act autonomously at high frequency when conditioned on sparse, higher-latency, and high-level text instructions generated by a VLM planner. To train controllers to be language-instructable, we relabel segments of controller policy rollouts with synthetic instructions and jointly optimize a behavior-cloning objective along with existing reward-maximizing and world-modeling objectives. We evaluate our proposed approach across seven embodied environments, including three multi-agent environments where VLM planners coordinate through language while trained controllers serve as their actuators. Under matched observation and action spaces, our decoupled approach consistently outperforms controller-only and direct VLM action-generation variants, preserves fast control, and lets us swap in different pretrained VLM planners without fine-tuning, while remaining competitive with strong vision-language-action and multi-agent RL baselines on six of seven tasks.

</details>


### [231] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Customer Churn Prediction in Telecommunications: A Framework for CRM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26151)
*Sandeep Gaddamwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对电信行业客户流失问题，对比四种分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM）在IBM Telco Churn基准上的表现，并引入SHAP和LIME提供可解释性，最后提出四层CRM集成架构将预测与留存行动衔接起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度流失预测模型虽是黑盒，缺乏可解释性，导致留存顾问无法依据概率分数设计个性化干预；本文旨在弥合预测性能与业务可操作性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于IBM Telco Churn数据集（7,043条记录、19个特征、SMOTE平衡至50%流失率），比较Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM四类分类器；采用全局SHAP排序与实例级SHAP/LIME分解提供双重粒度的可解释性；提出四层CRM集成架构（分层细分、特征到留存模板映射、结果反馈重训循环）。

Result: 逻辑回归取得最高AUC-ROC（0.8411），LightGBM取得最高准确率（78.42%）；四模型AUC差距仅0.011；全局SHAP揭示任期、总费用和按月合同为关键流失信号；针对最高风险五分位客户可降低总流失率3.3-5.3个百分点，每轮活动节省约$199K-$319K。

Conclusion: 可解释AI（SHAP/LIME）与高性能模型结合，配合结构化CRM集成架构，可使流失预测从分析层真正落地到前线留存运营，在保持模型性能的同时实现可操作的个性化干预。

Abstract: Subscriber attrition is a costly, persistent challenge for telecommunications providers, with monthly churn of roughly 1.9% in mature markets eroding billions in revenue annually. Predictive models can flag at-risk customers accurately, yet they are routinely excluded from frontline CRM workflows because high-performing ensemble and non-linear architectures are opaque: a retention specialist cannot design a personalised intervention from a probability score alone, without knowing why a subscriber is at risk. This paper addresses that gap. We benchmark four classifiers--Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM--on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (7,043 records; 19 features; 26.5% churn, balanced to 50% via SMOTE on the training partition only). Logistic Regression attains the strongest AUC-ROC (0.8411) and LightGBM the highest accuracy (78.42%); all four fall within a 0.011 AUC band (0.831--0.841), and 5-fold cross-validation confirms the leading models are effectively tied. Explanations are delivered at two granularities: a global SHAP ranking identifying tenure, total charges, and month-to-month contract as the dominant churn signals, and instance-level SHAP and LIME decompositions that expose the drivers behind each prediction. Building on these outputs, we introduce a four-layer CRM integration architecture that converts risk scores and attribution vectors into tiered segmentation, maps top features to structured retention-action templates, and routes campaign outcomes into a retraining feedback loop. Targeting the highest-risk quintile is projected to cut overall churn by 3.3--5.3 percentage points, preserving an estimated $199K--$319K per campaign cycle.

</details>


### [232] [LiveSim: Simulating Environment-Shaped Users in Multi-Agent Live-Stream Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26849)
*Jiaqi Xu,Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LiveSim，一个基于大语言模型的直播生态系统仿真框架。它将用户表示为可编辑的行为假设，通过轨迹接地交互逐步细化这些假设，将仿真与真实轨迹之间的差异提取为可迁移的环境-行为模式并积累到集体行为记忆中，以提升用户级行为保真度并支持生态系统级仿真。真实直播风控数据实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户行为仿真器通常依赖从历史观测推断出的静态用户画像，这在直播这类社交密集环境中不够充分，因为交互动态会持续重塑用户行为。需要一种能够捕捉环境动态影响并持续更新用户行为模型的仿真框架。

Method: LiveSim框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将用户表示为可编辑的行为假设；(2) 通过轨迹接地交互逐步细化这些假设，利用仿真轨迹与真实观测轨迹之间的差异来揭示缺失的环境塑造效应；(3) 将这些差异信号提取为可迁移的环境-行为模式，并积累到集体行为记忆中，同时支持用户级行为保真度和生态系统级仿真。

Result: 在真实世界直播风控数据上的实验验证了LiveSim在提升用户级行为保真度方面的有效性，并能够实现生态系统层面的风险演变分析和平台干预效果评估。

Conclusion: LiveSim通过将静态用户画像升级为动态可编辑的行为假设，并利用轨迹接地交互和集体行为记忆机制，有效应对了社交密集环境中用户行为动态变化的挑战，在用户级和生态系统级两个层面均取得了良好的仿真效果。

Abstract: User behavior simulation with large language models~(LLMs) is increasingly used to support multi-agent ecosystem simulation. Existing simulators typically rely on static user profiles inferred from historical observations, which become inadequate in socially intensive environments such as live streaming where interaction dynamics continuously reshape user behavior. We propose \textbf{LiveSim}, an LLM-based framework for live-stream ecosystem simulation. It represents users as editable behavioral hypotheses and progressively refines them through trajectory-grounded interactions, where discrepancies between simulated and observed trajectories reveal missing environmental shaping effects. These signals are further extracted as transferable environment-behavior patterns and accumulated in a collective behavioral memory to improve user-level behavioral fidelity and support ecosystem-level simulation. Experiments on real-world live-stream risk-control data validate the effectiveness of LiveSim in improving user-level behavioral fidelity and enabling ecosystem-level analysis of risk evolution and platform intervention effects.

</details>


### [233] [EEG-to-Report: An Annotation and Feature-Text Framework for Training Language Models on Clinical EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26153)
*Xuan-The Tran,Le Trung Kien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍EEG-to-Report，一个基于浏览器的注释和特征-文本框架，将常规EEG审查与AI就绪数据集构建相结合，支持多格式EEG导入、通道标准化、交互式查看器、多模态注释层（文本+语音转录）、特征提取引擎以及基于CNN集成和LLM的自动报告模块，为多模态EEG语言模型提供对齐的特征-文本监督数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG报告主要依赖人工且耗时，现有EEG软件生态系统无法生成训练现代语言模型所需的结构化EEG文本监督信号。大多数工具箱仅关注可视化或预处理，缺乏对生成AI高质量数据集工作流的支持。

Method: 构建浏览器端注释与特征-文本框架，集成多格式EEG数据摄取、通道标准化、交互式查看器，以及结合文本和转录语音注释的多模态注释层。对每个注释片段，特征提取引擎计算标准化谱域、时域、熵、Hjorth、连通性和尖峰相关描述符，与临床描述一并存储在可移植JSON模式中，形成对齐的特征-文本对。自动报告模块使用CNN集成结合LLM起草临床叙述供神经科医生审阅。

Result: 通过试点注释，展示了EEG-to-Report能够简化注释工作流并生成可编辑的草稿报告，为自动化EEG报告系统提供了可复用基础。

Conclusion: EEG-to-Report为多模态EEG语言模型提供了结构化的特征-文本监督数据生成基础设施，有望减少人工EEG报告负担并推动自动化EEG报告系统的发展。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) reporting remains largely manual and time-consuming, and current EEG software ecosystems do not produce the structured EEG-text supervision needed for training modern language models. Most toolboxes focus on visualization or preprocessing, providing limited support for workflows that generate high-quality datasets for AI. We introduce EEG-to-Report, a browser-based annotation and feature-text framework that links routine EEG review with the construction of AI-ready datasets. The framework integrates multi-format EEG ingestion, channel standardization, and an interactive viewer with a multimodal annotation layer that combines typed text and transcribed voice notes. For each annotated segment, a feature extraction engine computes a standardized set of spectral, temporal, entropy, Hjorth, connectivity, and spike-related descriptors, stored alongside clinical descriptions in a portable JSON schema. This yields aligned feature-text pairs designed to supervise multimodal EEG-language models. The framework also includes an auto-report module that couples an ensemble of convolutional networks with a large language model to draft clinical narratives for neurologist review. Using pilot annotations, we describe how EEG-to-Report streamlines annotation workflows and produces editable draft reports, providing a reusable foundation for automated EEG reporting systems.

</details>


### [234] [AI agents in Algorithmic Electricity Markets: On the Emergence of Tacit Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26896)
*Jakub Seredyński,Georgios Tsaousoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了电力市场中由自主学习算法控制的参与者是否可能产生默契合谋行为。作者将策略性投标建模为具有不完美公共监控的重复博弈，使用多智能体强化学习模拟参与者行为，并提出了超越纳什均衡利润比较的多维判定标准来评估默契合谋。实验表明，即使在智能体从未被指示合谋的情况下，它们也能学会维持超竞争性结果，支持默契合谋指标的成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场的参与者越来越多地采用基于学习的代理进行投标策略，使市场日益算法化。其他领域算法市场的证据表明，纯粹的独立学习即可引发默契合谋；且电力市场通常为寡头垄断、参与者数量少且存在重复互动，结构上易受非竞争行为影响。因此有必要验证自主学习算法是否会在电力市场中诱发默契合谋。

Method: 作者将策略性投标建模为具有不完美公共监控的重复博弈，采用多智能体强化学习模拟参与者的涌现行为，并提出一套多维判定标准（超越与纳什均衡的利润比较）来评估所得行为是否构成默契合谋。

Result: 实验结果表明，默契合谋风险在电力市场中是现实存在的：在若干案例中，即使智能体从未被指示合谋，它们也能够学习到维持超竞争结果（supra-competitive outcomes）的行为，且这些行为符合默契合谋的指标。

Conclusion: 研究证实了电力市场中自主学习代理可能通过独立学习产生默契合谋的假设，揭示了算法化电力市场在竞争性方面存在的潜在隐患，需引起政策制定者和市场监管者的重视。

Abstract: As electricity market participants increasingly adopt learning-based agents for their bidding strategies, electricity markets are becoming algorithmic. Evidence from algorithmic markets in other domains shows that tacit collusion can arise purely through independent learning. Moreover, electricity markets are typically oligopolistic and feature repeated interaction among a small number of participants, making them structurally susceptible to non-competitive behavior.
