To address this distinction, we propose SARA, which treats action induction and execution authorization as distinct runtime roles and separates action provenance from execution authority. On the Observation side, a context-isolated Action Probe exposes action-inducing semantics and persistently records action-origin provenance across steps as a review signal; on the execution side, actual tool calls are authorized only against the user objective and audited evidence from authorized successful executions, while satisfying goal, execution-chain, and argument-level support. To preserve this separation across multi-step execution, SARA applies No-History-Promotion to prevent historical recurrence from laundering action origins into execution authority. Across AgentDojo and AgentDyn, SARA limits ASR to no more than \(0.63\%\) across four primary evaluation settings while maintaining competitive task utility, and consistently reduces ASR across additional Agent backbones.

</details>


### [299] [Thomson: Continual Learning of Frontier Models for SovereignAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27147)
*Shengzhuang Chen,Jerrod Parker,Yejin Bang,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat,Stefan Winzeck,Daniil Glazko,Jannik Zgraggen,Fangyi Yu,Scott Arnott,Dietrich Trautmann,Luca Ciuffreda,Guglielmo Bonifazi,Davide Romano,Bradley Bell,Kirsty Fielding,Daniele Giofrè,Tom Zielund,Ipshita Chatterjee,Sneha Murthy Ghantasala,Manpreet Nanreh,John Scoville,Maciej Sakowicz,Wassim Seifeddine,Lukas Thede,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过持续学习基于开源权重模型实现前沿性能，使更多机构能以较低预算拥有SovereignAI（主权AI）栈。作者展示了Thomson模型，该模型结合现代中后训练栈与保持可塑性和稳定性的防护手段，在智能体任务、安全、法律、税务及多语言等专业领域达到与前沿模型竞争的水平，并呈现独特的π形能力提升模式，几乎消除了窄域适配中常见的遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的开发通常被视为少数资金充裕的机构的专属领域，造成开发者与广大AI用户之间的信息、经济和权力不对称。尽管公共讨论呼吁SovereignAI（组织独立构建、部署和治理AI的能力），但缺乏在多样化资金条件下短期内实现这一目标的具体指导。

Method: 采用持续学习（Continual Learning）方法基于现有开源权重模型进行开发。不同于小型微调、提示工程或冻结模型工具增强等受限方案，该方法利用完整的现代中端与后训练（mid- & post-training）栈，同时在每个阶段引入保护可塑性与稳定性的防护机制，对参数施加最少但高影响力的干预。由此训练出Thomson模型，重点强化高风险的职业专业任务。

Result: Thomson模型在智能体任务、安全性、法律、税务和多语言能力以及大规模深度研究方面与近期前沿模型表现相当。评估显示独特的π形模式：在广泛能力（包括未显式针对的能力）上均获得显著提升，同时几乎完全消除了窄域领域适配中常见的遗忘问题。训练所需的算力和人员预算远低于通常认知。

Conclusion: 前沿性能可由广泛机构通过持续学习开源模型实现，使更多行动者能够拥有SovereignAI栈中的大部分组件（模型、工具基础设施、价值观与数据隐私），这一路径在计算和人员预算上远低于传统认知，为打破前沿AI开发的权力集中提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The development of frontier models is commonly perceived to be the exclusive remit of a small number of heavily funded players, creating an information, economic and power asymmetry between developers and the diverse user base of modern AI. Recent public discourse acknowledges this concern, calling for SovereignAI (an organisation's capability to independently build, deploy and govern AI use), but offers little concrete advice on how this can be achieved in the short term under a diversity of funding settings. We argue that frontier performance is achievable by a wide range of institutions through Continual Learning on readily available open-weight models. Unlike limited approaches such as small-scale fine-tuning, prompt engineering, or tool-augmentation of a frozen model, our approach exploits a modern mid- & post-training stack while introducing safeguards that preserve both plasticity and stability at each stage, making the minimal number of high-impact interventions on the parameters. This yields gains comparable to those typically seen across multiple successive model generations, at compute and personnel budgets substantially lower than commonly thought, making ownership of large parts of the SovereignAI stack (model, tool infrastructure, values & data privacy) viable for far more actors. We demonstrate this with Thomson, a general-purpose frontier model trained with an enhanced focus on high-stakes professional work. Thomson performs competitively with recent frontier models across agentic tasks, safety, legal, tax & multilingualism, and large-scale Deep Research. Evaluations show a distinctive $π$-shaped pattern: distinct improvements across a wide range of capabilities, including those not explicitly targeted, while almost completely eliminating the forgetting problem common to narrow domain adaptation.

</details>


### [300] [BPMN4CAI: A BPMN Extension for Modeling Dynamic Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27149)
*Björn-Lennart Eger,Daniel Rose,Barbara Dinter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BPMN4CAI，一种符合BPMN标准的扩展方法，用于在业务流程中表示对话式AI系统的动态、上下文敏感交互。通过设计科学研究方法进行开发，并通过案例研究验证了其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI系统（如聊天机器人和虚拟助手）在数字业务流程中日益重要，但现有的业务流程模型和符号（BPMN）标准难以表示动态且上下文敏感的交互场景，存在方法论和实践上的研究缺口。

Method: 采用设计科学研究（DSR）方法论，系统地扩展现有BPMN元素并引入专门的组件，开发标准的BPMN4CAI扩展框架。

Result: 通过案例研究验证了BPMN4CAI框架的适用性和相关性，结果表明该扩展能够促进自适应决策过程、健壮的上下文管理和透明的交互。

Conclusion: BPMN4CAI扩展有效弥补了BPMN标准在对话式AI业务流程建模方面的不足，为业务流程中集成对话式AI提供了标准兼容的建模方案。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems, such as chatbots and virtual assistants, are becoming increasingly important to digital business processes. However, the established Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) standard faces challenges when representing dynamic, context-sensitive interactions. This paper addresses this methodological and practical research gap by developing a standard-compliant BPMN extension (BPMN4CAI). Using Design Science Research methodology, this paper develops an approach that systematically extends existing BPMN elements and incorporates specialized components. The applicability and relevance of the BPMN4CAI framework are demonstrated and evaluated through a case study. The results show that the BPMN4CAI extension facilitates adaptive decision-making processes, robust context management, and transparent interactions for Conversational AI within business processes.

</details>


### [301] [Calibrated Enough to Know, Not Calibrated to Act: Fabricated Evidence Makes LLM Agents Commit to the Unknowable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27167)
*Pranav Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了一个普遍存在的AI治理缺陷：在面对本质不可预测的问题时，12个前沿模型在专业市场面板的"包装"下，承诺做出方向性判断的比例从6.5%升至54.0%。关键在于，即使面板数据全是虚构的，承诺率仍与真实数据不可区分，说明触发自信行动的不是信息而是呈现方式的权威感。作者通过对照实验排除了能力不足、信念偏差和判断缺失等解释，定位到"行动/不行动门控"环节的失败。通过540个合成案例对3B模型进行监督微调，可将承诺率降至0.0%，并能迁移到三个未见领域，但该门控在严格格式约束下会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在面对不可知问题时，倾向于在各种"专业包装"下过度自信地给出方向性判断。研究者希望理解这种自信承诺的根源——究竟是信息本身、信念状态、判断能力，还是单纯的外部包装权威感在起作用——并探索这一缺陷是否可以定位、训练和修复。

Method: 研究设计了多层次的对照实验：(1) 将"裸问题"与附有专业市场面板的问题对比，逐步升级"证据"；(2) 使用完全虚构的面板数据与实际数据对照；(3) 纳入可回答的匹配问题作为能力基线；(4) 测量陈述概率和目标问题的可知性分类；(5) 最后对3B模型进行540个合成案例的监督微调，并在原问题和三个未见领域上测试迁移性。

Result: 12个前沿模型中，承诺率从裸问题的6.5%升至专业面板下的54.0%；纯虚构数据下为36.8%，与真实数据（37.6%）统计上不可区分。模型在可回答问题上准确率接近完美，但陈述概率几乎不随证据梯度变化。将问题分类为"不可约"时正确率达90%，但随即在0.4%的情况下仍会承诺。微调后3B模型在原问题上承诺率从24.5%降至0.0%，并迁移到三个未见领域，但在移除推理空间的刚性格式下门控失效。

Conclusion: 模型失败的本质不在于信息不足、信念错误或判断缺失，而在于"行动/不行动门控"机制的失效——权威包装比真实信息更能触发自信行动。该门控是可分离、可训练的（微调后承诺率归零）但也是上下文脆弱的（受格式约束影响）。部署时必须同时认识到这两个方面。

Abstract: An LLM agent shown a professional-looking market panel commits to a directional call on a provably unpredictable question far more often than one asked the bare question: across 12 frontier models, commitment rises from 6.5% to 54.0% as evidence is escalated. It commits just as readily when every number on the panel is invented: fabricating the entire display, so nothing the model can see is true except the question itself, still lifts commitment from 24.5% to 36.8%, statistically indistinguishable from the 37.6% produced by genuine market data. What unlocks confident action is not information but the authority of its packaging. The failure is narrow and locatable. Incapacity is not the answer: on matched answerable questions attached to the same panels, the same models answer essentially always, at near-perfect accuracy. Nor is it belief - stated probabilities barely move across the gradient that swings action by 48 points, and score worse than a climatological baseline. Missing judgment isn't it either: asked to classify a question's knowability before acting, models call it irreducible 90% of the time and then commit on just 0.4% of those. The act/don't-act gate is what fails, and the effect is concentrated in a few models rather than universal. Because the gate is separable, it can be trained. Supervised fine-tuning of a 3B model on 540 synthetic cases, predominantly dice, coins, jars and timers, drives commitment to 0.0% on the original cases and transfers to three unseen domains. It does not survive everything: the gate holds exactly when the response format leaves room to reason, and rigid formats that remove that room leave the model confident and wrong on questions it otherwise answers correctly. The gate is trainable and context-fragile, and deployment needs both halves of that sentence.

</details>


### [302] [What Makes Good Agentic Data? An ACE Lens on Data Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27260)
*Xingshan Zeng,Zishan Xu,Boju Zhang,Yuzhou Wu,Lingzhi Wang,Jianghao Lin,Liangyou Li,Yasheng Wang,Lifeng Shang,Xin Jiang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Qun Liu,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个关于LLM智能体数据生成的两级分析框架：将智能体数据分解为公因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，并通过精度-复杂度-多样性（ACE）透镜将生成建模为受约束的分布设计，系统梳理现有工作并揭示从执行锚定精度、学习器相对复杂度到超越表面多样性的研究范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体数据生成工作跨越多个领域，但领域中心的组织和异质化评估掩盖了共同的生成机制，并将候选构造与验证选择混为一谈。需要一个统一的框架来提炼共性、揭示深层原理。

Method: 两级框架：(1) 将智能体数据表示为公因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)——环境规范、任务信号、交互实现和可选验证器，按主锚点和依赖结构组织生成范式；(2) 通过ACE（精度Accuracy、复杂度Complexity、多样性divErsity）透镜将生成建模为受约束的分布设计——精度确立可行支撑集，复杂度在支撑集内根据学习者能力放置学习质量，多样性控制覆盖和冗余。

Result: 文献显示向执行锚定精度、学习器相对复杂度和超越表面变化/数据集规模的多样性转变。框架揭示共同生成机制并支持跨领域统一评估。

Conclusion: 智能体数据生成的核心挑战不是简单地生成更多数据，而是在智能体和环境演化过程中持续分配有效、信息丰富且非冗余的经验数据。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on generated interaction data to learn how to interact with external environments. Agentic data generation must maintain consistency among environments, tasks, interactions, and success signals while producing experience that is useful rather than merely abundant. Existing work spans many agent domains, but domain-centered organization and heterogeneous evaluation often obscure common generation mechanisms and conflate candidate construction with verification and selection. This work develops a two-level framework for the field. First, we represent agentic data as a common factorized object $(E,q,τ,v)$, comprising an environment specification, task signal, interaction realization, and optional verifier. We organize generation paradigms by their primary anchor and dependency structure. Second, we formulate generation as constrained distribution design through the Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE) lens. Accuracy establishes the feasible support of grounded and internally consistent data. Within this support, Complexity places learning mass relative to the capability of a declared learner and execution configuration, while divErsity controls coverage and redundancy of data. Using this framework, we explore how prior work verifies generated experience, constructs and calibrates difficulty, and expands behavioral coverage. The literature reveals a shift toward execution-grounded accuracy, learner-relative complexity, and diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size. We further discuss broader directions and emerging trends in agentic data generation through the ACE lens, including their implications for scaling, data sources, training regimes and adaptive learning. Overall, the central challenge is not simply to generate more data, but to continually allocate valid, informative, and non-redundant experience as agents and environments evolve.

</details>


### [303] [Naive Prompt Optimization: Rethinking the Need for Complex Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27266)
*Yuan Chang,Xiaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO)，一种轻量级的单谱系提示优化方法，通过教师模型与rollout反馈迭代修订提示。实验表明NPO在更少rollout下达到或超越复杂优化器GEPA的性能，且更强的教师推理可部分替代优化器的搜索复杂度，展示了简单线性提示优化足以媲美复杂搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代理式AI中加速递归自我改进（RSI）需要高效提升跨任务的自主代理性能，提示优化是一种有前景的方法，但近期发展日益倾向于使用不必要复杂的提示优化器。作者旨在挑战这一趋势，探索简单的提示优化方法能否匹敌复杂方法。

Method: 提出NPO方法：一种轻量级单谱系（single-lineage）方法，使用教师模型并利用rollout反馈迭代地修订提示。通过与GEPA（一种复杂搜索式优化器）及GRPO在交互式游戏中对比验证性能。

Result: NPO在更少的rollout下达到或优于GEPA的性能；其优势随教师模型增强而增大；在交互式游戏中与GEPA大致相当，而GRPO在部分不适合提示优化的任务上表现更好；NPO优化的提示可直接迁移到其他学生模型（尤其是同族模型）并带来相似性能提升。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，简单、线性的提示优化方法可以匹敌显著更复杂和精巧的搜索过程。更强的教师推理能力可以作为优化器侧搜索复杂度的替代方案，为提示优化的简洁性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Efficiently improving autonomous agents across diverse tasks is central to accelerating recursive self-improvement (RSI) in agentic AI, with prompt optimization emerging as a promising approach capable of delivering performance gains comparable to those achieved by fine-tuning model weights, while reducing computational costs in both optimization and serving. However, recent developments increasingly favor unnecessarily complex prompt optimizers. We introduce Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO), a lightweight single-lineage method that iteratively revises prompts using a teacher model with rollout feedback. NPO achieves comparable or better performance than GEPA with fewer rollouts, and its advantage increases with stronger teacher models, suggesting that stronger teacher reasoning can partially substitute for optimizer-side search complexity. In interactive games, NPO remains broadly competitive with GEPA, while GRPO performs better on some tasks less amenable to prompt optimization. We also show that NPO-optimized prompts elicit similar performance improvements when applied verbatim to other student models, especially across models within the same family. Overall, our preliminary results show that simple, linear prompt optimization can rival substantially more sophisticated and complex search procedures.

</details>


### [304] [BrailleBench: Investigating Multi-Criteria Braille Comprehension in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27268)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Zhiyu Chen,Xiaoyan Han,Kunpeng Liu,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BrailleBench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在盲文理解上的能力。该基准包含5,570个实例，覆盖数学、常识和多跳问答，支持英文及盲文1级和2级。评估了六个代表性LLM，发现它们在印刷英文能力和盲文可及性之间存在持续差距，且盲文理解与表达不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在知识获取和计算辅助方面具有能力，但其功能应当同样惠及弱势群体。当前不确定现有AI系统是否足够包容，能让盲人和聋盲用户通过盲文访问相同功能。盲文中的指示符、缩写和数字表示对模型理解提出了独特要求，而现有研究缺乏相关评估。

Method: 构建BrailleBench基准，从五个数据集对齐5,570个实例，覆盖数学、常识和多跳问答，包含英文及盲文1级和2级。设计了不同配置以考察系统能否理解盲文撰写内容、用盲文表达答案以及完成端到端盲文交互。基准通过自建盲文工具包以确定性的、专家审核的流水线构建，未使用LLM生成的数据。

Result: 评估了六个代表性LLM后发现：印刷英文能力与盲文可及性之间存在持续差距；盲文理解与表达表现不对称；盲文2级在输入端尤为脆弱（相比1级）；完全盲文请求会进一步降低性能。

Conclusion: 实验观测为未来盲文AI系统的开发提供了有价值的指导方向。BrailleBench的所有相关资源已公开，供后续研究使用，旨在推动更包容的AI系统发展。

Abstract: Although Large language models (LLMs) mediate access to knowledge and computational assistance, their capabilities should benefit vulnerable groups in the same way. However, it is unclear whether existing AI systems are inclusive enough for blind and deafblind users to access the same functionality through Braille, whose indicators, contractions, and digital representations introduce distinct requirements for model comprehension. To this end, we introduce BrailleBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs in Braille comprehension from different Criteria. BrailleBench aligns 5,570 instances from five datasets, including mathematics, commonsense, and multi-hop question answering across English and Braille Grades 1 and 2. Different configurations are designed to understand whether the systems can comprehend Braille-authored content, express answers in Braille, and complete end-to-end Braille interaction. To ensure the quality and prevent evaluation bias, the benchmark is built through a deterministic, expert-reviewed pipeline via a self-created Braille Toolkit without using any data instances generated by LLMs. We evaluate six representative LLMs from various aspects. The results reveal a persistent gap between print-English capability and Braille accessibility. Braille understanding and expression are asymmetric, where Grade 2 is especially fragile on the input side compared to Grade 1, and fully Braille requests further reduce performance. The experimental observations provide valuable guidance for the development of future Braille AI systems. All related resources in BrailleBench are publicly available for future research.

</details>


### [305] [LLMs Can Design Near-Optimal OR Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27296)
*Jackie Baek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）是否能为明确指定的运筹学（OR）问题设计有效算法，涵盖库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化三类问题。研究发现，最强模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有测试实例上匹配或超越了现有最优方法，且即使在级别2（模型仅基于问题类别描述和参数范围，在见到评估实例前就返回固定算法）也能达到这一水平。性能在不到8个月内发布的模型之间显著提升，表明该能力正在快速进步，前沿LLM可作为明确指定OR问题算法设计的严肃经验基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索前沿LLM是否能够超越仅仅回答问题，进而为结构化的运筹学优化问题自主设计有效算法，从而在专业方法之外提供一个竞争性基线。研究旨在以最小人工干预（单个未调优提示词和固定计算预算的Python沙盒工具）考察LLM在两种使用级别下的算法设计能力。

Method: 在库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化三个明确的OR问题领域，评估LLM的两个使用级别：级别1中模型接收单个问题实例并返回该实例的解决方案；级别2中模型仅接收问题类别描述和宽泛参数范围，返回一个将实例参数映射到解决方案的算法。实验使用单个未调优提示词描述问题，并给予模型固定计算预算的Python沙盒工具，比较不同版本的模型在不同实例上的表现。

Result: 最强测试模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有评估实例上匹配或超越了现有最优方法，即使在级别2（算法在见到评估实例前就已固定）也成立。此外，相隔不到8个月发布的模型之间的性能显著提升，表明该能力正在迅速进步。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的明确定义的运筹问题，单次未调优的LLM查询即可生成与专业方法竞争甚至更优的算法。这暗示前沿LLM可以成为明确指定OR问题算法设计的严肃经验基准，且随着模型能力快速演进，其在这一领域的潜力值得持续关注。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) can design effective algorithms for well-specified operations research (OR) problems. We study inventory control, queueing network control, and assortment optimization. We evaluate two levels of LLM use: at level 1, the model receives one problem instance and returns a solution for that instance; at level 2, it receives only the problem class description and broad parameter ranges, and returns an algorithm that maps instance parameters to solutions. Human input is minimal: we give one untuned prompt that describes the problem, and the model has access to a Python sandbox tool with a fixed compute budget.
  The strongest model we test, gpt-5.6-sol, matches or outperforms the best existing method on almost all evaluated instances. This holds even at level 2, where the returned algorithm is fixed before seeing the evaluation instances. Performance also improves sharply across models released less than eight months apart, suggesting that this capability is moving quickly. Thus, for the well-specified operations problems we study, a single untuned LLM query can already produce algorithms competitive with specialized methods. These results suggest that frontier LLMs can be a serious empirical baseline for algorithm design in well-specified OR problems.

</details>


### [306] [Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27311)
*Jinghan Xu,Yikai Zhang,Aili Chen,Weiyuan Li,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarnessLens是一个预算感知的自动化智能体框架演化框架，通过共同探索任务空间和可配置组件、从执行轨迹中派生候选修改、并利用可归因证据门控仅在与行为相关的任务上选择性验证候选，在三个智能体框架和四个基准上实现了7.6-13.6%的留出数据性能提升，同时显著减少评估预算消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架的适配需要昂贵的验证成本，传统的提出并验证方法在所有固定任务集上对每个候选打分，浪费大量评估资源在与行为无关的任务上，且聚合分数会掩盖特定行为的回归问题。

Method: HarnessLens采用预算感知的联合探索策略：共同遍历任务空间与用户可配置组件，从执行轨迹中生成候选修改，并通过可归因证据门控，仅在与候选行为相关的任务上进行选择性验证，从而实现行为感知的验证。

Result: 在三个智能体框架和四个基准上，HarnessLens将平均留出性能提升了7.6-13.6%，同时比竞争基线消耗更少的评估预算。

Conclusion: 行为感知验证与显式归因机制能够在受限交互预算下实现更可靠、更采样高效的智能体框架演化，验证了该方法在真实场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Agent harnesses shape how language-model agents use instructions, tools, and runtime components, but adapting these harnesses requires costly verification. Existing propose-and-verify methods typically score every candidate on a fixed task set, wasting rollouts on unrelated behaviors and allowing aggregate scores to obscure specific regressions. We introduce HarnessLens, a budget-aware framework for automated harness evolution. HarnessLens jointly explores the task space and user-configurable components, derives candidate modifications from execution trajectories, and selectively verifies each candidate on behavior-relevant tasks using an attributable-evidence gate. Across three agent harnesses and four benchmarks, HarnessLens improves average held-out performance by 7.6-13.6% while consuming substantially less evaluation budget than competing baselines. These results demonstrate that behavior-aware verification with explicit attribution enables more reliable and sample-efficient harness evolution under constrained interaction budgets. Our code is available at https://github.com/jhxu5214/HarnessLens.

</details>


### [307] [Not All Eval-Awareness Is Equal: Capabilities Framing Predicts Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27340)
*Allison Zhuang,Santiago Aranguri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出CoT中语言化表达的评估意识（eval-awareness）并非单一同质量，而可区分为能力导向、安全导向、两者兼具或两者皆非等不同框架，且这些框架对模型服从行为（compliance）的预测差异显著（能力框架比安全框架高+24到+46个百分点）。通过CoT前缀干预验证了因果性，并指出聚合层面的"评估意识抑制率"不能反映安全相关成分的实际变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估管线中越来越多地使用针对评估意识的转向（steering）干预，但现有做法把评估意识当作单一量予以抑制，忽略了其内部可能存在不同语义框架，这可能导致安全评估失真。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B模型上使用FORTRESS数据集，将CoT中语言化的评估意识分类为能力导向、安全导向、两者兼具或两者皆非四类框架；通过CoT-prefill干预对评估意识为负的rolled-out结果进行因果验证；并比较不同转向条件下的服从率差异。

Result: 能力导向框架预测服从的差距比安全导向框架高+24至+46个百分点（覆盖所有测试转向条件）；10/11个prefill干预使服从方向按预测方向偏移，支持因果性；评估意识并非行为均质，聚合抑制率的变化可能不伴随安全相关成分的变化，同一抑制百分比可对应不同行为结果。

Conclusion: 评估意识应被细分为不同语义框架来理解，盲目的聚合抑制无法保证安全相关评估意识得到有效控制，安全评估管线需要更精细的评估意识测量与干预策略。

Abstract: Steering interventions targeting eval-awareness, a model's recognition that it is being tested, are increasingly used in safety evaluation pipelines, where evaluation-awareness is treated as a single quantity to be suppressed. We show that verbalized eval-awareness in chain-of-thought can be identified as capabilities-flavored ("the user is testing my ability to follow instructions"), safety-flavored ("the user is testing my boundaries"), both, or neither: framings that predict compliance very differently. On Qwen3-32B over the FORTRESS dataset, capabilities-framing predicts compliance with a +24 to +46 percentage-point gap over safety-framing across all tested steering conditions. A CoT-prefill intervention on eval-awareness-negative rollouts suggests the link is causal, with 10 of 11 prefills shifting compliance in the predicted direction. Then, eval-awareness is not behaviorally uniform: aggregate suppression rates can move while the safety-relevant component does not, and the same "X% suppression of eval-awareness" can correspond to qualitatively different behavioral outcomes.

</details>


### [308] [Sophistication in GenAI Use: Field Evidence from a Large Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27364)
*Nicholas J. Hallman,Zachary T. Kowaleski,Anu Puvvada,Jaime J. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于一家大型企业约4,000名后台员工在2025年八个月间的713,564条提示词及大语言模型响应数据，考察生成式AI使用成熟度的差异性。研究发现：高级别员工表现出更高的AI使用成熟度；成熟度在不同职能间差异显著，在战略、数字创新和项目管理三个领域最高；随时间推移未观察到成熟度改善，正式AI培训也未带来持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在企业后台员工中的实际使用成熟度尚缺乏大规模实证研究，管理者需要了解成熟度分布的驱动因素以优化AI投资与培训策略。

Method: 利用专有数据，对一家大型企业近4,000名后台员工在15个职能领域、8个月内的713,564个员工提示词及对应的大语言模型响应进行量化分析，衡量AI使用成熟度及其与员工级别、职能领域和时间变化的关系。

Result: 三项主要发现：一是高级别员工AI使用更成熟，反映领域专长与生成式AI能力互补；二是成熟度随职能领域差异显著，战略、数字创新和项目管理三类职能最高，这三者共同关注企业级战略举措与组织变革；三是成熟度既不随时间改善，也未因正式AI培训产生持续提升，表明成熟使用可能难以通过现有人工干预改变。

Conclusion: 研究提供了生成式AI使用成熟度的度量工具与实证洞察，可供管理者改善AI使用成效，也可供研究者用于后续研究。

Abstract: We study how sophistication in generative AI (genAI) use varies among the back-office workforce of a large firm. Using proprietary data, we observe 713,564 employee prompts and their corresponding large language model responses from nearly 4,000 back-office employees across 15 functional areas over eight months in 2025. We document three main findings. First, senior employees exhibit more sophisticated genAI use, consistent with domain expertise complementing genAI capabilities. Second, sophistication varies considerably across functions and is highest in Strategy, Digital Innovation, and Project Management, three groups that share a focus on firmwide strategic initiatives and organizational change. Third, we observe neither improvements in sophistication over time nor lasting improvements following formal AI training, suggesting that sophisticated use can be difficult to change. Together, our study provides measures of and insights into sophisticated genAI use that managers can use to improve outcomes and that researchers can use in future research.

</details>


### [309] [CorporateBench: Large-Scale Q&A Benchmarking with Temporal Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27391)
*Sil Hamilton,Albert Yu Sun,Oscar J. Romero,Carl-Leander Henneking,David Mimno,Bishan Yang,Igor Labutov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CorporateBench（CB），一个大规模、人类验证的多任务问答基准测试，语料库超过23万文档，用于评估LLM在企业级文档检索和多跳推理场景下的表现。实验发现，当输入规模接近真实企业规模时，五个LLM的性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的企业文档问答评估面临两难困境：真实企业内部数据因保密无法共享，而合成数据集又过于简单，无法反映真实企业级规模下的复杂条件。需要构建一个既符合企业通信网络规模又具有逻辑一致性的可公开评测基准。

Method: 通过构建四个合成的企业实体（员工数从12到10,000人不等），从随时间演化的知识库中采样生成评估语料库，确保跨文档逻辑一致性。基准覆盖两个维度：信息抽取（information extraction）和知识库查询（knowledge base querying），并经过人类验证保证质量。

Result: 对五个LLM在CB上的评估显示，输入规模接近真实企业规模时，模型性能急剧下降，揭示了当前LLM在企业级文档推理场景中的短板。

Conclusion: CorporateBench为LLM开发者提供了衡量企业通信推理能力的指标，填补了大规模、可扩展企业文档问答基准测试生态中的关键空白。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly able to answer complex questions about enterprise-scale document collections. But evaluation is hard: companies don't want to share internal communications, and synthetic datasets have been overly simple. We present CorporateBench (CB), a human-validated multi-task Q&A benchmark whose scale approaches the conditions LLMs encounter in corporate communication networks, with evaluation corpora surpassing 230,000 documents. CB evaluates LLMs across two dimensions (information extraction and knowledge base querying) through four synthetically generated firms ranging from 12 to 10,000 employees. Each corpus is sampled from a temporally evolving knowledge base describing a consistent world, guaranteeing cross-document logical consistency even across hundreds of thousands of documents. We evaluate five LLMs on CB, revealing increasingly poor performance as input size approaches realistic scales. CB provides LLM developers a metric for corporate communication reasoning, filling a crucial gap in the benchmarking ecosystem.

</details>


### [310] [Learning a Continuous Sepsis Severity Score Without Hour-by-Hour Supervision: A Two-Site Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27421)
*Kevin Zhu,Ryan Zhang,Baraa Abed,Tilendra Choudhary,Malvern Madondo,Mehak Arora,Yixuan Yang,Alasdair Gent,Aditya Nagori,Omer T. Inan,Krista L. Haines,Patrick Georgoff,Suresh M. Agarwal,Vijay Krishnamoorthy,Tetsu Ohnuma,Mihai V. Podgoreanu,Michael R. Pinsky,Gilles Clermont,Craig M. Coopersmith,Craig S. Jabaley,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究基于29,116名马萨诸塞州和7,691名佐治亚州符合Sepsis-3标准的成人患者回顾性队列数据，利用43个常规记录的临床变量和72小时治疗窗口，开发了一种新的脓毒症严重程度指数。与传统固定权重评分（如SOFA）不同，该方法将死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号而非逐状态目标，允许信用非均匀地跨时间步重新分配。该指数在0-10量表上能区分幸存者与非幸存者（非幸存者高1.19-1.64分），与乳酸变化相关（rho=0.39），跨机构一致性为同机构相关性的70-77%，结论认为其具备作为临床决策支持工具的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脓毒症严重程度指数（如SOFA）依赖数十年前确定的固定变量和权重，变量离散化粗糙，且校准队列已不符合当代重症监护环境。没有其他直接从患者轨迹学习的方法进入常规临床应用，亟需一种更适应现代临床数据的动态脓毒症严重程度评估工具。

Method: 采用回顾性双队列研究设计，分别纳入来自马萨诸塞州和佐治亚州两家医院系统的29,116和7,691名成年脓毒症患者。使用43个常规记录的变量，在72小时治疗窗口内构建脓毒症指数。创新点在于将死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号而非逐状态目标，使信用可跨时间步非均匀分配。通过永久20%测试集、临床叙述和Spearman相关性进行评估，不确定性区间通过bootstrap重采样整患者获得。

Result: 在所有基线SOFA-2分层内，非幸存者在0-10量表上比幸存者高1.19-1.64分，在乳酸、平均动脉压和肌酐分层中结果相似。患者内指数变化与乳酸变化相关（Spearman rho=0.39，n=1,854），MAP和肌酐呈较弱相关。跨机构同意的Spearman相关性为同机构相关性的70-77%，外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59（天花板分别为0.92和0.90）。与已有指数相关，而零对照接近零。

Conclusion: 该脓毒症指数展示了每小时级的预后信息，能有效区分患者结局并与临床预期一致，表明其作为辅助临床判断的决策支持工具具有潜力。

Abstract: Currently used sepsis severity indices rely on fixed variables and weights established decades ago, which are coarsely discretized and calibrated to a cohort that no longer reflects contemporary critical care. No alternative learned directly from patient trajectories is in routine use. We conducted a retrospective two-cohort study on a total of 29,116 and 7,691 adult patients meeting Sepsis-3 criteria from two hospital systems in Massachusetts and Georgie, respectively.
  We developed a sepsis index using 43 routinely charted variables over a 72-hour treatment window. Unlike previous studies, we use mortality as a treatment-level ranking signal rather than a per-state target, allowing credit to be redistributed non-uniformly across timesteps. Evaluation was done on a permanent 20% test holdout, using clinical vignettes and Spearman correlation. Uncertainty intervals were obtained by bootstrap resampling of whole patients. Under this ranking scheme, non-survivors scored 1.19-1.64 points higher than survivors on a 0-10 scale within all strata of baseline SOFA-2, with similar results stratifying within lactate, mean arterial pressure (MAP), and creatinine. Within-patient change in the index correlated with change in lactate (Spearman rho = 0.39; n = 1,854). Similar, weaker correlations were found for MAP and creatinine. On a cohort level, cross-institutional agreement measured by Spearman correlation between models trained on different sites, were 70-77% of same-site correlation. External within-patient correlations were 0.54 and 0.59 against ceilings of 0.92 and 0.90. Our index also correlated with established indices, while null controls stayed near zero.
  Our index demonstrated hourly prognostic information that meaningfully separates patient outcomes and is consistent with clinical expectation, indicating potential as a decision support tool complementing clinical judgement.

</details>


### [311] [Mechanistic Reaction Prediction via Discrete Flow Matching on Graph-Structured Electron Occupation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27429)
*Nguyen Xuan-Vu,Octavian Susanu,Daniel Armstrong,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAELLE提出了一种将化学反应建模为电子占据向量上的离散流匹配（discrete flow matching）的新方法，通过在连续时间马尔可夫链中学习电子重排的编辑轨迹，实现了机理可解释的反应预测，并在USPTO-480K基准及多种分布外场景下展现出竞争性乃至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习反应预测方法要么通过从头生成产物分子（de novo generation），要么通过直接在分子拓扑上进行启发式图编辑，二者均未从化学反应的本质——电子空间的变换——出发建模。作者希望通过显式建模电子重排过程，既提升预测精度，又获得机理可解释性和分布外鲁棒性。

Method: 将反应物到产物的映射形式化为定义在所有成键、非键和氢位点上的图结构整数电子占据空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。作者将离散流匹配的混合路径推广到离散电子重排场景，利用最优传输（Optimal Transport）构造中间编辑轨迹，从而获得一系列机理可解释的编辑步骤序列，且无需基本步骤（elementary step）的标注。

Result: MAELLE在USPTO-480K基准上与领先的反应预测模型相比取得竞争性表现。在两个分布外设置（结构复杂度和反应类型）下，MAELLE保持了强性能，而现有方法出现性能退化。此外，由于学习的流覆盖完整的电子重分配，MAELLE能够自然恢复与已知化学一致的机理轨迹，并能预测反应的副产物。

Conclusion: 通过在电子空间而非分子拓扑层面建模化学反应，MAELLE不仅达到了主流的反应预测精度，还在分布外鲁棒性、机理可解释性和副产物预测能力上展现出独特优势，验证了从电子重排视角建模化学反应的可行性。

Abstract: Chemical reactions are fundamentally transformations in electron space, yet most machine learning approaches model them either through \textit{de novo} generation of product molecules or through heuristic graph edits that operate directly on molecular topology.
  We introduce MAELLE (\textbf{M}ech\textbf{A}nistic \textbf{E}dit f\textbf{L}ow-matching on e\textbf{L}ectron r\textbf{E}arrangements), which instead models reactions as discrete flow matching over electron occupation vectors.
  Concretely, we formulate the reactant-to-product mapping as a Continuous-time Markov Chain (CTMC) over the graph-structured integer-valued electron occupation space defined on all bonding, non-bonding, and hydrogen sites.
  To construct the intermediate edit trajectories, we generalize the discrete flow matching mixture path to discrete electron rearrangements using Optimal Transport, yielding a sequence of mechanistically interpretable edit moves without requiring elementary step annotations.
  MAELLE achieves competitive performance on the USPTO-480K benchmark compared with leading reaction prediction models.
  Beyond in-distribution accuracy, we evaluate robustness across two out-of-distribution settings - structural complexity and reaction type - and find that MAELLE maintains strong performance where existing methods degrade.
  Finally, because the learned flow operates over the full electron redistribution, MAELLE naturally recovers mechanistic trajectories that align with known chemistry and can predict side products of a reaction.

</details>


### [312] [WikiSkill: Compiling Agent Experience into Persistent Knowledge for Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27454)
*Liyan Tang,Cyrus Rashtchian,Chun-Sung Ferng,Andrew Tomkins,Da-Cheng Juan,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WikiSkill框架，通过将原始执行经验、累积知识和可执行技能分离，并持续将经验整合进持久化知识库（wiki）中，使后续技能更新能够基于积累的知识进行，从而提升AI智能体技能的演化效率和可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能发现方法虽能从智能体经验中自动发现技能，但指导技能开发的洞察通常散落在优化历史中，缺乏系统性复用，限制了跨迭代的技能演化。

Method: WikiSkill将原始执行经验、累积知识与可执行技能三者分离，通过持续将执行经验整合到持久化知识库（wiki）中，使后续技能更新能建立在累积知识之上，实现技能与wiki的共同演化。

Result: 在多个基准和模型上，WikiSkill一致优于最先进的技能演化方法，并在大多数设置下优于无技能基线。技能演化与模型规模互补：更大模型从演化技能中获益更多，而小模型配合技能可超越更大的无技能模型。演化技能在跨模型和模型家族间可有效转移，且其他模型演化的技能可优于自演化技能。消融实验确认持久知识积累对有效技能演化至关重要。

Conclusion: 系统性地积累和精炼智能体经验对于开发可复用和可转移的技能至关重要，WikiSkill证明了持久化知识库在技能演化中的核心价值。

Abstract: Agent skills package specialized knowledge and workflows into reusable resources that extend AI agent capabilities. Recent work automatically discovers such skills from agent experience, which enables agents to progressively adapt through interaction. However, the insights that guide skill development typically remain scattered across optimization histories, limiting their systematic reuse across iterations. We introduce WikiSkill, a framework that co-evolves agent skills with a persistent knowledge base (wiki). At a high level, WikiSkill separates raw execution experience, accumulated knowledge, and executable skills, while continuously consolidating experience into the wiki, which subsequent skill updates can build on. Across diverse benchmarks and models, WikiSkill consistently outperforms state-of-the-art skill-evolution methods and improves over no-skill baselines in most model-benchmark settings. We find that skill evolution complements model scaling: larger models generally benefit more from evolved skills, while smaller models with skills can outperform substantially larger models without them. We also find that evolved skills transfer effectively across models and model families, and skills evolved by other models can outperform self-evolved skills. Finally, our ablation studies confirm that persistent knowledge accumulation in the wiki is critical for effective skill evolution. These results demonstrate the benefits of systematically accumulating and refining agent experience for developing reusable and transferable skills.

</details>
